pulavar. irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ

veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉai -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vittai ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. atai muḷaikkac ceytavar paritimāl kalaiñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟaimalaiyām maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭaiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟaiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkaiḷai nilai nāṭṭiya malaiyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavai eḻutiyavai aṉaittum meymmaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭait tam āyvu matukaiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuraiyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uraiyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavai alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkkai añciṟait tumpiyāka avaṟṟai yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉai akara niral tokaiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kālaiyil, tamiḻ moḻiyaiyum tamiḻ maṇṇaiyum tamiḻ iṉattaiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmaiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uraiyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉai vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉaivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkaruttai yāṉ uraikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷai amaittār.
tēvanēyar paṭaippukaḷ aṉaittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷait tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amaikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakaiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷaiyum kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimaiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭaikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉaivar kaikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya nilaiyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpatai nilaippaṭuttum vakaiyil avarkkup paṭaiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉaivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷaiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumaiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭaiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!								iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!							      irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



patippurai
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇaiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutaiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatait tam paṉmoḻip pulamaiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ aṉaittaiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatait tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talaippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakaiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭaitta vairaccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷai veḷiyiṭu vatil perumaip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlattai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

paṇṭai viḷaiyāṭṭu viḻākkaḷ
(1) puṉal viḷaiyāṭṭu
paṇṭaik kālattil, āṟṟarukēyirunta nakaramāntarellārum, āṇṭutōṟum āṟṟiṟ putuveḷḷam vantavuṭaṉ oruṅkē ceṉṟu, oru pakaliṟ perumpakuti avveḷḷattil tiḷaittāṭi iṉpuṟṟa viḷaiyāṭṭu viḻā, puṉal viḷaiyāṭṭu allatu puṉalāṭṭu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. atu nīrviḻā, nīrāṭṭu, nīrāṭṭaṇi mutaliya peyar koṇṭum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
nīrāṭuvārellām, āṟṟiliṭuvataṟkup poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum ceyta mīṉ mutaliya kāṇikkaik karuvikaḷaiyum, puṇai teppam parical mutaliya mitavaik karuvikaḷaiyum, kātalar mītu vāca neyyaiyum vaṇṇa nīraiyum teḷittaṟkut turutti kompu civiṟi mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷaiyum, nīrāṭiyapiṉ vēṇṭum ūṇ uṭai akil mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, tattamakku iyaṉṟavāṟu, yāṉai kutirai tēr mutaliyavaṟṟil ūrntum kālnaṭaiyāy naṭantum, koṇṭu celvar. kuṭivāriyāka āṅkāṅku amaikkap peṟṟa kuṟṟilkaḷum putuk kaṭaikaḷum, cērntu, oru viḻavūr pōlak kāṭciyaḷikkum.
nīntavallār caṟṟu āḻattilum allātār karaiyaiyaṭuttum nīrāṭuvatum, pūcuñ cuṇṇam cāntu kuḻampu mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu paṟṟip peṇṭir ikalāṭuvatum, tam kaṇavaṉmār piṟa peṇṭiroṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭiṉāreṉṟu maṉaik kiḻatti yar ūṭuvatum, pūcucāntam puṉaintamālai uḻakkum nīrāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kalaṅkal veḷḷam putumaṇam peṟuvatum, puṉalāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷām.
nīrāṭiyavar mālaikkālattil makiḻntum ayarntum maṉai tirumpuvar.
nīrāṭṭu viḻā uñcai nakarattil 21 nāḷ nikaḻnta teṉap peruṅkatai kūṟum.
ikkālattuk koṇṭāṭappeṟum āṭip patiṉeṭṭām perukku, paṇṭaip puṉalāṭṭai oru puṭaiyokkum.
(2) poḻil viḷaiyāṭṭu
nakaravāṇar, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil, ūrukkuc caṟṟut tolaivi luḷḷa oru cōlaikkuc ceṉṟu, taṉit taṉiyākavum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākavum oru pakal camaittuṇṭu makiḻnta viḻā, poḻil viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ikkālattil uṟaviṉarum naṇparumāka oru cilar ceṉṟuṇṇum kāṭṭuṇāp pōṉṟa jkum.nrhiyia, aṭaintapiṉ, aṭicil toḻilil īṭupaṭṭava roḻinta ēṉaiyarellām vevvēṟu viṉaipaṟṟi vevvē ṟiṭañceṉṟu viṭuvar. āṭavaruḷ periyōr vēṭṭaiyāṭa vum, ciṟiyōr maramēṟutal kāykaṉi paṟittuṇṭal viḷaiyāṭu tal mutaliya viṉai nikaḻttavum, pirintuviṭuvar. peṇṭiruḷ mūttōr aṭicil toḻilil amara; iḷaiyōr,  malar koytu mālai toṭukkavum, pāvai puṉaintu pārāṭṭi makiḻavum, ūñcalamaittu untiyāṭavum, ciṟṟiliḻaittuc ciṟucōṟu camaikkavum, āṅkāṅku akaṉṟuviṭuvar.
cilar kaṭṭamutu koṇṭu celvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṉṉār aṉaivarum iṉpa viḷaiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭuvar.
naṇpakal uṇavuṇṭapiṉ, cila nāḻikai nēram iḷaippāṟi mālaik kālam vīṭu tirumpuvatu iyalpākum.
putitāy maṇanta kātalar, tāmē ceṉṟu āṭum poḻilāṭṭum, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu potu nakara māntar koṇṭāṭum poḻilāṭṭum, vevvēṟām.


paṇṇāṅkuḻi viḷaiyāṭṭu
I potuvakai
āṭṭiṉ peyar :  neṟkuttum paṇṇaipōl vaṭṭamāṉa paḷḷam allatu kuḻitōṇṭi, atiṟ kaṟkaḷaiyiṭṭu āṭum āṭṭu paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉappaṭum. paṇṇaiyeṉpatu paḷḷam. paṇṇai paṟittal kuḻitōṇṭutal.
paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉṉum peyar, avvav viṭattaip poṟuttup paṉṉāṅkuḻi, pallāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉa vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkum. perumpālum patiṉāṉku kuḻi vaittu ivviḷaiyāṭṭu āṭap peṟuvatāl, patiṉāṉku kuḻi eṉpatu muṟaiyē patiṉāṅkuḻi paṉṉāṅkuḻi eṉat tirintatākac cilar koḷvar. āyiṉ, patiṉāṅkuḻi eṉa eṅkēṉum vaḻaṅ kāmai yāṉum, paṉṉāṉku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakkātalāṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷē peru vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, patiṉāṉkiṟkuk kuṟaintum kūṭiyum kuḻikaḷ vaittuk koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paḷḷāṅ kuḻi eṉpataṟkup paḷḷamāṉa kuḻi eṉṟē potumakkaḷāṟ poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi allatu paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉpatē tiruntiya vaṭivamām.
āṭuvār tokai :  itai iruvar āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi:  nilattiṟ camamāṉa irupaṭukkai varicaiyākak killappaṭṭa 10 allatu 14 allatu 16 kuḻikaḷum, avaṟṟuḷ avvaintāy iṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya kaḻaṟcikkāy (kaccakkāy) allatu puḷiyaṅkoṭṭai allatu kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum, itaṟku vēṇṭuṅ karuvikaḷām.
cilar, eṉṟum eṅkum vacatiyāy āṭutaṟ poruṭṭu, vēṇṭiya aḷavu paḷḷañ cetukkap peṟṟa marakkaṭṭaikaḷai vaittiruppar.
āṭiṭam: itu vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭu allatu mara niḻalilum āṭap peṟum. itu ēṉai vakaikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟai:  kuḻi varicaikkoruvarāka iruvar varicaiyaṭuttu etiretir uṭkārntu, kuḻikkaintāka ellāk kuḻikaḷilum kaṟkaḷaip pōṭuvar. muntiyāṭupavar, tam varicaiyil ētēṉu moru kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaintaiyum eṭuttu, valappuṟamākac cuṟṟik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭuk koṇṭē pōtal vēṇṭum. kaṟkaḷaip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭaicikkal pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaintaiyum eṭuttu, ataṟkappāluḷḷa kuḻiyil ovvoṉṟākap pōṭṭuk koṇṭu pōtal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam pōṭumpōtu, tam varicaiyil iṭavalamākavum, etiri varicaiyil vala iṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cellavēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi, veṟumaiyāka iruntāl ataṟkaṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaṉait taiyum eṭuttut tammiṭam vaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, etiriyār tam varicaiyiluḷḷa oru kuḻiyiliruntu toṭaṅki, muṉ coṉṉavāṟē āṭuvar. kaṭaiciyākappōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻiyilum kal illāviṭiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum. ovvoruvarum tattam varicaiyiluḷḷa kuḻiyoṉṟil 4 kaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭiṉ, avaṟṟaip pacu eṉṟu colli eṭuttuk koḷvar. cilaviṭaṅ kaḷil, 8kal cērntu viṭiṉ avaṟṟaip paḻam eṉṟu colli eṭuttuk koḷvar.
iṅṅaṉam iruvarum māṟi māṟi āṭivarumpōtu, eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭātu eñciyuḷḷa kaṟkaḷellām orē varicaiyiṟ pōyc cērntuviṭiṉ, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. iruvarum tāntām eṭuttuvaittirukkum kaṟkaḷai eṇṇuvar. kaṭaici yil orē varicaiyiṟ pōyc cērnta kaṟkaḷellām, avvaricai yāraic cērum, mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷai eṭuttavar veṉṟavarāvar.
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, orē āṭṭaiyil veṟṟiyait tīrmāṉiyāmal, aintāṭṭaiyiṉ piṉ allatu pattāṭṭaiyiṉ piṉ tīrmā ṉippar. ammuṟaippaṭi āṭumpōtu, muṉ āṭṭaikaḷil tōṟṟavar veṉṟavariṭam tamakku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ vāṅkik koḷvar. veṉṟavar kaṭaṉ koṭāviṭiṉ, irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷai avvaintākak kuḻikaḷil pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta allatu aintu kal illāta kuḻikaḷai veṟumaiyāka viṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum. avveṟum kuḻikaṭkup pavvīkkuḻikaḷ eṉṟu peyar. avaṟṟil ovvoru kuccu iṭappaṭum. oruvarum avaṟṟil āṭal kūṭātu.
tōṟṟavarkku oru kuḻiyum nirampātapōtu (atāvatu aintu kaṟkum kuṟaivāka irukkumpōtu) irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷai ov voṉṟākak kuḻikaḷil pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta kuḻikaḷaip pavvīk kuḻikaḷāka viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu etiriyārum tam varicaiyiluḷḷa ellāk kuḻikaṭkum ovvoru kallē pōṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvoru kallē pōṭṭu āṭum muṟaikkuk kañci kāyccutal eṉṟu peyar. avvaintu kal pōṭṭu āṭit tōṟṟavar, kañcikāycci āṭumpōtu vella iṭamuṇṭu. orumuṟai veṉṟavar maṟumuṟai muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum.
ōr āṭṭai muṭintapiṉ, veṟṟiyum tōlviyumiṉṟi iruvarum camamākak kaṟkaḷ vaittiruppiṉ, aṭutta āṭṭaiyil caripāṇṭi  āṭal vēṇṭum. mutalāvatu avvaintākavum piṉpu pappattākavum kaṟkaḷaiyeṭuttu, attaṉaiyē pōṭṭu āṭuvatu, caripāṇṭiyāṭalākum. itil viraintu viḷaivu kāṇalām.
II kaṭṭuk kaṭṭal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : nāṉku mūlaikkuṅ kaṭṭukkaṭṭi āṭum paṇṇāṅkuḻi viḷaiyāṭṭuvakai, kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār tokai:  iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi :  iru camavaricaiyākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkaintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟai: āṭakar ovvoruvarum tattam varicaiyiṉ iṭakkōṭi (atāvatu iṭappuṟak kaṭaici)k kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki āṭal vēṇṭum. eṭutta kaṟkaḷaiyellām ovvoṉṟākap pōṭṭapiṉ, kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kaṟkaḷaiyeṭuttu āṭal vēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē oru kallēṉum iruntāltāṉ, atiṉiṉṟu eṭukka muṭiyum; illāviṭṭāl (atāvatu pōṭṭa piṉ oru kallēyiruntāl) eṭukka muṭiyātu. veṟuṅ kuḻiyait tuṭaittu aṭutta kuḻiyiliruntēṉum, piṟa vakaiyi lēṉum, kaṟkaḷai eṭuttu vaittuk koḷḷutal ivviḷai yāṭṭil illai. oru mūlaikkuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē mūṉṟu kalliruppiṉ, nālāvatu kallaip pōṭumpōtu, ataik kuḻikkuḷ pōṭāmal kuḻikkup pakkamāka vaittuviṭa vēṇṭum. ituvē kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭuvatu. kaṭṭuk kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil, kaṭṭiṉavarē mēṟkoṇṭu kalpōṭa muṭiyum;  aṭuttavar pōṭamuṭiyātu. iṅṅaṉam oruvarēyō iruvarumākavō nāṉku mūlaikkum kaṭṭuk kaṭṭik koḷḷalām. kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil kal pōṭalāmēyaṉṟi eṭukka muṭiyātu. ātalāl, kaṭṭukkaṭṭiya kuḻikaḷil mēlum  mēlum kal cērntu koṇṭēyirukkum. ellāk kaṟkaḷum nāṉku mūlaikkum pōyc cērntapiṉ avaravar kaṭṭiya kuḻikkaṟkaḷai yeṭuttu eṇṇal vēṇṭum. mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyavar veṉṟava rāvar.
III aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum
āṭṭiṉ peyar: aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum eṉa mūvar āṭum paṇṇāṅ kuḻiyāṭṭuvakai, avar puṉaipatavip peyaraiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭukaruvi : kaṭṭukkaṭṭaṟkup pōṉṟē, iru camavaricai yākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkaintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, itai āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟai : iru varicaik kuḻikaḷuḷḷum, naṭu iru mūṉṟu aracaṉuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟum, iṭappuṟa īriraṇṭu mantiriyuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟum, valappuṟa īriraṇṭu cēvakaṉuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟum koṇṭu, aracaṉ mantiri cēvakaṉ eṉṟa varicaiyoḻuṅkil, āṭakar mūvarum āṭal vēṇṭum.
ovvoruvarum, tāntām āṭum ovvoru taṭavaiyum, tattam kuḻiyiliruntē toṭaṅki āṭalvēṇṭum. āyiṉ, ellār kuḻikaḷilum varicaippaṭi kaṟkaḷaip pōṭalām. ovvoṉ ṟākak kaṟkaḷaip pōṭṭu muṭinta piṉ, kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaiyeṭuttu muṉpōl āṭavēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi veṟumaiyāka iruppiṉ atait tuṭaittu ataṟkaṭutta kuḻiyi luḷḷa kaṟkaḷaiyellām eṭuttu vaittukkoḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭalvēṇṭum.  kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻikaḷum veṟumaiyāka iruppiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. veṟuṅkuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷai eṭukkumpōtu, yārum yār kāyaiyum eṭukkalām.
avaravar kuḻikaḷiluḷḷa pacuvai avaravar eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ellāk kāyum eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭa piṉ, aracaṉ ēṉaiyiruvarkkum ovvoṉṟu koṭuttuviṭavēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, avaṉukku 6 kuḻi nirampiṉāl (atāvatu 30 kāy iruntāl), avaṉukku veṟṟiyām. aṉṟi, yār mikunta kāy eṭukkiṉṟaṉarō avarukkē veṟṟiyām. veṉṟavar tamakkaṭutta mēṟpataviyum tōṟṟavar tamakkaṭutta kīḻppataviyum aṭaital vēṇṭum. aracaṉ velliṉ taṉ pataviyaik kāttuk koḷ vāṉ. āyiṉ, aracaṉum mantiriyum mūṉṟumuṟai toṭarntu paḻamāyiṉ, kīḻppatavi yaṭaital vēṇṭum.
ulaka vāḻkkaiyiluḷḷa ēṟṟiṟakkam ivviḷaiyāṭṭāl uṇarttap paṭuvatu, kavaṉikkattakkatu.
IV acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvar tamittirukkumpōtu tāmāy āṭik koḷḷum paṇṇāṅkuḻiyāṭṭuvakai, acōka vaṉattil taṉittiruntatākac collappaṭum cītaiyoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu.
āṭu karuvi : kaṭṭuk kaṭṭaṟkuriyavaiyē itaṟkum.
āṭumuṟai : irukuḻi varicaiyuḷḷum, mēlvaricaiyil, muṟaiyē oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintum āṟum ēḻumāka mēṉmē lēṟṟa muṟaiyilum, kīḻvaricaiyil, muṟaiyē ēḻum āṟum aintum nāṉkum mūṉṟum iraṇṭum oṉṟumāka mēṉmē liṟakka muṟaiyilum, iṭavalamākak kāykaḷaip pōṭṭuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi, mēl varicaiyil mēṉmē liṟakka muṟaiyilum kīḻvaricaiyil mēṉmēlēṟṟa muṟaiyilum pōṭṭuk koḷḷalām. eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, iruvaricaiyilum ēṟṟiṟakka muṟai māṟi yiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatē vitiyām.
toṭaṅkumpōtu kīḻ varicaiyiluḷḷa peruntokaik kuḻiyi ṉiṉṟu (atāvatu 7 kāykaḷ uḷḷa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu) toṭaṅkal vēṇṭum. kāykaḷaik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭuc cellum pōtu, piṟavakai yāṭṭukkaḷiṟ pōṉṟē kīḻvaricaiyil iṭa valamākavum mēl varicaiyil valaiṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cella vēṇṭum. kāykaḷaip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta peruntokaik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu kāy kaḷai eṭuttāṭavēṇṭum. atu kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭuttēyirukkum.
veṟuṅkuḻi amaivatum, atait tuṭaittu aṭutta kuḻiyi luḷḷa kāykaḷai eṭuttukkoḷvatum, ivviḷai yāṭṭil illai.
mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu (atāvatu eṉṟum oru varicaiyiluḷḷa perun tokaik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kāyeṭuttu) oṉpatu muṟai toṭarntu āṭiṉ. āṭṭan toṭaṅku muṉ pōṭṭirunta vāṟē, avvak kuḻikaḷiṟ kāykaḷ amaintuviṭum. aṅṅaṉam irāviṭiṉ āṭiṉatu tavaṟeṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷalām.
oruvar tamittāṭutaṟkēṟṟa viruppattai ivviḷaiyāṭṭu uṇṭupaṇṇa vallatu.
kuṟippu :- paṇṇāṅkuḻi peṇpiḷḷaikaṭkē ēṟṟa tāyiṉum, paiyaṉ kaḷālum āṭappaṭuvatu uṇṭu.
paṇṇiyam
paṇ - paṇṇiyam = paṇṇappaṭṭa palvēṟu poruḷ.
koṭuppōr kaṭaitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa  (maṇi : 7 : 124).
vaṭavar, meccattakkatu, vāṅkavum viṟṟaṟkum uriyatu eṉṟum ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil vevvēṟu mūlapporuḷ kūṟuvar.  (va.va : 199).
paṇṇai
uḻavar ceyyum payirttoḻilaic ciṟuvar naṭittāṭum āṭṭu paṇṇai...  (ta.vi.muka.kū.)
paṇpaṭṭa moḻi
iyaṟkaimoḻi allatu muḻaittal moḻi muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭaintapiṉ paṇpaṭṭa moḻi (Culticulate Language) allatu iḻaittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) tōṉṟiṟṟu. muṟaiyē cēymai aṇmai muṉmaiyuṇarttum M.ī.C eṉṉum muccuṭṭukkaḷiṉiṉṟē iḻaittal moḻi perumpālum uruvāyiṟṟu. am mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum muṉmaic cuṭṭākiya ūkāramē tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakutiyaip piṟappittuḷḷatu.  (ta.i.va.muṉṉurai.)
paṇpāṭu eṉṉum col viḷakkam
paṇpaṭuvatu paṇpāṭu. paṇpaṭutal cīrppaṭutal, allatu tiruntu tal. tiruntiya nilattaip paṇpaṭṭa allatu paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭa nilameṉṟum, tiruntiya tamiḻaip paṇpaṭṭa centamiḻ (taṉippāṭal) eṉṟum, tiruntiya vuḷḷattaip paṇpaṭṭavuḷḷa meṉṟum, colvatu vaḻakkam.
paṇ eṉṉum peyarc coṟku mūlamāṉa paṇṇutal eṉṉum viṉaic collum, ciṟappāka āḷappeṟumpōtu, palvēṟu viṉaikaḷait tiruntac ceytalaiyum palvēṟu poruḷkaḷaic cevvaiyāy amaittalaiyum, kuṟikkum.
paṇṇutal = 1. nilattait tiruttutal.
(paṇṇappaṭṭa marutanilam paṇṇai).
2. 	ūrtiyait takutip paṭuttutal.
	pūnutal yāṉaiyoṭu puṉaitēr paṇṇavum  (puṟam.12)
3. 	cuvaṭittal (alaṅkarittal)
	paṭṭamo ṭilaṅkal paṇṇi  (cūḷā. kalyā. 14)
4.	icaiyala kamaittal.
	paṇṇal parivaṭṭaṇai yārāytal  (cīvaka. 657, urai)
5. 	paṇ amaittal.
	marutam paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ  (malaipaṭu. 534).
6. 	camaittal.
pālu mitavaiyum paṇṇātu peṟukuvir   (malaipaṭu. 417).
cey eṉṉum viṉaic colliṉiṉṟu, tirunta allatu aḻakāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil, cey eṉṉum nilappeyarum ceyyuḷ eṉṉum iyaṟṟamiḻppāṭṭiṉ peyarum tōṉṟiyiruppatu pōṉṟē; paṇ eṉṉum viṉaic colliṉiṉṟum, tirunta allatu iṉitāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil, paṇṇai eṉṉum nilap peyarum paṇ eṉṉum icait tamiḻ amaippiṉ peyarum; tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
paṇpāṭu pala poruṭku uriyatēṉum, nilamum makkaḷ uḷḷa mum paṟṟiyē peruvaḻakkākap pēcappeṟum. āṅkilattil Culture eṉṉum peyarc col ciṟappāka nilap paṇpāṭṭaiyum uḷap paṇpāṭṭaiyum kuṟippatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. cultivate eṉṉum viṉaiccollum aṅṅaṉamē. ivviruvakaip paṇpāṭṭuḷḷum, makkaḷait taḻuviya uḷappaṇpāṭē ciṟappākak koḷḷavum collavum peṟum.
cēlaṅ kallūrimēṉāḷ mutalvar pērā. irāmacāmik kavuṇṭar kuṭumpam paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻk kuṭumpattiṟkum, tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻait talaimaiyākap pōṟṟum ceṭṭikuḷam paṇpaṭṭavūrukkum, talai ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭām.
uḷḷam paṇpaṭuvatu perumpālum kalviyālātalāl, paṇpāṭu kalvi mikutiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
tamiḻil taṉmaiyaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuḷ, iyalpu eṉpatu iyaṟkaiyāṉa taṉmaiyaiyum, paṇpu eṉpatu paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟa nalla taṉmaiyaiyum, kuṟikkum.
paṇimoḻi
ettuṇaik kaṭuñciṉattaiyum eḷitil taṇikkum iṉimaiyum tāḻmaiyum kalanta vaciyac coṟkaḷ.  (ti.ma. 1258)
paṇaiyam
pantayap poruḷ. vaṭṭiṉi eṉṟum peyar peṟum.  (ti.ma.933).
patti
patti - pakti (bh)
paku - paj (bh). pattaṉ - pakta (bh). iṅṅaṉam paku, pattaṉ, patti eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum, paj, pakta, pakti eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷum, orē mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruppatāl, vaṭacoṟkaṭkuk kāṭṭiya moḻipporuṭ kāraṇamē teṉcoṟkum okkum eṉa aṟika.  (va.va).
pakta = pakukkappeṟṟatu, pakutiyāṉatu, pāṟpaṭṭatu, pāṟpaṭṭavaṉ, paṟṟuḷḷavaṉ, pōṟṟupavaṉ, vaṇaṅkupavaṉ, vaḻipaṭupavaṉ, aṭiyāṉ (mā.vi.a.).
pakti = pakukkap peṟṟamai, pakutiyāṉamai, pāṟpaṭṭamai, pōṟṟukai, vaṇaṅkukai, vaḻipaṭukai, tēvaṭimai (mā.vi.a.).
patti eṉṉum colliṉ vaṭiva varalāṟṟaiyum, ataṉ potupporuḷai yum, paku eṉṉum colliṉ kīḻk kāṇka.
paṟṟu eṉpatu irutiṇaip poruḷkaḷ mītumuḷḷa aḻuntiya ācaiyaiyē kuṟikkum; patti eṉpatu iṟaivaṉiṭattum talaivaṉiṭattuṅ koṇṭa accattoṭu kūṭiya uṇmaiyāṉa paṇi vaṉpaiyē kuṟikkum.  (va.va)
pattup poruttaṅkaḷ

ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (gerunidial form)
pati - tal = (ce.kuṉṟā.vi). 1. pativēṭṭil eḻututal, to register, enter in writing. 2. peyarp paṭṭiyiṟ cērttal, to take on the roll.
(ce. kuṉṟiya.vi.) 1. āḻtal, to sink in, as the foot or a wheel in mud; to enter, penetrate, as into a soft body. vaṇṭic cakkaram cēṟṟiṟ patikiṟatu. (ulaka vaḻakku). 2. tāḻntiruttal, (to be low-lying. as land; to be depressed, sunk, hollow, worn away. nilam patintirukkiṟatu. (u.va.). 3. taṅkutal, to settle, abid; to perch, roost, as a bird, paticeṉṟu patintaṉaṉ (taṇikaippu piramaṉ. 2.) 4. nilaiyātal, to be permanent, as a post. eṉakku araṇmaṉaiyiṟ patinta vēlai yillai. (nāñ.u.va.) 5. ūṉṟutal, to be absorbed, engrossed, involved, as the mind in any object. maṉam pāṭattiṟ patikiṟātā? (u.va.). 6. muttirai, eḻuttaccu mutaliyaṉa aḻuntutal, to be imprinted, impressed, marked, stamped, engraven, indented. 7. atikāram peṟutal, to be invested with power, authority. (W.). 8. iṟaṅkutal, to be decline after meridinal transits; to be near setting, as a heavenly body; to descend, alight, as a bird. 9. vilai taṇital, to be low, as price. vilaiyaip patiyak kēṭṭāṉ. (u.va.) 10. amaitiyātal, to be mild, gentle or tractalle; to become submissive, modest or humble. paiyaṉ paḷḷikkūṭattiṟ patintirukkiṟāṉ. (u.va.) 11. añci yiṇaṅkutal allatu piṉvāṅkutal, to quail, flinch.
peyareccam : (adjectival participle)
i. kā. pe. patinta nilam low land.
ceyaṟpeyarum toḻiṟpeyarum : (gerund and verbal noun.)
patital, patikai, pativatu, the act of registering, sinking etc.
pativu, registration, sinking etc.
toḻiṟpeyarum, toḻilākupeyarum : (Verbal noun and metonymical verbal noun.)
pati = 1. patikai, penetration, transfixion, thrust. nuṇṇilaivēl patikoṇṭu, (cīvaka. 1186). 2. patiyam, cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 3. nāṟṟu, sapling for transplantation. 4. pativiḷakku, lamp fixed on a pot while exercising devils (W.). 5. ciwÉl«, abode, residence (âth.), patiyiṟ kalaṅkiya mīṉ (kuṟaḷ. 1116), 6. vīṭu, home, house (tivā.). 7. kōyil, temple (caṅ. aka.) 8. kuṟi collum iṭam. an oracular shrine, patiyirunta patiyellām pativākac ceṉṟēṉ (nāñ.marumak.mā.) 9. ūr, town, city, village. patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi (cilap. 1 : 15) 10. ñālam (pūmi), the earth. (tailava. taiva.). 11. kutirai, horse. (aka. ni).
pativu = 1. aḻuntukai, impression, indentation. 2. paḷḷam, lowness of a surface; depression. nilam pativāyirukkiṟatu. (u.va.) 3. patiyam. cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 4. viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ cāyvu declination of a heavenly body. (W.) 5. eḻuvataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ) nilai, situation of the moon before its rise. (W.) 6. patukkam, stooping, crouching, lurking as a thief or a beast ready to spring on its prey; ambush. 7. maṉam ūṉṟukai. engrossment, absorption in an object or pursuit. 8. kaṇakkup patikai, registering, entering, as in account. 9. pativu ceyyappaṭṭatu. that which is registered. 10. patiyap paṭṭatu. registry, entry. 11. tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭa celavu, allotment, as in a budget. intak kōvilil caṅku ūtuvataṟkup pativu ēṟpaṭavillai (nāñ.u.va.) 12. nilaippu (nilaivaram), permanence. intavūril avaṉ pativāyirukkiṟāṉ (u.va.). 13. vaḻakkam, custom, habit. 14. amaiti, submission, obedience, humility.  pativāy naṭantukoḷ (u.va.) 15. vilaittaṇivu. lowness of price. (W.).
patiyam = 1. nāṟṟu muṭiyai iraṇṭoru nāṭkuc cēṟṟil patittu vaikkai, temorary planting of a cluster of saplings in mud. 2. ūṉṟi naṭuñ ceṭi koṭi kiḷai mutaliyaṉa, slip, shoot, graft. 3. ilaippāci, a species of duckweed. ceṟiyaṇip patiyattiṭai vaḷariḷañ cēlkaḷ (iraku. nāṭṭup. 7).

(W.). 3. ceyyuḷaṭi, (Pros.) unit of metrical measure; line of stanza vāṅkarum pātanāṉkum vakutta vāṉmīki (kamparā. pāla. nāṭṭup. 1.) 4. malai maram mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭiyiṭam; base, as of a mountain, a tree (W.). 5. kāṟpaṅku quarter (cūṭā). muṉṉaip pīṭattiṉ pātaṅ kuṟaintu (mēruman. 1172). 6. vaṭṭattiṉ kāṟpaṅku; quadrant of a circle. 7. veḷḷik kāl (naṭcattira pātam); a fourth part of a duration of a naksatra.

pativāḷar, pe. 1. āvaṇam, oppantam, caṭṭatiṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟaip pativu ceyyum araciyal aluvalar. Registrar of title-deeds, contracts, rules and regulations of corporations etc. (ikkāla vaḻakku). 2. palkalaikkaḻaka aluvalakat talaivar; Registrar of a University (i.va.).
pativiṭam = pe. oḷittirukkumiṭam; hiding place, ambush.
pativiḷakku = pe. pēyōṭṭutaṟkākac kuṭattiṉ mēṟ patiyavaitta viḷakku; lamp fixed on a pot while exorcizing devils (W.).
pativuc cāppāṭu = pe. uṇṭiccālaiyil mātak kaṇakkāka ēṟpāṭu ceytu uṇṇum uṇavu; regular boarding at a hotel or mess on monthly account.
pativēṭu = pe. kaṇakkup patiyum pottakam; register, account-book, ledger.

pati-ttal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. aḻuttutal, to imprint, impress, stamp, engrave, as in mind; to plunge. pattik kaṭaluṭ patitta parañcōti (tiruvāca. 11 : 12). 2. maṇi mutaliyaṉa iḻaittal; to infix, insert, ingrant, inlay, as gems; to enchase. 3. kaṟpāvutal; to pave, as floor with bricks or stones. 4. tāḻttutal, to lower height, price, etc; to set lower, insert deeper. 5. patiyam pōṭutal; to plant a custer of saplings temporarily in mud; to plact a shoot, runner or creeper. 6. acciṭutal, to print, reprint, edit. 7. eḻututal to inter in a register. 8. atikāraṅ koṭuttal; to invest with power, authority or prerogative (W.).

paḷ - paṭu - paṭi - pati. paḷ - paḷḷam.

iṉac col  (cognates and allied words)
1. tiraviṭam 
malaiyāḷam - pati, pativu.
patikka, to impress. patiyuka, to be impressed, be pressed down. pati, being fixed in, pressed down. pativu, settlement, custom.
patam, softness, elasticity, yielding temer. patama, patuma. pliancy. patarmma, rottenness of rice through damp, softness of mind. patukka, to be soft, tender, pliable. patuppu, softness. patuppikka, to soften. patam, the right degree of ripeness, temperature, etc.
teluṅku - pataṉu, patuṉu, moisture, dampness, wetness; ripeness maturity temper.
kaṉṉaṭam - pata, proper or good state or condition, proper degree or temperature, the seasoning of any food, the right degree of a ripeness, keeness of edge or sharpness hata, proper condition.
tuḷuvam - pataṉuṉi, pataṇuṉi, to become soft.
kuṭakam - pata soft.
kōttam - patm, temper of iron.
mālattam - petke, to be soft. patō, sharp.
kuvī - pete. soft and damp.
pirākuvī - putēṉ, cold, cool. putī, coldness, frost.
2. āriyam
pataṉam - va. (Skt.) pataṉa.
patavi - va. (Skt.) patavī.
patam - pātam - va. (Skt.) pata, pāta. Gk. pod. L. ped, pedis, OE, OS fot, OHG. fuoz, ON. fotr. Goth, fotus, E. foot.
pātai - OE. poeth. OLG. pad, OHG. pfad, E.path.
camaṟkirutattiṟ pātai eṉṉum col illātatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. pata eṉṉum collē atiṟ pātaiyaik kuṟikkum.
ped eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟē, pedal, pedate, pedestal, peduncle eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa.
vati - vaci - va. (Skt.) va. iv vaṭacollē āṅkilattil was eṉṉum iṟanta kālat tuṇaiviṉaic collākat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
“was. - The OE. weson, to be, is eognate with Goth, wisan; ON. vera. to be, abide; Skt. vas. to dwell.” (P.266)
“were = OE. woreon, where is for original S.” (P. 267).
eṉṟu iriccārṭu māricu (Richard Morris) tam āṅkilac coṟṟiripu varalāṟṟuc caṭṭakam (Historiacal Outlines of English Accidence) eṉṉum nūlil varaintiruttal kāṇka. iv viṟanta kālac tuṇaiviṉaic col maṭṭumiṉṟi, is, are eṉṉum nikaḻkālat tuṇaiviṉaic coṟkaḷum iru eṉṉum tamiḻccol tiripē.
tamiḻt takaram vaṭamoḻiyil sakaramākat tirivatai māsa eṉṉum collālum uṇarka. mati - mātam - māsa(va.) - mā(i).
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
pata, pāta eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, tamiḻp pulavar palar avaṟṟai vaṭacolleṉṟē mayaṅki aṭi eṉṉum collaip pātam eṉṉum poruḷil āṇṭuvarukiṉṟaṉar. aṭi vēṟu; pātam vēṟu. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippakutiyaiyum ōr uyiriyiṉ kālaiyum pātattaiyum kuṟikkum potuc col. e-ṭu: aṭittaṭṭu, mayilaṭi, tiruvaṭi, aṭip pakuti yeṉpatu kīḻ iṭattaiyum kīḻp poruḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. e-ṭu : pantalaṭi, tēraṭi, ciṟiya tiruvaṭi periya tiruvaṭi. maravaṭi eṉpatu kālpōṉṟa aṭimaram. pātam eṉpatō nilattiṟ patiyum paranta aṭiyuṟuppaiyē allatu pakutiyaiyē kuṟikkum ciṟappuc col. e-ṭu : pātat tāmarai, pātam vaitta viḷakku. ākavē, patital eṉpatai vērp poruḷākak koṇṭa patam, pātam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum tūya teṉ collēyeṉṟum, avaiyē muṟaiyē pata, pāta eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum eṉṟum aṟika. iṉi, pātam paṭuvatāl ēṟpaṭum vaḻiyaik kuṟikkum pātai yeṉṉum col, āṅkilattil (path eṉa) iruppataiyum vaṭamoḻiyil iṉmaiyaiyum, nōkkuka.
va (vaci), eṉṉum camaṟkiruta viṉaic coṟkum. ataṉ tirivāṉa  ‘was’ eṉṉum āṅkila iṟanta kāla viṉaiccoṟkum, vati eṉṉum tamiḻccol mūlamāyiruppatiṉiṉṟu, tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum muṉmaiyaiyum aṟika.
tiruttam
patam, pātam, patavi, pataṉam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ tirivāṉa pata, pāta, patavī, pataṉa eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷai, avaṟṟiṉ mūlamāṉa teṉcoṟkaṭkē mūlameṉac ceṉṉai yakara mutaliyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu, mūla vaḻu eṉa aṟika.
patikam
patikam - pratīka
pal - patu - pattu. patu - patikam = 1. oru poruḷpaṟṟi varum orē vakaip pattuc ceyyuḷ.
2. pattu allatu patiṉoru kuṟippaik kūṟum ciṟappup pāyiram.
ākkiyōṉ peyarē vaḻiyē ellai
nūṟpeyar yāppē nutaliya poruḷē
kēṭpōr payaṉō ṭāyeṇ poruḷum
vāyppak kāṭṭal pāyirat tiyalpē.  (naṉ. 47)
kālaṅ kaḷaṉē kāraṇam eṉṟim
mūvakai yēṟṟi moḻinarum uḷarē.  ( 48).  (va.va : 199).
tolkāppiyam, naṉṉūl, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappuppāyiram, perumpālum immuṟaiyait taḻuvi amaintavaiyē.
mā.vi.a. prati - aka eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu pratīka eṉṉuñ collaik kāṭṭi, nōkkiyatu. nōkku, tōṟṟam, mukam, muṟpakuti, ceyyuḷiṉ muṟpakuti mutaṟcol eṉṟu palvēṟu mēṟkoḷkaḷai iṭarppaṭṭup porutti, vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷait toṭarpupaṭuttiyiruppatu, ataṉ ceyaṟkait taṉmaiyait teḷivākap pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu.  (va.va : 199 -200).
panti
panti - paṅkti (a.vē.)
pakkam, pākam eṉṉum iraṇṭum paku eṉṉum orē mutaṉilai yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyirukkavum, vaṭavar avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatai āriya iyalpu vallolip pakarattilum (p), piṉṉataik kaṉaippolip pakarattilum (bh), toṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkēṟpa, pakkam eṉpataṟkup pakṣ eṉpataiyum, pakavaṉ, paṅku, pākam eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup paj (bhaj) eṉpataiyum, mūlamākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam orumūlac coṟkaḷaiyē vevvēṟu mūlattiṉavākak kāṭṭuvatu vaṭavar vaḻakkam.
pakṣ eṉpatu pakku eṉpataṉ tiripu. tātupāṭa eṉṉum nūl ataṟkup paṟṟu, koḷ, eṭu eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟum. āyiṉ, vilcaṉ akaramutali pakkam (pakṣa) eṉṉum coṟkēṟpap pakkañ cārtal eṉṟu poruḷuraikkum.  (va.va.)
pakkam eṉṉum coṟkut tamiḻilum, pakuti, kūṟu, nūṟpakuti, nūl, puṟam (side), ēṭṭup puṟam (page), aruku, aṇmai, iṭam, vīṭu, nāṭu, vilāppuṟam, ciṟaku. iṟaku, ampiṟaku, kai, kaiyaṇi, naṭpu, aṉpu, cuṟṟam, marapu (vamicam), cēṉaippakuti, cēṉai, patiṉaintu piṟaināṭkālam, piṟaināḷ, mēṟkōḷ (Proposition), tuṇiporuṭ kūṟṟu, taṉmai eṉap palaporuḷkaḷuṇṭu.
kai, ciṟaku eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pakkattaik kuṟippatupōṉṟē pakkam eṉṉum collum kaiyaiyum ciṟakaiyum kuṟikkum. vaṭavar ciṟakup poruḷiṉiṉṟu paṟavaip poruḷai virittiruk kiṉṟaṉar eṉak karutalām.
pakkam - pakṣa - pakṣi = ciṟakaiyuṭaiyatu.
iṉi, caturakkaḷḷiyil taṅkum oruvakaip paṟavai pakki eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. pakki yuvaṇaṅ kaḻuku (tiruppu.319) eṉa aruṇakiri nātarum pāṭiyuḷḷār.  va.va.
paka (bhaga) eṉṉum irukkuvētac coṟku, “dispenser”, gracious lord, patron (applied to gods, esp. to Savitri), R.V.” eṉṟu mā. vi. a. poruḷ vakukkiṉṟatu.
ākavē, pakavaṉ eṉṉum coṟku, pakuttaḷippavaṉ, paṭiyaḷappavaṉ eṉpatē mūlapporuḷām. āṇṭavaṉ paṭiyaḷappāṉ. eṉṟu aṭikkaṭi makkaḷ kūṟuvatai nōkkuka.
namakkum paṭiyaḷappār nāriyōr pākar  (taṉippāṭal).
pakavaṉ eṉpatu pala teyvaṅkaṭkum potuppeyarāy vaḻaṅkiya tiṉālēyē, muḻumutal teyvamākiya kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikka āti pakavaṉ eṉṟu aṭaikoṭuttuk kūṟiṉār vaḷḷuvar.
ātipakavaṉ = ātippakavaṉ.
vali iṭaimikātē pala kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.  va.va.
e-ṭu : mārikālam, kār kālam.
panti eṉpatu patti eṉpataṉ melittal tiripē.
āyiṉ, vaṭavar pañcaṉ (c) eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttip pac (c) eṉpatai mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. ataṉ viḷakkam varumāṟu:-
pac allatu pañc = viri, kaiviri.
pañc - pañcaṉ = viritta kaiyiluḷḷa aintuviral, aintu.
pañcaṉ - paṅkti = aimporuḷ tokuti allatu varicai, tokuti, varicai, kūṭṭam.
vaṭavar ēmāṟṟukkalaiyaik kāṭṭa iḵtoṉṟē amaiyum.  (va.va : 194 - 195).
pantu viḷaiyāṭṭu
tuṇiyālum kayiṟṟālum iṟukak kaṭṭappaṭṭa uruṇṭaiyai eṟintāṭum āṭṭu, pantu eṉappaṭum (pantu = uruṇṭaiyāṉatu).
I. pēyppantu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēyttaṉamāka oruvaṉmēloruvaṉ eṟintāṭum pantu, pēyppantu eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalām.
āṭuvār tokai : perumpālum nālvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar āṭuvatē ivviḷaiyāṭṭiṟkēṟṟatām. āṭuvār tokai perukapperuka iv āṭṭuc ciṟakkum.
āṭu karuvi : āṭuvār ettuṇaiyarāyiṉum oru pantē iv āṭṭiṟkuriyatām.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalum veḷinilamum itai āṭu miṭamām.
āṭu muṟai : palar iṭaiyiṭṭu niṉṟu koṇṭirukka avaruḷ oruvaṉ pantai mēlē pōṭṭup piṭittuk koṇṭu, pantē pantu eṉṟu urakkakkattuvāṉ. piṟar eṉṉa pantu eṉṟu kēṭpar. avaṉ pēyp pantu eṉpāṉ. yār mēlē eṉṟu oruvaṉ kēṭka, avaṉ uṉmēlē eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu avaṉmēl vaṉmaiyāy eṟivāṉ. atu avaṉmēl paṭṭālum paṭum; paṭātupōṉālum pōm. pantu yār kaippaṭṭatō avaṉ atai ōṅki yār mēlum eṟivāṉ. iṅṅaṉam viruppa muḷḷavarai māṟi māṟi aṭittu āṭik koṇṭēyiruppar. yār eṟiyiṉum avaṉuṭaiya valimaikkut takkavāṟu vaṉmaiyāy eṟivatē vaḻakkam.
āṭṭuttōṟṟam : kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāyiṉiṉṟō, pēyc ceyalākak karutappaṭṭa oru pantu vīḻcciyiṉiṉṟō, iv āṭci tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
viḷā māttilirukkum kuraṅkaik kallāleṟiya atu viḷaṅkāy koṇṭeṟivatu, kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāyām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : caṟṟut tolaivil niṟkum allatu iyaṅkum irutiṇaip poruḷkaḷmēlun tappātu eṟitaṟkēṟṟa payiṟciyai, iv viḷaiyāṭṭu aḷikkum. vēṭṭaiyāṭal, pōr ceytal, tiruṭaṉaik kallāl aṭittal mutaliya viṉaikaṭku ippayiṟci ēṟṟatām.
II. piḷḷaiyār pantu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : piḷḷaiyāraik kuṟikkum oru kalliṉmēṟ pantai eṟintāṭum āṭṭu piḷḷaiyār pantu. itu tirucci vaṭṭārattiṟ piḷḷaiyār viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār tokai : potuvāka, eṇmarkkuk kuṟaiyāta palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi : ēṟattāḻa āṟaṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa oru kallum, oru pantum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭiṭam : cuvaraṭiyum ataiyaṭutta veḷi nilamum itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : āṭuvōrellārum camat tokaiyiṉavāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar. oru ceṅkallai allatu ciṟu nīḷak kallaip piḷḷaiyārākap pāvittu oru cuvaraṭiyil ciṟitu maṇalaik kuvittu atil atai naṭṭu, oru kaṭciyār aiṅkacat tolaivil etirniṉṟu ovvoruvarāy ovvoru taṭavai piḷḷaiyāraip paṭukiṭaiyākac cāyttaṟkup pantālaṭikka, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār iru pakkattilum piḷḷaiyārkkum avarukkum iṭaiyil varicaiyāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, eṟiyap paṭṭa pantaip piṭikka muyalvar. piḷḷaiyāraip paṭukiṭai yāyc cāytta pantai yārēṉum antarattiṟ piṭittu viṭiṉum, eṟinta pantu piḷḷaiyārmēṟ paṭāviṭiṉum, paṭṭum ataip paṭukiṭaiyāyc cāykkāviṭiṉum, eṟiyuṅ kaṭciyār āḷ māṟikkoṇṭēyiruntu aṉaivaruntīrntapiṉ, etirk kaṭciyār aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārākavum aṭitta kaṭciyār piṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārākavum, māṟal vēṇṭum.
piḷḷaiyāraip paṭukiṭaiyāyc cāytta pantu piṭikkap paṭāviṭiṉ, piṭikka niṉṟa kaṭciyar aṉaivarum uṭaṉē oṭip pōyc caṟṟut tolaivil iṭaiyiṭṭu niṟpar. aṭitta kaṭciyāraṉaivarum nerukkamākak kūṭi niṉṟu, avaruḷ oruvaṉ pantait taṉ aṭi vayiṟṟiṉmēl vaittu atu veḷikkut teriyāmal iru kaiyālum pottik koṇṭum, piṟarum tām pantu vaittiruppatāka etirk kaṭciyārukkut tōṉṟumāṟu taṉittaṉi naṭittukkoṇṭum, avariṭaiyē pirintu celvar. etirkkaṭciyāruḷ yārēṉum oruvaṉ, uṇmaiyāyp pantu vaittiruppavaḷai aiyuṟātu avaṉukkup pakkamāka niṟpiṉ, pantu vaittiruppavaṉ tiṭumeṉṟu avaṉmēl eṟintu viṭuvāṉ. atōṭu ōr āṭṭai muṭiyum. aṭutta āṭṭai āṭuvatu āṭakar viruppattaip poṟuttatu.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, piṟamatap pakaimai paṟṟiyum koḷḷai yaṭittaṟ poruṭṭum, iṭaik kālattiṟ cila aracarum koḷḷait talaivarum teyvaccilaikaḷai (vikkiraṅkaḷai) uṭaittatum kavarntatum, iv viḷaiyāṭṭut tōṟṟattiṟkutk kāraṇamāyiruntirukkalām. kaciṉi makamatu, mālikkāpūr, tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār mutaliyōr ceyalkaḷ, iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
pappaṭam
pappaṭam - parppaṭa (!)
appaḷittal = camaṉākat tēyttal. kavarai appaḷittup pūcukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
appaḷi - appaḷam = camaṉākat tēyttamaikkum uḻuttamā vaṭṭam.
mēvukapan tīrkkuṅkāṇ ōtumuḻun tappaḷam  (patārtta. 1426).
te. appaḷamu - appapamu - ka. appaḷa - pappaḷa. ma. pappaṭam.  va.va : 200
pampara viḷaiyāṭṭu
I. oyākkaṭṭai
ciṟuvar, tam pamparaṅkaḷai vaṭṭattuḷāyiṉum, varampilā nilattilāyiṉum oruṅkē āṭaviṭṭu, yāratu nīṇṭa nēram āṭukiṉṟateṉṟu pārkkum āṭṭu ōyākkaṭṭaiyām. itu mīṇṭum mīṇṭum orē vakaiyāy āṭappeṟum.
II. uṭaitta kaṭṭai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : āṭṭattil tōṟṟavaṉatu pamparattai uṭaikkum āṭṭu uṭaitta kaṭṭai eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟai : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar palar, oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil oru māṅkoṭṭaiyai vaittu, ovvoruvaṉākap pamparattai ataṉmēlēṟṟi, atai veḷiyēṟṟuvar. atu veḷiyēṟiyavuṭaṉ, ellārum oruṅkē viraivākat taṉ taṉ pamparattai āṭṭik kaimēl ēṟṟuvar. mikap pinti ēṟṟiyavaṉ taṉ pamparattai vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil vaittal vēṇṭum.
pamparattai yāṭṭik kaimēlēṟṟumpōtu, apiṭkōcu allatu ciṅkōcu eṉṟu collikkoḷvatāl, aṅṅaṉam ēṟṟuvataṟku, apiṭkōceṭuttal allatu ciṅ kō ceṭuttal eṉṟu peyar.
vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa pamparattai ēṉaiyōrellārum, muṉpu māṅkoṭṭaiyai veḷiyēṟṟiyatu pōl veḷiyēṟṟi, ēṟattāḻa irupatu kacat tolaiviluḷḷa ellaik kōṭ ṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōvar. ovvoruvaṉāka avaṉavaṉ taṉtaṉ pamparattai ataṉmēlēṟṟit taḷḷiyē, ataikkoṇṭu pōtal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam koṇṭu pōmpōtu, yārēṉum maṭṭai vīḻttiṉum cāṭṭai pōkkiṉum, kīḻēyirukkum pamparattiṟkup patilākat taṉ pamparattai vaittuviṭal vēṇṭum. kīḻēyirunta pamparakkāraṉ atai eṭuttup piṟarpōl āṭṭuvāṉ.
pamparam talaikīḻāy viḻutaṟku maṭṭai eṉṟum, pakkamāka uruṇṭupōtaṟkuc cāṭṭai eṉṟum peyar.
yār pamparam ellaik kōṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōkap paṭṭatō, atu uṭaṉē piṟarāl uṭaikkappaṭum.
III. pamparak kuttu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : tōṟṟavaṉatu pamparattaik kutti yāṭum āṭṭu pamparakkuttu eṉappaṭum.
āṭu muṟai : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar palar, oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil māṅkoṭṭai vaittu veḷiyēṟṟi, apiṭkōceṭuttu, atil mikap pintiyavaṉ taṉ pamparattai vaṭṭattuḷ vaitta piṉ, ēṉaiyōrellām ovvoru vaṉākat taṉ taṉ pamparattāṟ kutti atai veḷiyēṟṟuvar. atu veḷiyēṟṟappaṭiṉ uṭaṉē mīṇṭum muṉpōl apiṭ kōceṭuttu, atiṟ pintiyavaṉ taṉ pamparattai vaṭṭattuḷ vaittal vēṇṭum. ēṉaiyarellām muṉpōṟkutti atai veḷiyēṟṟuvar.
maṭṭai vīḻttiyavaṉ pamparamum, cāṭṭai pōkkiyavaṉ pamparamum vaṭṭattuḷ ēṟkeṉavē vaittiruppatuṭaṉ cērttu vaikkappaṭum.
vaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu veḷiyēṟiyāṭum pōtu, atai vaṭṭattuḷ vaittirukkum pamparakkāraṉ cāṭṭaiyāl taṉ kaiyilēṟṟi āṭṭiṉ, atuvum vaṭṭattuḷ vaikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ etaiyēṉum, vaṭṭattuḷ vaikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparakkāraṉ, taṉ kaiyālaḻuttip patittuviṭiṉ, atai eṭuttal kūṭātu.
uḷḷē vaikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparam mēlum mēlum kuttap paṭṭuc cētamākātapaṭi, ataṉ contakkāraṉ āṭṭikkoṇ ṭirukkum pamparakkāraruḷ oruvaṉiṭam, nī iṉṟu eṉ pamparattai veḷiyēṟṟiṉ, uṉ pamparam uḷḷirukkumpōtu nāṉ veḷiyēṟṟuvēṉ, eṉṟu oppantañ ceytukoḷva tuṇṭu.
pala pamparaṅkaḷ uḷḷē vaikkappaṭṭuk kiṭappiṉ, avai yaṉaittum veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa piṉputāṉ apiṭkōcu eṭukkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vēṇṭum aḷavu toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.
vaṭṭattuḷ vaikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ naṇpaṉatai veḷiyēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, piṟaṉatai veḷiyēṟṟak kūṭāteṉṟum, iruvēṟu nōkkuk koṇṭu ataṟkēṟpap pamparattai āṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
IV. iruvaṭṭak kuttu
vaṭṭattuḷ vaṭṭamāka iruvaṭṭam kīṟi avviraṇṭuḷḷum pamparattaik kuttuvatu, iruvaṭṭakkuttu. itu perumpālum pamparak kuttup pōṉṟatē.
itaṉ uḷvaṭṭattil māṅkoṭṭai vaittu veḷiyēṟṟi apiṭ kōceṭuttup pintiyavaṉ pamparattai ataṉuḷ vaittapiṉ, ataik kutti veḷiyēṟṟuvar. maṭṭaiyum cāṭṭaiyum uḷvaikkappaṭum. uḷ vaṭṭattil āṭum pamparam veḷivaṭṭattuḷḷum vantu āṭalām. āṭum pamparattai aḻuttuvatu uḷvaṭṭattiltāṉ nikaḻum; āyiṉ, ataik kuttuvatu iruvaṭṭattilum uṇṭu. veḷivaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparam tāṉāy nakarntu veḷiyēṟi viṭiṉ, atai uṭaiyavaṉ eṭuttu āṭṭalām; aṉṟi uḷḷēyē ōyntuviṭiṉ atai eṭuttal kūṭātu. atuvum maṭṭaipōṟ pāvikkappaṭum.
V. talaiyāri
āṭuvār tokai : mūvarkkuk kuṟaiyāta ciṟuvar palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi : āḷukkoru pamparamum, ēṟattāḻa irupatu kacam iṭaiyiṭṭa iru cama aḷavāṉa vaṭṭaṅkaḷum, oru pōkāka (atāvatu camatūramāka) irupuṟamum nīṭṭappaṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ viṭṭaṅkaḷum, ivviḷaiyaṭṭiṟ kuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭu muṟai : mutalāvatu oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa māṅ koṭṭaiyaip pamparattiṉ mūlamāy veḷiyēṟṟi, apiṭkō ceṭuttu atiṟ pintiyavaṉ pamparattai uḷ vaittu, atai ēṉaiyō rellām ovvoru vaṉāyp pamparamēṟṟi veḷi yēṟṟuvar. aṅṅaṉam veḷiyēṟṟumpōtu vaṭṭattiṟku valappuṟamāy veḷiyēṟṟal vēṇṭum; illā viṭiṉ mīṇṭum apiṭkō ceṭuttup pintiyavaṉ pamparattai uḷḷē vaikka vēṇṭiyirukkum.
veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa pamparattai nērē etiruḷḷa vaṭṭattiṟkup pamparattiṉ mūlamāy aṭittuk koṇṭu pōvar. oruvaṉ iṭaiyil maṭṭai vīḻttiṉum cāṭṭai pōkkiṉum, kīḻirukkum pamparattiṟkup patilākat taṉatai vaittuviṭal vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻirunta pamparakkāraṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu āṭuvāṉ.
aṭittukkoṇṭu pōkappaṭum pamparam etirvaṭṭattuḷ cērnta vuṭaṉ, piṟa pamparakkārarellām tam cāṭṭaikaḷaik kaḻuttiṟ cuṟṟik koṇṭu, ataip pampara āṇiyāl ovvoru taṭavai kuttuvar. kuttumpōtu cāṭṭai taḷarntu nilattait toṭiṉ, kuttappaṭum pamparakkāraṉ taṉ cāṭṭaiyāl vaṉmaiyāy aṭittu viṭuvāṉ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭām vaṭṭattiliruntu mutal vaṭṭattiṟku, muṉpōṉṟē ap pamparam aṭittukkoṇṭu pōkappaṭum. aṅku atai ivviru taṭavai kuttuvar.
ataṉpiṉ, atu iraṇṭām vaṭṭattiṟku mīṇṭuṅ koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭu, mummūṉṟu taṭavai kuttappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṅkumaṅku māka iyakkappaṭṭu, ovvoru muṟaikkum ovvoru taṭavai kūṭutalākak kuttappaṭum.
ivvakaiyil itu virumpiya aḷavu toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.  (ta.nā.vi.)
payirc ceṟivu vakai
putar kuttuc ceṭikaḷiṉ ceṟivu; potai peruñceṭi koṭi kaḷiṉ ceṟivu; potumpar maraṅkaḷiṉ ceṟi.  (col : 72).
payir vakai 
pūcaṉam allatu pūñcaṇam (pūccāḷam) nonta cōṟṟilum īramarattilum tōṉṟum nuṇpāci; pācam nīr mēlum īramuḷḷa iṭattilum tōṉṟum pacumaiyāṉa nuṇpayir; pāci nīril aṭivarai paṭarntuḷḷa pācam; kāḷāṉ īramuḷḷa iṭattil kuṭaipōl tōṉṟum payir vakai; pul aṟuku kucai pōṉṟavai; pūṇṭu veṅkāyam veḷḷaip pūṇṭu pōṉṟavai; ceṭi miḷakāyc ceṭi kattiric ceṭi pōṉṟavai; koṭi avaraik koṭi pākaṟkoṭi pōṉṟavai; putar aṭarnta kuttuc ceṭi; payir nel kampu kēḻvaraku pōṉṟavai; maram mā puḷi pōṉṟavai.  col : 70

paramaṉ
paramaṉ - pirahmā (B)
vaṭavar para eṉṉuñ coṟku uyarccik karuttōṭu, tolaivu, ayaṉmai, piṟitākai, piṉmai mutaliya karuttukkaḷaiyum cērppar. atōṭu parama eṉṉum vaṭivaip para eṉpataṉ uccattaramākak koḷvar.

paricam vāṅkāmai
peṇṇiṟkup paricam kēṭpatu ōraḷavu vilai kūṟuvatu pōṉṟiruttalāl, celvap peṟṟōr. ataṉaik kēḷātiruttal ciṟappām. ēḻaippeṟṟōrāyiṉ, maṇac celavu nōkki oru tokai kēṭpatu kuṟṟamākātu.
iṉi peṇṇiṟkuk kēṭpataṟkup patilāka maṇavāḷap piḷḷaikkuk kēṭpatu, iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉatāyum, ek kāraṇattālum carimaippaṭutta muṭiyātatāyum irukkiṉṟatu. ivvulakattil oruvaṉ peṟakkūṭiya pēriṉpap pēṟu peṇṇē akattaḻakum puṟattaḻakum oruṅkē yamainta arumaip peṇṇirukkavum avaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu, kāciṟkācaippaṭṭu aḻakiliyai maṇappatu, illaṟa iṉpattaiyum vāḻkkai vacatiyaiyum paṇattiṟku viṟpatu pōṉṟatē.  ta.ti. 50 - 51.

paḻattōl vakai
toli mika melliyatu; tōl tiṇṇamāṉatu; tōṭu vaṉmaiyāṉatu; ōṭumika vaṉmaiyāṉatu; kuṭukkai curaiyiṉ ōṭu; maṭṭai tēṅkāy neṟṟiṉ mēṟpakuti; umi nel, kampu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ mūṭi; kommai varaku kēḻvaraku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ umi.  (col. 69).
paḻu
paḻu - pal, paḻam - pala (ph) - i.vē.
paḷ - paṇṭu = paḻam, paḻamai. te. paṇṭu = paḻam.
paḷ - paḻu - paḻam - paḻaṉ, paḻam - payam. paḻaṉ - payaṉ.
vaṭavarum pala eṉṉum collaip payaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil āḷvar.  (va. va : 202).
paḷḷi
pullutal = tuḷaittal. pul = uṭṭuḷai, tuḷai, uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa nilaittiṇai vakai.
pul - puḷ - puḻu. puḻuttal = puḻuttuḷaittal.
pul - pol. pollutal = tuḷaittal. pollam pottutal = nārp peṭṭiyiṉ ōṭṭaiyaṭaittal. pollāmaṇi = tuḷaiyillāmaṇi. pollāp piḷḷaiyār = uḷiyiṭāp piḷḷaiyār paṭimai.
pol - poḷ. poḷḷutal = tuḷaittal. poḷḷal = tuḷai, ōṭṭai.
cītanīr poḷḷaṟ ciṟakuṭattu nillātu
vītalō niṟṟal viyappu  (naṉṉeṟi, 12).
puḷ - paḷ - paḷku. paḷkutal = patuṅkutal, paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil maṟaital. paḷku - paḻku.
paḷ - paḷḷam = 1. tāḻviṭam, 2. tāḻnilam, 3. tāḻmaṭṭam, tāḻvu. kuḻivu. 5. kaṉṉattil viḻuṅkuḻivu. 6. āḻam. 7. kiṭaṅku. 8. kuḻi.
tuḷaittal tōṇṭutal. tōṇṭum nilam mutaliṟ paḷḷamākum; piṉpu kiṭaṅkākum; ataṉpiṉ kuḻiyākum; kuḻi oṉṟai ūṭuruviṉ tuḷaiyākum. tōṇṭat tōṇṭa āḻam mikum.
paḷ - paḷḷi. paḷḷik kiruttal = vitaikaḷ cēṟṟiṟ patintu kiṭattal. nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku).
paḷ - paḷḷai = kuḷḷam. paḷḷaiyaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ, kuṟukip paruttavaṉ. paḷḷaiyāṭu = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭṭuvakai.
kuḷḷam paḷḷampōl tāḻmaṭṭamāyiruttalāl, paḷḷai yeṉappaṭṭatu.
paḷḷai - paḷḷaiyam = tāḻ maṭṭamāṉa (taṭṭaiyāṉa) uṇkalam. ma. paḷḷaiyam.
paḷḷaiyam pōṭutal = ciṟu teyvattiṟkuk kīḻē paṭaittal.
paḷḷi = 1. tāḻvu. 2. tāḻvāṉa iṭam, tāḻ maṭṭam. 3. tāḻvāṉa vīṭu allatu kuṭicai. 4. tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷ cērnta ciṟṟūr. 5. tāḻvāṉa iṭaiccēri. kāvum paḷḷiyum (malaipaṭu. 451). o. nō: paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi = iṭaiccēri paṭuttal = tāḻvāyiruttal. 6. neṭitāy eḻuntu niṟkum nilaiyum kuṟitāy amarntirukkum nilaiyum iṉṟit tāḻvāy nila allatu aṭittaḷa maṭṭamāy nīḷakkiṭakkum nilai. 7. aṅṅaṉaṅ kiṭattal, paṭuttal. 8. paṭukkum iṭam, vilaṅku tuyiliṭam. 9. paṭukkum pāyal, parappal, virippu, mettai. 10. paṭuttut tūṅkutal, tūkkam. 11. paṭukkum aṟai. 12. paṭuttut taṅkum vīṭu. (maṭaippaḷḷi = camaiyal ceyyuma taṉi vīṭu). 13. aracaṉ vīṭākiya araṇmaṉai. 14. teyva vīṭākiya tirukkōvil. kaḻāakkāl paḷḷiyuḷ vaittaṟṟāl (kuṟaḷ. 840); camaṇak kōyil, puttakkōyil. 15. tuṟaviyar taṅkum maṭam. 16. muṉiva rirukkai. 17. aṟaccālai. 18. taṅkum iṭam. 19. iṭam. colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa vāyiṉum (tol. eḻut. 100). 20. kōvilil allatu maṭattil naṭaipeṟum tuvakkak kalviccālai. 
21. paṇimaṉai. taccaṉ viṉaipaṭu paḷḷi (kaḷavaḻi. 15). 22. pala vīṭukaḷ cērnta pērūr, pala kārai vīṭukaḷ cērnta nakar allatu nakaram. 23. ūrp peyarīṟu. e-ṭu: kurāppaḷḷi, tirukkāṭṭup paḷḷi. te. gšÈ (b.), mataṉapalli, paṅkaṉappalli, paḷḷi, haḷḷi, māraṇṭahaḷḷi, paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭattil paḷḷiyeṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya ūrppeyar īṟukaḷellām, putuk (hoca) kaṉṉaṭattil haḷḷi eṉṟu māṟiviṭṭaṉa.
paḷḷi - va. pallī, palli (katācaritcākara) = ciṟu vīṭu, ciṟṟūr.
palli eṉṉum vaṭacol vētattil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālac camaṟkiruttilēyē vaḻaṅkutalum pakaramutal eṭuppoli peṟāmaiyum, vērccol iṉmaiyum, vēṟu poruḷ koḷḷāmaiyum, atu tamiḻt tiricol eṉpatait teḷḷat teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
paḷḷik kuṟippu, paḷḷi koṇṭāṉ, paḷḷi koḷḷutal, paḷḷi maṇṭapam, paḷḷi māṭam, paḷḷiyantulā,paḷḷiyampalam, paḷḷiyayartal, paḷḷiyaṟai, paḷḷiyeḻucci eṉpaṉa paṭukkai allatu tūkkam paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷi paṭuttal, paḷḷipaṭai eṉpaṉa aracaraiyum muṉivaraiyum aṭakkañ ceytal paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ, aṭakkañ ceytal nilaiyākap paṭukkaip paṭuttutal pōṉṟatu.
paḷḷi kampu vaittal eṉpatu, vari celuttātavaṉ vīṭṭiṉ muṉ kampu naṭṭu maṟiyal ceyyum nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakkup paṟṟiya col. paḷḷiyōṭam ciṟu vīṭu pōṉṟa amaippuḷḷa marakkalam.
paḷḷikkaṭṭil, paḷḷikkaṭṭu, paḷḷittēvāram, paḷḷippīṭam, paḷḷivēṭṭai eṉpaṉa, araṇmaṉai allatu aracaṉ toṭarpāṉa vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷiccantam eṉpatu camaṇa puttakakōvil māṉiyamum paḷḷivācal eṉpatu makamatiyar kōvilum paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷik kaṇakkaṉ, paḷḷik kaṇakku, paḷḷikku vaittal, paḷḷikkūṭam, paḷḷikkūṭattāṉ, paḷḷittōḻamai, paḷḷippiḷḷai, araippaḷḷi, uccippaḷḷi eṉpaṉa, tiṇṇaip paḷḷik kūṭam allatu tuvakkak kalviccālai paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
iṉi, vēṟu cila vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum uḷa.
ittakaiya vaḻakkac coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭukkai, paṭai. o. nō : Goth. badi, OE. bed, OS. bedi(d) E.bed. paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi, pāṭai. paṭu - paṭi. paṭital. o. nō : OE, OS. bidan, Goth, beidan, E. bide.
paḷ - pāḷ - pāḷam = 1. parantu taṭṭaiyāṉa kaṭṭi. 2. kaṉatta takaṭu.
paḷḷi - (pāḷi) - pāḻi = 1. makkaḷ tuyiliṭam. perumpāḻi cūḻnta viṭattaravai (tiv. iyaṟ. 1 : 80). 2. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam. (piṅ.). 3. kukai. (tivā.) 4. kōyil. aiyaṉ pāḻiyil āḷaipōrk kurittām aṉṟu (īṭu, 1 : 1 : 3). 5. pācaṟai. (piṅ). 6. muṉivarirukkai. pūtantampāṟ pāṭṭik koṇṭuṇpavar pāḻitoṟum (tēvā. 186 : 5). 7. vatiyum iṭam, iṭam. vāṉavarkōṉ pāḻi (tiv. iyaṟ. 2 : 13). 8. marutanilattūr. (cūṭā.) 9. nakaram. (piṅ.) 10. ciṟukuḷam. (tol. col. 400, urai). 11. iṟaṅku tuṟai. ivar tammait tāṉuṇarntāl ivarkkup pāḻi kiruṣṇāvatāra miṟē (tiv. iyaṟ. tiruvirut. 61). 12. elivaḷai. elippāḻi.
iṉi, pāḷ - pāḷi - pāḻi eṉṟumām.
pāḷ - pāḷai - pāḷaiyam = 1. pācaṟai. 2. pōrkkuc cellum paṭai vaḻiyil taṅkiyirukkum iṭam. 3. paṭai nilaiyāka irukkum ūr.
pāḻi = Gk. polis, city.
E. acropolis (f. Gk. akropolis, akros, topmost, outer-most, polis, city.) citadel or elevated part of a Greek city, esp of Athens.
E. decapolis (f. Gk. deka, ten, polis, city), confederacy of ten cities in the 1st century B.C. in a region in the NE part of ancient Palestine.
E. necropolis (f. Gk. nekros, corpse, dead body, polis, city), cemetery.
E. cosmopolis (f. Gk. kosmos, universe, polis, city), a cosmopolitan city.
E. cosmopolitan, a. Belonging to all parts of the world; n, person free from national limitations.
E. cosmopolite, n. Citizen of the world; a free from national prejudices; Gk. kosmopolites, kosmos, universe, polites, citizen.
E cosmopolitical, a. Belonging to universal polity.
E. metropolis, n. Chief city of a country, capital, metropolitan bishop’s see, centre of activity. [LL. f. Gk. metropolis, meter, mother, polis, city.]
E. metropolitan, a & n. Of a or the metropolis; belonging to, forming part of, mother country as dist. from its colonies; of an ecclesiastical metropolis; metropolitan bishop; bishop having authority over bishops of a province, in the west equivalent to archbishop, in Greek church ranking above archbishop and below patriarch, whence metropolitanate; inhabitant of a metropolis.
[LL. metropolitanus f. Gk. metropolites f. metropolis.]
E. Metropolitan magistrate paid London magistrate.
E. Police. n. Civil administration, department of  government concerned with public order, civil force responsible for enforcing law and maintaining public order. [F. f. med. L. politia, policy].
E. policlinic, n. Clinic in private houses; f. G. polik linik f. Gk. polis, city + clinic.
E. policy, n. Statecraft, course of action adopted by government, party. etc; political sagacity, prudent conduct. [ME f. OF. policie f. L. f. Gk. politeia, citizenship, polity, polites, citizen f polis, city.
E. politarch, n Governor of some Oriental cities. e.g. Thessalonica under Romans. [f. Gk. politarkhes, polites, citizen, arkhes, ruler.]
E. politic, a & n. (Of persons) sagacious, prudent, (of actions etc.) judicious, expedient, Scheming; n. (pl.) Science and art of government, political offairs or life, political principles. [ME. L. Of. politique f. L. f. Gk. politikos, f. politea f. polites f. polis, city].
E. political, a & n Of the State or its government, of public affairs, of politics; f. politic.
E. politician, n. One skilled in politics, statesman, one interested or engaged in politics, esp. as profession, one who makes a trade of politics; f. politic.
E. politicize, ise, v.i. & t. Act the politician; engage in, talk, polities; give political character to; f. politic + ize (sef.)
E. politico -, in comb. as politico - economical, politico - geographical, politico - social, politico - religious etc; f. politic.
E. polity, n. Condition of civil order; form, process, of civil government; orgranized society, state. [f. obs. F. politic or L. politia, policy.]
ūr kāval, pāṭi kāval eṉpaṉa pōṉṟē, police eṉṉum kāval tuṟaip peyarum nakarap peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttalai nōkkuka.



paḷḷikkūṭa viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ
kōḻikkuñcu - 1
pala piḷḷaikaḷ, oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka oruvar iṭuppai iṉ ṉoruvar cērntu kaṭṭikkoṇṭu allatu paṟṟikkoṇṭu, varicaiyāka niṟpar. talaimaiyāṉa piḷḷai mutalil niṟkum. appiḷḷaikku etirē maṟṟoru piḷḷai niṟkum.
talaimaiyāṉa piḷḷai kōḻiyaiyum, piṉṉāl niṟkum piḷḷaikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷaiyum, etirē niṟkum piḷḷai kaḻukaiyum, nikarppar. kaḻuku kaṭaicik kuñcaip piṭikka varum. varicai vaḷaintu kuñcukaḷ tappum.
kaḻuku kōḻiyai nōkki eṉakkoru kuñcu tā; iṉṟēl paṟantu vantu piṭittukkoṇṭu pōy viṭuvēṉ eṉṉum. kōḻi maṟuttu, kaḻuku kuñcaip piṭikkātapaṭi maṟikkum. kaḻuku cuṟṟic cuṟṟi vantu kaṭaicik kuñcaip piṭittuk koṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉamē, piṉpu ēṉaik kuñcukaḷaiyum ovvoṉ ṟāyp piṭittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭum.
kōḻi mika mika varuntum.
kōḻikkuñcu -2
eṭṭup piḷḷaikaḷ cērntukoḷvar. avaruḷ oru piḷḷai kōḻiyaiyum, aintu piḷḷaikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷaiyum, maṟṟoru piḷḷai kaḻukaiyum, maṟṟumoru piḷḷai nariyaiyum, nikarppar.
kōḻi taṉ kuñcukaḷaik kūṇṭiṟkuḷ irukkac colliviṭṭu, iraitēṭa veḷiyē ceṉṟuviṭum. kuñcukaḷ tāy pēccait taṭṭik kūṇṭiṟku veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku oru kuñcai eṭuttuk koṇṭupōkum. ēṉaikkuñcukaḷellām kūṇṭiṟkuḷ ōṭiviṭum. piṉpu caṟṟunēram poṟuttu mīṇṭum veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku mīṇṭum oru kuñcai eṭuttukkoṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉam nālu kuñcu pōṉapiṉ tāy tirumpivarum. eñciyuḷḷa kuñcu naṭantataic collum.
kōḻi kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, eṉ kuñcukaḷaiyellāṅ koṭuttuviṭu. avaṟṟiṟkup patilāka nāṉ uṉakku nāḷaikkuk kaṟitaruvēṉ eṉṟu vākkaḷittu, kuñcukaḷai mīṭṭukkoṇṭu pōkum.
maṟunāḷ, kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟi eṅṅaṉam koṭuppateṉṟu kōḻi kavaṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu, nari ataṉiṭam vantu, nī kavalaippaṭātē. kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟikoṭukka vēṇṭiyatillai. ataṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟku uṉakkoru valakkaram (tantiram) collikkoṭuppēṉ, eṉṟu collum. kōḻi atai nampikkoṇṭu cummā iruntuviṭum. piṉpu nari kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, kōḻi uṉakku vākkaḷittapaṭi kaṟi koṭukkātām. nī pōy ataṉ kuñcukaḷaip piṭittukkoṇṭu vantāltāṉ uṉakkuk kaṟi kiṭaikkum eṉṟu mūṭṭiviṭum. kaḻuku uṭaṉē pōy, ellāk kuñcu kaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟākap piṭittuk koṇṭu vantuviṭum. narikku oru kuñcu paṅku kiṭaikkum.
kōḻi nariyai nampik keṭṭuppōṉatai niṉaittu mikat tuyaruṟum.
kuṟippu :- mēṟkūṟiya viḷaiyāṭṭiraṇṭum pētaip paruvap peṇkaḷuk kuriyavai. aiyāṭṭaip paruvattārāyiṉ āṇpiḷḷaikaḷum cērntu koḷḷalām.  (ta.nā.vi.)
paḷiṅku (t´) 
paḷiṅku - paṭika (ph, t´)
paḷ - paḷa, paḷapaḷa - paḷapaḷappu. paḷiccu. paḷīr eṉpaṉa miṉṉaṟ kuṟippukkaḷ.
paḷ - paḷiṅku = 1. paḷiṅkukkal.
aṭuttatu kāṭṭum paḷiṅkupōl  (kuṟaḷ. 706).
2. kaṇṇāṭi. 3. kaṟpūram.
paḷa - paḷitam = kaṟpūram.
pācilait tiraiyalum paḷitamum paṭaittu  (maṇi. 28 : 243).  (va.va. 203) (ti.ma. 748).
paḷiṅku (2) 
paḷ - paḷi - paḷiṅku = paḷiṅkukkal. kaṇṇāṭi, kaṟpūram. paḷi - paḷitam = kaṟpūram. paḷ - paṭṭu = paḷapaḷappāṉa nūl allatu tuṇi paṭṭup paṭṭeṉṟu nilā aṭikkiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. paḷiṅku = paṭika. (va.).  (ti.ma. : 748)
paṟṟu 
nāṉ eṉṟu taṉṉaip paṟṟiya akappaṟṟum eṉatu eṉṟu taṉ uṭaimai kaḷaip paṟṟiya puṟappaṟṟum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.  (ti.ma : 48)
paṟṟuk kōṭu 
paṟṟuk kōṭu eṉpatu mutalāvatu koṭikaḷ paṟṟippaṭarum koḷ kom piṟkum, iraṇṭāvatu koḷ kompu pōṉṟa tāṅkalukkum peyar. paṟṟu - piṭippu. kōṭu - kompu.  (col. 14).
paṉṉiru poruttam 
1. kātaṟ poruttam. 2. uṭal nalap peruttam, 3. oḻukkap poruttam. 4. karuttup poruttam. 5. uṇṭip poruttam.  6. akavaip poruttam. 7. uruvap poruttam. 8. kalvip poruttam. 9. muṟaip poruttam. 10. viṉait tiṟapporuttam. 11. toḻiṟ poruttam. 12. kuṭumpa nilaip poruttam.  
ta.ti : 43 - 49.
paṉṉīrkkuḷattil muḻukutal viḷaiyāṭṭu
patiṉoru peṇpiḷḷaikaḷ kūṭit teruvil ōriṭattil vaṭṭamāy uṭkārntapiṉ, avaruḷ talaimaiyāṉavaḷ ovvoruttiyiṉ muṭṭik kālkaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟāka varicaippaṭi cuṟṟic cuṟṟic toṭṭukkoṇṭu.
	1	2	3	4
	oruppuṭṭam	tiruppuṭṭam	oṭivā	maṅkaḷam
	5	6	7	8
	cekkait	tiruppic	cevveṇṇey	vārttu
	9	10	11	12
	māṭuṅ	kaṉṟum	varukiṟa	vēḷai
	13	14	15	16
	mañcaḷ	taṇṇīr	teḷikkiṟa	vēḷai
	17	18	19
	kālai	maṭakkaṭi	kāmāṭci
eṉṉum maraput toṭarai, toṭukaikkoṉṟākap pat toṉpatu cīrpaṭac colvāḷ. kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭikiṟa peṇ uṭaṉē oru kālai maṭakki uṭkāra vēṇṭum (atāvatu maṇṭiyiṭṭukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum). iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum ammaraput toṭar collappaṭṭu, ovvoru taṭavai yum kāmāṭci eṉṟa muṭikiṟa peṇ taṉ kālai maṭakkik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iraṇṭān taṭavaiyākak kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭiyum peṇ, taṉ maṟu kālaiyum maṭakkiṉa vuṭaṉ eḻuntu pōyviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ēṉaip patiṉ marum eḻuntu pōy ōriṭattiṟ kūṭṭamāyiruppar.
piṉpu, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ ōriṭattil taṉimaiyāka iruntu koṇṭu, mutalil pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā eṉpāḷ. avaḷ vantavuṭaṉ uṉ kuḻantaiyai eṉṉa ceytāy? eṉṟu kēṭpāḷ. avaḷ, eṉ kuḻantaiyaik kiṇaṟṟil muṟittup pōṭṭuviṭṭuk kuḷattil muḻukiviṭṭu vantēṉ eṉṟu colvāḷ. nī entak kuḷattil muḻukiṉāy? eṉṟu talaimai yāṉavaḷ kēṭpāḷ. ataṟku avaḷ, tayirkkuḷattil muḻuki ṉēṉ eṉṟu patiluraippāḷ. uṭaṉē, talaimaiyāṉavaṉ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām veḷḷaiyāyp pōy viṭṭatu eṉṟu colli, avaḷait turattiviṭuvāḷ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā mūṉṟavātu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam eñciyōruḷ ovvoruttiyaiyum muṟaiyē aḻaittu muṉpōṟ kēṭpāḷ, ovvoru taṭavaiyum muṉ coṉṉavāṟē nikaḻum. kuḷattil muḻukiṉataip paṟṟic collumpōtu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci paruppuk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ  eṉpāḷ. aṉṟu, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām mañcaḷāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turatti viṭuvāḷ. mūṉṟāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci neyk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām eṇṇeyāyp pōy viṭṭatu eṉṟu, collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. nālāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci pavvīk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ  eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō,  eṉ vīṭellām pavvīyāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. iṅṅaṉam oṉpatu peṇkaḷ ēṟkāta ovvoṉṟaic collit turattappaṭṭapiṉ, pattāvatu peṇ maṭṭum nāṉ paṉṉīrk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē talaimaiyāṉavaḷ vā vā vā eṉṟu colli avaiḷac cērttu aṇaittuk koḷvāḷ. atōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
talaimaiyāṉavaḷiṭam ovvoruttiyum ōṭi varum pōtu, ōr iṭakkarāṉa marapurai kūṟik koṇṭu varuvatu vaḻakkam. (ta.nā.vi.)
paṉuval 
parutti nūlukkum aṟivu nūlukkum paṉuval eṉpatu potup peyar.
pañcitaṉ collāy paṉuval iḻaiyākac
ceñcol pulavaṉē cēyiḻaiyā - eñcāta
kaiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
maiyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu
eṉṟum,
urattiṉ vaḷam perukki uḷḷiya tīmaip
purattiṉ vaḷamurukkip pollā - marattiṉ
kaṉakkōṭṭam tīrkkum nūl;  aḵtēpōl māntar
maṉakkōṭṭam tīrkkumnūl māṇpu
eṉṟum uṉṉittuk kūṟiṉār pavaṇantiyār (naṉ. 24,25)
pā eṉpatu neyavup pāviṟkum ceyyuṭpāviṟkum potup peyar.
(āṅkilattilum nūṟporuḷaik kuṟittaṟku necavut toḻiliṉiṉṟu Text, yawn  eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷ eṭuttāḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa (E. Text from L. Texre weave)  nūl maṭiyaik kuṟikkum yawn eṉṉum col ōcunar (Sailors) kūṟum kataikkup potup peyarākum. atai Sailor’s yawn eṉpar.
pākkiyam
paku - pāku - pākku = pakuti, naṟpakuti, naṟpēṟu pākku + iyam (īṟu) = pākkiyam.
pākku veṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē . . . viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pākkuveṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkum viḷaiyāṭṭu, accollaiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu:  itu vaṭakoṅku nāṭṭil paruppuc caṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār tokai :  potuvāka aṟuvarkkuk kuṟaiyāta palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam:  ūrppoṭṭal itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭu muṟai:  talaimaiyāṉa iru petumpaiyār1  aṇṇāviyar pōl etiretir niṉṟu koḷvar. avaruḷ oruttiyiṉ piṉṉāl, ēṉaic ciṟumiyarellārum orutti araiyāṭaiyai iṉṉorutti paṟṟik koṇṭu varicaiyāy niṟpar. iṉṉorutti, avvaricaikku etir niṉṟu maṟukkāṭṭi valamum iṭamum cuṟṟic ceṉṟu, varicaiyāy niṟkum ciṟumiyaruḷ aṇṇāviyoḻinta piṟaruḷ oruttiyai allatu palarait toṭamuyalvāḷ. avaḷ valañ cellumpōtu iṭamum, iṭañ cellum pōtu valamumāka, varicaiyāy niṟkuñ ciṟumiyar vaḷaintu vaḷaintu iyaṅkuvar. toṭappaṭṭa peṇ nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam (aṇṇāviyoḻinta) ellāp peṇkaḷum toṭappaṭumvarai, āṭṭut toṭaralām.
ōr āṭṭai muṭintapiṉ maṟumuṟaiyum muṉ pōṉṟē āṭap peṟum.
āṭṭu nikaḻumpōtu, taṉittu niṟpavaḷum varicai mutalviyumāṉa aṇṇāviyar iruvarum, piṉ varumāṟu pāṭṭuppāṭi neṭukalum uṟaḻnturaippar. pāṭṭu muṭintavuṭaṉ tiruppap paṭum.
pāṇṭināṭṭup pāṭṭu
1.	ta: 	pākkuveṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē.
	va:	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
2.	ta:	veṟṟilaip peṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē.
	va:	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.

	va:	āṭṭup piḻukkaiyait tūrttup pār.
4.	ta:	kuṭṭi kiṭakkiṟa kiṭaiyaip pār.
	va:	kuṭṭip piḻukkaiyait tūrttup pār.
5.	ta:	pallē valikkutē
	va:	nellaik koṟittuk koḷ.
koṅkunāṭṭuppāṭṭu

	va:	tiruppi nakku. 
2.	ta:	vāḻai yilai.
	va:	vaḻittu nakku. 2

	va:	vīṭṭumēlē ēṟuvēṉ

	va:	kalleṭuttup pōṭuvēṉ.

	va:	talaiyaṇai pōṭṭukkoḷ.

pāṭivācal 
callikkaṭṭu māṭṭut toḻuvai iṉṟum pāṭi allatu pāṭivācal eṉṟaḻaippatu, ivvaḻakkam paṇṭaik kālattil āyarpāṭiyil nikaḻntatu eṉpatai uṇarttum.  (ta.ti. 5).
pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻc ciṟappu
kaṭaik kaḻakam kalaintu pala nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭanta piṉpum, pāṇṭiyavaraciṉ talaimaipōyp paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu ceṉṟa piṉpum, pāṇṭiyaṉait tamiḻ nāṭaṉeṉṟu tivākaram ciṟappittaṟkum.
nallampar nalla kuṭiyuṭaittu cittaṉvāḻ (vu)
illan toṟumūṉ ṟeriyuṭaittu - nallaravap
pāṭṭuṭaittu cōmaṉ vaḻivanta pāṇṭiyaniṉ
nāṭṭuṭaittu nalla tamiḻ.
eṉṉum paḻañ ceyyuṭkum ēṟpa, pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻ iṉṉum kīḻkkāṇum palavakaiyiluñ ciṟantuḷḷatu.
(1) pāṇṭi nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, ciṟappāka nāṭṭuppuṟatti luḷḷavar, tamiḻ valliṉa meyyolikaḷait tiraviṭa āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōl mika valittum eṭuttum olikkātu, paḻaiya muṟaippaṭiyē palukki (uccarittu) varukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭa coṟkaḷiluḷḷa vallolikaḷum polivolikaḷum avar vāyil nuḻaivatillai.
e-ṭu : cākṣi - cākki, jāti - cāti
(2) coṟṟūymai pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻc ciṟappukkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
e-ṭu:	cikiccai (va.) - paṇṭuvam (ta.)
		cuttam (va.) - tuppuravu (ta.)
pāṇṭi nāṭṭār caikkiḷ (Cycle)  eṉpatai mitivaṇṭi eṉṟum, nāñcil nāṭṭār puṉal (Funnel) eṉpatai vaittūṟṟi eṉṟum, tūya tamiḻc collāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
(3) nī, nīr eṉṉum muṉṉilaip peyarkaḷiṉ muntiya vaṭivāṉa nīṉ, nīm eṉpavai, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭilēyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāmam eṉṉum tirumāliya (vaiṇava)k kuṟip peyariṉ mūlamāṉa irāmam eṉpatum, aṅkuttāṉ  vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇ kāppu irāmavaṇakkam paṟṟi irāmam eṉṟum, kōpāla vaṇakkam paṟṟik kōpālam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. kōpālam eṉpatu kōppāḷam eṉac citaintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ituvum teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakkē.
(4) tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷapaṭi, paṭiyai nāḻi eṉpatum, araippaṭiyai uri eṉpatum, kāḷaiyaiyum āvaiyum viravup peyarāl cāttaṉ cāttiyeṉṟaḻaippatum, iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakkāma.
(5) pāṇṭi nāṭṭulakavaḻakkil, vēṟeṅkum vaḻaṅkāta tūya teṉcoṟkaḷum, marapukaḷum (Idioms) vaḻakkāṟukaḷum (Usages), iṇaimoḻikaḷum (words inpairs) toṭar moḻikaḷum (Phrases), paḻamoḻikaḷum (Proverbs) ērāḷamāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭaikkaṇṇi, kutiraivāli mutaliya tavacaṅkaḷum karampaip payaṟu, kaṟpayaṟu mutaliya payaṟu vakaikaḷum, aṅkut tāṉ viḷaikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iḷattal, uṇattutal, iḷavaṭṭam, ēttāppu, karaṭṭai, kāṇam, kāyal, kāmputal kiṇṇutal kiḷiyañciṭṭi, kuṭimakaṉ, kuṇṭaṭiyaṉ, kuṇaṭṭutal, kutāvaṭai, kuntakkam, kentaḷippu, cavaṅkal, cavuttal, cillāṉ, cilaiyōṭutal, cīyāṉ, cēṭā, takkaṉai, taṭaiyam, tavval, tavattutal, tāyamaṭṭam, tikaital, tuppuravu, tēri, nalavu collutal, nōṅkutal, paṇṇaiyār, pattanaṭai, pataval, parital (ōṭutal), parumpu, paṟamputal, pāṭu paṭutal (payiriṭutal), putuniṟam, pullai, pūṭṭaṉ, poṇṭāṉ, mayilai, māṉai, mettutal, vaṭali, vatiyaḻital, vaḷḷitāy (muḻutum), vāḻkkaippaṭutal, vāḻvaraci eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ nellai vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟappākum, ātalāl, vaḻakkai ārāytal vēṇṭum, iṟṟait nāṭṭulaka vaḻakkai ārāytal vēṇṭum, iṟṟait talaimait tamiḻp pērāciriyar ceṉṉait tamiḻaiyum cōḻakoṅku nāṭṭut tamiḻaiyumē aṟintatiṉāl, centamiḻaip piṟaḻavuṇarn tuḷḷaṉar.

pāṇṭiyar kuṭi kuma


rikkaṇṭattil ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  (va.va. 203).
pāṇṭiyaṉ urōma nāṭṭut toṭarpu
pāṇṭiyaṉ akattacu (Augustus- ki.mu.44- ki.pi.14) eṉṉum urōma nāṭṭup pēraracaṉukkut tūtu viṭuttatākavum, atu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatākavum, collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ēṟkaṉavē irunāṭukaṭkum iṭaiyē irunta vāṇikat toṭar pōṭu tūtāṇmait toṭarpum ēṟpaṭṭataṉ viḷaivāka, oru ciṟu urōmak kuṭiyēṟṟam maturaiyil amaintatākavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ mey kāvaṟ paṭai urōmap porunaraik koṇ ṭiruntatākavum, terikiṉṟatu. maturai mānakarkkōṭṭai vāyilaiyum, kōvalaṉ kālattil urōmap paṭaiñar kāttu niṉṟatai,
kaṭimatil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta
aṭalvāḷ yavaṉark kayirātu pukkāṅku  (cilap. kaca: cācā-e)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyalām. innilaimai ki.pi.ca- ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai nīṭittatu. ataṉāl, akkālattu urōmak kācukaḷ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻakattiṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
yavaṉar naṉ kalan tanta taṇkamaḻ tēṟal  (puṟam. rucā: kaa)
eṉpatu, yavaṉar pāṇṭiyar vāṇikat toṭarpaik kāṭṭum.
