OF. gingibre, gingimbre, mod. F. gingembre, Pr. gingibre, gingebre, Sp. gengibre, agengibre, Pg. gengivre, It. zenzevero, zenzero, gengero, gengiovo.
Arab. zanjabil, MDu, gengber, D.gember, MHG, ingewer, Ger, ingwer, MLG. engewer, Da. ingefeer, Sw. ingefara. 
kōḻikkōṭṭiliruntu (Calicut) ēṟṟumatiyāṉa paruttit tuṇi āṅkilattiṟ kalikkō (calico) eṉṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu.
pāṇṭi viḷaiyāṭṭu
(1) pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muṟai
āṭṭiṉ peyar: pala kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷḷatāy nilattiṟkīṟappaṭṭa araṅkiṉuḷ vaṭṭeṟintu, atai noṇṭiyaṭittuk kālāl taḷḷiyāṭum āṭṭu, pāṇṭi eṉappaṭum.
ivviḷaiyāṭṭu, ēṉaiyiru tamiḻanāṭukaḷilum, vaṭṭu eṉṟum cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum eṟikaruviyāṟ peyar peṟṟiruppatāl, orukāl pāṇṭi eṉpatum eṟi karuvip peyarāy irukkalām.
pāṇṭil eṉṉuñ col vaṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, ataṉ kaṭaikkuṟaiyāṉa pāṇṭi eṉpatum apporuḷ paṭalām. vaṭṭu cilli (cillākku) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, vaṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷaiyē moḻip poruṭ kāraṇamākak koṇṭiruppatupōl, pāṇṭi eṉpatum koṇṭirukkalām.
pāṇṭi viḷaiyāṭṭiṟku vaṭṭu allatu vaṭṭāṭṭu eṉpatu paḻam peyar. iṉṟum paḻañcēra nāṭākiya malaiyāḷattil appeyarē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṭuvār tokai:  itai iruvar āṭuvar. itu ēṉai vakai kaṭkum okkum.
āṭu karuvi: varicaikku mūṉṟu āka irunaṭṭu varicaiyāka āṟu kaṭṭaṅkoṇṭa oru nīḷcatura araṅkum, āṭakar ovvoru varkkum araiyaṅkulak kaṉamum īraṅkula viṭṭamumuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamāṉa kal allatu ōṭṭāñcalliyum,  itai āṭu karuviyām. araṅku, potuvāka, āṟaṭi nīḷamum nālaṭi akalamum uḷḷa tāyum, mēṟkuṟumpakkattil, camuttiram eṉṉum ōr araivaṭṭaṅ koṇṭatāyum, irukkum. avvarai vaṭṭattiṉ ucciyil ciṟupāṉmai amaikkappaṭum oru ciṟu vaḷaivu, kumpakkuṭam eṉappaṭum. kaṭṭaṅkaṭkut taṭṭukkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. kalliṟku allatu oṭṭāñcallikku vaṭṭu eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam: akaṉṟa teruvum peru muṟṟamum poṭṭalum itai āṭiṭamām. itu ēṉai vakaikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟai: uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉpaṭiyē yār munti yāṭuvateṉṟu tuṇintu koṇṭu iruvaruḷ oruvar āṭat toṭaṅkuvar. tiruvuḷaccīṭṭut tīrppu kācu cuṇṭi allatu vaṭṭeṟintu aṟiyappeṟum.
āṭupavar, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, iṭavaricai mutaṟkaṭṭattil tam vaṭṭaiyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil avvaṭṭai mitittu, ataik kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil atai mitittal vēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa vaṭṭu kaṭṭattiṟkappāl viḻiṉum kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ viḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl viḻiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum veḷiyum orēyeṭṭil vaṭṭai mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum tūkkiya kālai āṭum pōtu kīḻē ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orē yeṭṭil mitikka iyalātavāṟu tolaiviṟ ceṉṟuviṭiṉum, āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉ piṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam māṟi māṟik kaṭaipōka āṭuvar.
mutaṟkaṭṭa āṭṭut tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ, aṭutta iru kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, piṉpu camuttirattilum, ataṉpiṉ valappuṟa mukkaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, muṟaiyē muṉpōl āṭa vēṇṭum. 2ām, 3ām, 5ām, 6ām kaṭṭaṅkaḷil āṭumpōtu, kaṭṭan toṟum mitittu noṇṭiyaṭippatum, oru kaṭṭam iṭaiyiṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭippatumāka, iruvēṟu muṟaiyuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatē perumpāṉmai. vaṭṭaik kālāl taḷḷuvatilum, mitikkak kūṭiya kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamāyt taḷḷuvatum, orēyaṭiyāy veḷiyē taḷḷuvatum, eṉa irumuṟaiyuṇṭu.
eṉṟum iṭavaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavē araṅkiṟkuṭ pukavēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, iṭavaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum camuttiramum muṟaiyē kīḻiruntu mēlākavum, valavaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē mēliruntu kīḻākavum, āṭappeṟum eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
iruvaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, araṅkiṉ muṉ niṉṟu, āyiṉ araṅkup pakkam puṟaṅkāṭṭit talaikkumēlāka, camuttirattil viḻumāṟu vaṭṭaiyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭi yaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittuk kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum mitittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṭaiyil oṉṟum, iṟutiyil oṉṟumāka, irutaṭavai camuttirattil vaṭṭeṟintu āṭappaṭum. mutal taṭavai etirnōkkiyum, iraṇṭām taṭavai puṟaṅkāṭṭiyum, vaṭṭeṟivatē vēṟupāṭām.
yārēṉum oruvar iraṇṭāntaṭavai camuttirattil tavaṟātu āṭiyapiṉ, āṭṭai muṭiyum, veṟṟi peṟṟavar, araṅkiṟku veḷiyē muṉpuṟa mūlaiyoṉṟai yaṭuttu, kutiṅkālai naṭuvāka vūṉṟip peruviral allatu pātattāl oru vaṭṭam varaivar. ataṟku uppu eṉṟu peyar. atai varaital uppu vaittal  eṉappaṭum. ovvoru veṟṟikkum ovvōr uppu muṉṉēr varicaiyāka vaikkappeṟum. munti veṉṟavar araṅkiṉ iṭappuṟa mūlaiyaṭuttum. pinti veṉṟavar ataṉ valappuṟa mūlaiyaṭuttum uppuvaittal marapu. araṅkiṟku muṉṉāl taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu, araṅkiṉ mutaṟkaṭṭattiliruntu orē yeṭṭil matikka muṭiyātavāṟu eṭṭattiliruppavaṉ, āṭakar tām vaitta uppuvaḻiyāka naṭantēṉum noṇṭiyaṭittōṉum ceṉṟu mitikkalām. itu uppu vaittavarukkum ēṟpaṭum vacati. ivvacati uppu mikutikkut takkavāṟu mikum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam :  orukāl, ivviḷaiyāṭṭu, oru noṇṭi vaṇikaṉ palanāṭu kaṭantu arumporuḷ tēṭi vantataik kuṟittatāka irukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ: oṟṟaikkāṟ celavu, kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oru poruḷai eṟital, kālāl oru poruḷaik kuṟitta iṭattiṟkut taḷḷutal, piṉpuṟamākak kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oṉṟai yeṟital mutaliya payiṟcikaḷ ivviḷaiyāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
(2) cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭumuṟai
1. oṟṟaic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pāṇṭināṭṭiṟ pāṇṭiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum viḷaiyāṭṭu, cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. oṟṟaikkaṭṭai araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli oṟṟaic cilli eṉappaṭum.
cillikkup pāṇṭi eṉṉum peyarum cilaviṭattu aruki vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār tokai:  iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi:  nāṉku camakaṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatum, ēṟattāḻa 6 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamum uḷḷatumāṉa, oru nīḷ catura araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilli (cillākku) eṉappaṭum vaṭṭum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. araṅkiṉ mēṟpakutiyāṉa araivaṭṭat tiṟkuc cōḻa nāṭṭil malai eṉṟum koṅku nāṭṭil karakam eṉṟum peyar. cēlam vaṭṭārattiṟ karakaṅ kīṟāmalum āṭap peṟum.
āṭumuṟai: mutalāvatu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu akkaṭṭattait tāṇṭi aṭutta kaṭṭaṅkaḷai ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuc ceṉṟu karakattil allatu kaṭaicik kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē kālūṉṟi, mīṇṭum muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuk kīḻ vantu cilliyai mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, atai ōr eṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam piṟa kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, toṭarntu cilliyeṟintu āṭal vēṇṭum.
mēṟ kaṭṭaṅkaḷil eṟinta cilliyai ovvoru kaṭṭamāyk kīḻē taḷḷik koṇṭu vantu, aṭik kaṭṭattiliruntu veḷiyē taḷḷi orē yeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉūṭu cellumpōtum mīḷum pōtum, noṇṭi yaṭikka vēṇṭiyapōtellām, noṇṭiyaṭittē ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa cilli kaṭṭattiṟkappāl vīḻiṉum, kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vīḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl vīḻiṉum noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷḷum veḷiyum orē yeṭṭil cilliyai mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭikkumpōtu tūkkiya kālaik kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷap paṭṭa cilli mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orē yeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu neṭuntolaiviṟkuc celliṉum āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam, āṭṭai muṭiyumvarai iruvarum māṟimāṟi yāṭuvar.
nālu kaṭṭattilum cilliyeṟintāṭiyapiṉ, akaṅkaiyil oru muṟaiyum puṟaṅkaiyil orumuṟaiyum kuttukkaimēl oru muṟaiyum cilliyai vaittuk koṇṭu, karakamvarai noṇṭi yaṭittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. karakattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām.
ataṉpiṉ, talaimēl orumuṟaiyum valappātattiṉ mēl orumuṟaiyum cilliyai vaittuk koṇṭu, ovvoru kaṭṭattaiyum ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuk karakam varai naṭantu ceṉṟu naṭantu mīḷavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu atai mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu aṅku niṉṟu araṅkiṟku muṉpākac cilliyeṟintu, aṅkiruntu noṇṭiyaṭittu vantu atai mitittal vēṇṭum.
piṉṉar, mīṇṭum orumuṟai karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, kaṇṇai mūṭik koṇṭāvatu mēṉōkkik koṇṭāvatu ovvoru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum ovvōr eṭṭuvaittu naṭantuceṉṟu, cilliyai mitittal vēṇṭum. ovvōr eṭṭuvaikkum pōtum amarēcā  eṉṟu collavēṇṭum. kāl kōṭṭil paṭātu eṭṭu cariyā yiruppiṉ, etiriyār uṭaṉ uṭaṉ rēcu (amarēcu) eṉṟu vaḻi moḻivar;  cariyāyillāviṭiṉ illai eṉpar. ataṉpiṉ etiriyār āṭal vēṇṭum.
amarēcāp pakuti tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ; etiri yārai yāṉaiyā, pūṉaiyā? eṉṟu viṉavi, yāṉai eṉṟu colliṉ nimirntu niṉṟu talaikku mēlākavum, pūṉai eṉṟu colliṉ kuṉintu irukāṟ kavaṭṭūṭum cilliyai oru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ eṟiya vēṇṭum. atu ekkaṭṭattiṟkuḷ viḻukiṟatō akkaṭṭattil, mūlaikkuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷ kīṟalvēṇṭum. atu paḻam eṉap paṭum. paḻam pōṭṭa kaṭṭattiṟku etiriyār kāl vaittal kūṭātu;  eṉṟum atait tāṇṭiyē cella vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, etiriyāriṉ vacatikkāka, ak kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē valap puṟattil allatu iṭappuṟattil, kaṭṭattai yoṭṭi ōr arai vaṭṭam amaittuk koṭukkappaṭum. ataṟku `yāṉaikkāl eṉṟu peyar. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr āṭakarum, tattam paḻak kaṭṭattiṉ pakkamāka etiriyārkku yāṉaik kāl amaittuk koṭuppar.
orumuṟai paḻam pōṭṭavar maṟumuṟai muntiyāṭuvar. maṟumuṟai paḻattiṟkuc cilliyeṟiyumpōtu, atu paḻak kaṭṭattil viḻuntu viṭiṉ tavaṟām.
oruvarēyō iruvarumō ellāk kaṭṭattilum paḻam pōṭṭapiṉ, araṅku kalaikkappaṭṭu mīṇṭum kīṟappaṭum.
II. iraṭṭaic cilli
mutalām vakai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : iraṭṭaik kaṭṭa araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli iraṭṭaic cilliyām.
āṭuvār tokai: iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi:  varicaikku nāṉkāka iruvaricaikku eṭṭuc caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟōṭu malaiyuṅkoṇṭa, ōr araṅkum;  āḷukkoru cilliyum; itai āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟai: itu perumpālum pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi āṭappeṟum.
iraṇṭām vakai
āṭu muṟai: itu ēṟattāḻa oṟṟaic cilli pōṉṟē āṭap peṟum. oṟṟaic cilliyil, ovvoru kaṭṭattilum cilli yeṟintapiṉ, karakam allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷivarai noṇṭi yattuc ceṉṟu mīḷa vēṇṭum. itilō, valappuṟak kaṭṭaṅ kaḷiṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu iṭappuṟak kaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa cilliyai mitittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. valappuṟa uccik kaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām. itaik kuṟittaṟku atil mūlaik kuṟukkukkōṭukaḷ kīṟappaṭṭirukkum.
naṭantu cella vēṇṭiya pakutikaḷil, orē camayattil iru kālaiyum irupuṟak kaṭṭattilum vaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
mūṉṟām vakai
āṭṭiṉ peyar: itu carikaip pāṇṭi, eṉappaṭum.
āṭu muṟai :  ituvum oṟṟaic cilli pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ iruvaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum āṭappeṟum. iṭavaricaiyil cilli yeṟiyumpōtu valavaricai vaḻiyākavum, valavaricaiyiṟ cilli yeṟiyumpōtu iṭavaricai vaḻiyākavum cella vēṇṭum. iṭavaricai yuccikkaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām.
naṭantu cella vēṇṭiya pakutikaḷ mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭām vakaiyaip pōl āṭappeṟum.
III. vāṉūrtic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar:  vāṉūrtipōl araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli vāṉūrtic cilliyām. ivai ērōppiḷēṉ (Aeroplane) cilli eṉa āṅkilac coṟkoṇṭē aḻaippar.
mēṉāṭṭu vāṉūrti tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉarē inta āṭṭu āṭappaṭṭiruppiṉ, aṉṟu itaṟku vēṟoru tamiḻp peyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṭuvār tokai:  iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi: mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭapaṭi ōr araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilliyum, itai āṭukaruviyākum.
āṭumuṟai:  mutalāvatu, mutaṟkaṭṭattil cilliyeṟintu noṇṭi yaṭittu akkaṭṭattait tāṇṭi, mēṟkaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṟṟaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām ovvōr eṭṭuvaittu noṇṭi yaṭittum, iraṭṭaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭattiṟ koṉṟāka orē camayattil irukālum ūṉṟiyum, mēliraṭṭaik kaṭṭam varai ceṉṟu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭuvantu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiluḷḷa cilliyai mitittu muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, atai orē eṭṭil noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittal vēṇṭum.
āṉāl, kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu kutittu mēliraṭṭaik kaṭṭattiṟkuk kālūṉṟumpōtu, malainōkki ūṉṟāmal, ataṟkup puṟaṅkāṭṭik kutittu muṉpuṟam nōkki ūṉṟa vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam eṭṭāṅ kaṭṭamvarai āṭal vēṇṭum. cilli yirukkiṟa kaṭṭam etuvāyiṉum, mēlē cellum pōtu atai mitittal kūṭātu;  kīḻē varumpōtutāṉ atai mitittal vēṇṭum. ākavē kīḻiraṭṭaiyilāyiṉum mēliraṭṭaiyilā yiṉum cilli irukkumpōtu, atiṟ piṟacamayampōl iru kālum ūṉṟa muṭiyātu. cilliyuḷḷa kaṭṭattai viṭṭu viṭṭuc cilliyillāta kaṭṭattait tāṉ oṟṟaik kaṭṭampōl noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittuc ceṉṟu mīḷa vēṇṭum. mēṟcellum pōtum kīḻvarumpōtum, kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇmuṟaippaṭiyē ceṉṟu varavēṇṭum. kaṭṭattiluḷḷa cilliyai mitittu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷumpōtu, kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamākavum taḷḷalām; orēyaṭiyākavun taḷḷalām.
eṭṭuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷum iṅṅaṉam āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, oṟṟaic cilliyiṟpōl, veḷḷaikkaiyum karuppukkaiyum kuttukkai yum talaiyum kālum, muṟaiyē āṭappeṟum. talaiyuṅkālum āṭumpōtu, iraṭṭaik kaṭṭattil orē camayattil iru kālum vaittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, malaikkuc cilliyeṟintu noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷi avvāṟē vantu mitittal vēṇṭum. ippakuti noṇṭi eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum itilum, iraṭṭaik kaṭṭattil irukālum ūṉṟa vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, malaikku mīṇṭum cilli yeṟintu amarēcar āṭic ceṉṟu mitittal vēṇṭum. iraṭṭaik kaṭṭattil orē camayattil irukāl vaittal vēṇṭum.
amarēcā muṭintapiṉ, malaiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu etiriyārai yāṉaiyā, pūṉaiyā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, patilukkēṟpac cilli yeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittuvantu atai mitittu, cilli viḻunta kaṭṭattil oru kālāl niṉṟu koṇṭu, paḻam pōṭṭu viṭṭuc cella vēṇṭumā? vantu paḻam pōṭa vēṇṭumā? eṉṟu etiriyārai viṉavi, muṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ pōṭṭu viṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭittu muṉvaravēṇṭum;  piṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittu araṅkiṟku muṉpuṟamāka vantapiṉ ceṉṟu pōṭa vēṇṭum; pōṭṭapiṉ āṭṭai muṭiyum.
noṇṭiyilum amarēcāvilum tavira, piṟa pakutikaḷil malaikkuc celvatillai.
āṭṭattil tavaṟum vakaiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻum ceyalum, piṟa cillikaṭkuk kūṟiyavaiyē.
ōr āṭṭaiyil veṉṟavar aṭutta āṭṭaiyil muntiyāṭuvar.
IV  vaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar:  vaṭṭamāṉa araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli vaṭṭac cilliyām.
āṭu karuvi:  cakkaravaṭimāṉa ōr araṅkum āḷukkoru cilliyum itai āṭu karuviyām.
cakkarattiṉ kuṟaṭṭil oru kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭṭat tokaiyum, ataṉ āraikaṭkiṭaiyil muṟaiyē attokaikku varicai yoḻuṅkākak kīḻppaṭṭa ciṟutokaikaḷum, kuṟikkappaṭum.
āṭu muṟai :  mutalāvatu kīḻttokaiyuḷḷa kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu atai orēyeṭṭil mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum orēyeṭṭil atai mitittal  vēṇṭum, iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu mēṉmēluyarnta tokai yuḷḷa cuṟṟuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām āṭiyapiṉ, naṭukkaṭṭattil āṭal vēṇṭum. naṭukkaṭṭattuḷ eṟinta cilliyai, atu aṅkirukkumpōtum atai veḷiyē taḷḷiya piṉpum, nērē orēyeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟic cuṟṟuk kaṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyāyc ceṉṟu mitittal kūṭātu.
oruvar tavaṟiyapiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum.
kuṟitta vaṭṭat tokaiyai munti yeṭuttavar (atāvatu ellāk kaṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum tavaṟātu muntiyāṭiyavar), kelittavarāvar, kelippataṟkup paḻam eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru paḻattiṟkum kālāl ovvōr uppu vaikkappaṭum, uppukkaḷ, vaṭṭaiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭṭuk koṇṭu varicaiyāka irukkum. veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa cilli orēyeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu tolaiviliruppiṉ, āṭupavar tām vaitta uppu varicaimēl naṭantu ceṉṟu atai mitikkalām.
V. kālippaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar :  kālippaṭṭam pōl araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli kālippaṭṭac cilliyām. 
kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkaviṭum. paṭṭattaik kālippaṭṭam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattār.
āṭu karuvi:  mūlaikkuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa oru caturamum, atai yoṭṭi paṭukkaiyāṉa oru ciṟu nīḷ caturamum koṇṭa oru perunīḷcatura araṅkum;  āḷukkoru cilliyum; itai āṭu karuviyām. mūlaikkuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa caturam nāṉku kaṭṭamāka amaiyum mēluḷḷa nīḷacaturam aintāṅ kaṭṭamākum.
āṭumuṟai : mutalāvatu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, atai orēyeṭṭil noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum atai muṉpōl mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mēṟ kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum cilli yeṟintu, eṇ muṟaippaṭi kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamāy noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, nēraṭiyākavō kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamākavō veḷiyē taḷḷi, muṉpōṉṟē noṇṭi yaṭittu vantu mitittal vēṇṭum.
malai vēṇṭuvāruḷ cilar, aintāṅ kaṭṭattai malaiyākak koḷvar. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷātār, ataṉ mēṟpuṟa veḷiyai aṅṅaṉam payaṉpaṭuttuvar. malaikkuc cilli yeṟiyum pōtu etir nōkkiyum, malaiyiliruntu cilli yeṟiyumpōtu puṟaṅ kāṭṭit talaikku mēlākavum, eṟivatu vaḻakkam.
paḻamāṉavar uppu vaippar.
iṅṅaṉam eḷiyamuṟaiyil itai āṭuvatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakkamām.
cēlam  vaṭṭārattil, mēṟkūṟiya pakutikaḷōṭu veḷḷaikkai karuppukkai kuttukkai talai kāl amarēcā eṉpavaṟṟaiyum, cērttuk koḷvar.
iṉi, uttiyil niṉṟu cilliyeṟintu, 4-ām allatu 5ām kaṭṭattil viḻiṉ uṭaṉē paḻamāvatum, piṟakaṭṭaṅkaḷil viḻiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, uttivarai noṇṭi yaṭittut taḷḷik koṇṭu pōyp paḻamāvatum;  cēlam vaṭṭārattil maṟṟoru vakaiyāy itai āṭum muṟaiyām.
 VI. kaiccilli
taṉiyāyirukkum ciṟuvaṉ allatu ciṟumi, kīḻē uṭkārntu oṟṟaic cilliyaraṅku ciṟiyatāy varaintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralaik kālpōṟ pāvittuk kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamāy vaittuc ceṉṟu, āṭik koḷḷum āṭṭu kaiccilliyām. itu cēlam vaṭṭārattil āṭap peṟum.
pāṇar
tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭak kulaṅkaḷuḷ pāṇar kulamum oṉṟu. itu mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa teṉpatu tolkāppiyattālaṟiyappaṭum.
pāṇar pāṇait toḻilākak koṇṭavar. pāṇ eṉpatu pāṭṭu, paṇ, pāṇ, pāṭṭu, pā eṉpavai ōriṉac coṟkaḷ. cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil pāṇiyāḻ (1500). pāṇvalai(2040). pāṇuvaṇṭu (2447) eṉṉum toṭar moḻikaḷil pāṇ eṉṉum col pāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷil vantuḷḷatu. cilappatikārattum (pa.349) pāṇ-pāṭṭu eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyuḷḷār.
pāṇaruḷum icaippāṇarum yāḻppāṇarum maṇṭaip pāṇarumeṉap palarām  eṉṟu (tol. puṟam. 36. urai) nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvar. icaik karuvikaḷ: tōṟkaruvi, tuḷaikkaruvi, narappukkaruvi. kañcakkaruvi. miṭaṟṟuk karuvi eṉa aivakaiyāyk kūṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ. miṭaṟu(toṇṭai) eṉpatu iyalpāṉa vāyppāṭṭātaliṉ itaṉai nīkki ēṉaiya nāṉkaiyumē karuviyeṉak kūṟuvar cilar. in nāṉkaṉuḷ kañcam (veṇkalam) tāḷakkaruvi. itu mutaliṟ karuvipaṟṟi veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭataiyum. piṉpu iṉavilakkaṇat tāṟ piṟavaṟṟiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭavaṟṟaiyum kuṟikkum. tāḷak karuviyiṉṟiyum tāḷam kākkappaṭumātalāṉum. oru tāḷak karuviyum taṉittu iṉpam tārāmaiyāṉum atu mukkiyamāṉa taṉṟu. mēṟkūṟiya pirivāruḷ icaippāṇar vāyppāṭakarum, yāḻppāṇar narappukkaruviyiṉarum, maṇṭaip pāṇar tōṟ karuviyiṉarumāvar. tuḷaikkaruvi iyakkum kuḻaṟpāṇar maṇṭaip pāṇaruḷ aṭaṅkuvar. maṇṭai-paṟai. kuḻaloṭu kūṭip paṟaiyaṭippatē peruvaḻakku. iṉi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar pāṇarai mūvakaiyar eṉṉātu palar eṉṟatāl, ataṉuṭ kuḻaṟpāṇarai aṭakkiṉum amaiyum. cilappatikārattil.
kuḻaliṉum yāḻiṉuṅ kuralmuta lēḻum
vaḻuviṉ ṟicaittu vaḻittiṟaṅ kāṭṭum
arumpeṟaṉ marapiṟ perumpā ṇirukkaiyum  (intiraviḻā 35-8)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum.
perum pāṇ- kuḻalar mutalōr  (pa.139) eṉa arumpatavurai kārarum kūṟiyiruppatāl kuḻaṟpāṇarumiruntaṉareṉa aṟika. kuḻalaip paṟaiyiṉṟit taṉittu vācippatiṉmaiyiṉ, kuḻaṟ pāṇar pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭilar.
ākavē, icait toḻil muḻumaiyuṅ koṇṭu ikkālattu mēḷakkārarpōla iruntavar pāṇar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. pāṇcērip paṟkiḷakkumāṟu eṉṉum paṇṭaip paḻamoḻi yum itaṉai vaṟpuṟuttum. tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ icaippulavarākak kūṟappaṭum pāṇapattirarum. tiruñāṉacampantar tēvārap patikaṅkaṭku yāḻ vācitta tirunīṇṭa yāḻppāṇarum. paṉṉīrāḻvāruḷ oruvarum yāḻaṟiñarumāṉa tiruppāṇāḻ vārum pāṇar kulattavarē, pāṇaruḷ, āṭavaṉ (puruṣaṉ) pāṇaṉ eṉṟum, peṇṭu pāṭiṉi, pāṇicci, pāṭṭi, viṟali eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭuvar.
vēḷāḷar kulam vēḷāṇ eṉṟum, camaṇar neṟi camaṇ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭutal pōla, pāṇar kulamum pāṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatuṇṭu. puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaiyil.
kiḷai yāyntu paṇṇiya kēḷviyāḻp pāṇum  (cū.144)
aṅkaṭ kiṇaiyaṉ tuṭiyaṉ viṟalipāṇ  (cū. 16)
pāṇpāṭṭu  (cū.137)
pāṇkaṭa ṉiṟukkum  (puṟam. 203)
eṉa vantiruttal kāṇka.
pāṇarukkuc ceṉṉiyar, vayiriyar, ceyiriyar, mataṅkar, icaikārar, paṇṇavar, paṇṭavar(paṇṭar), ōvar, ampaṇavar mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu, ivaṟṟuḷ paṇṭar, ōvar eṉpaṉa pāṇaruṭ kīḻ makkaḷaik kuṟikkumeṉṟu piṅkala nikaṇṭu kūṟum, mataṅkaṉ, ampaṇavaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyarkaṭku mataṅki, ampaṇatti eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷākum, ampaṇam yāḻ. ampaṇavaṉ yāḻ vācippōṉ.
arumpeṟaṉ marapiṟ perumpā ṇirukkaiyum eṉṟu cilappatikārattum, perumpāṇirukkaiyum eṉṟu maturaik kāñciyiṉum (942). aruṭperum pāṇaṉārai eṉṟu tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar purāṇattum(3). ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭai, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai eṉappattup pāṭṭiṉum vantiruppatu koṇṭu. ciṟupāṇar perumpāṇar eṉappāṇar iruperum piriviṉarō eṉṟu aiyuṟavum iṭamuṇṭu.
pattuppāṭṭu mukavuraiyil,  “nkiy¥gh£L«  (ciṟu pāṇāṟṟup paṭaiyum) ituvum (perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyum) pāṇarāṟṟuppaṭaiyāyiruppiṉum, aṭivaraiyaṟaiyiṟ ciṟitum peritumāyiruttal paṟṟi atu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai yeṉavum itu perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai yeṉavum peyar peṟṟaṉa.  eṉa ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātaiyar avarkaḷ, eḻutiyiruppatu teḷivāṉatēyāyiṉum, perumpāṇ, perumpāṇar eṉṟu nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatāṉum, ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭaiyil,
poṉvārn taṉṉa puriyaṭaṅku narampiṉ
iṉcūraṟ cīṟiyāḻ iṭavayiṉ taḻīi
eṉac curukkamākavum cīṟiyāḻ (ciṟu + yāḻ) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉum, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil paccai, tuḷai, pōrvai, vāy, kavaikkaṭai, tivavu, maruppu, narampu mutaliya uṟuppukaḷaiyuṭaiyatāka virivākap patiṉāṟaṭikaḷiṉum yāḻ kūṟappaṭutalāṉum, ciṟupāṇ perumpāṇ eṉpavai karuvi paṟṟiya kulappirivō eṉṉum aiyam muṟṟum akaṉṟapāṭillai. ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka akarātiyiṉum perumpāṇar pāṇaruḷ oru piriviṉar eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
pāṇar vāyppāṭṭum karuviyumākiya iruvakai icaiyiṉum vallavarā yiruntaṉar. cilappatikārattiluḷḷa pāṭaṟ pāṇar  (anti. 186). kuralvāyp pāṇar (200) eṉṉun toṭarkaḷ vāyp pāṭakaraik kuṟikkum.
karuvikaḷil tōṟkaruvikaḷellām paṟai eṉṉum potup peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum. tolkāppiyattil,
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭ paṟai  (tol.)
eṉṉum karupporuṭ cūttirattuḷḷum.
aṉiccappūk kālkaḷaiyāḷ peytāḷ nucuppiṟku
nalla paṭāa paṟai  (kuṟaḷ. 1115)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiṉum paṟai eṉpatu tōṟkaruvip potup peyarāyuḷḷamai kāṇka. maṇappaṟai, piṇappaṟai, paṟaicāṟṟiṉāṉ mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷil paṟai eṉpatu palvēṟu tōṟkaruvikaḷaik kuṟittatu. paṟaikaḷai aṭippavar paṟaiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ip peyar ikkālattup piṇappaṟai yaṟai pavarai maṭṭuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
paṇṭaik kālattil maṇṭai eṉṉum peyar paṟaikku vaḻaṅkivanta maṟupeyarākum. paṟaikaḷaṭikkum pāṇar maṇṭaip pāṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. maṇṭaiyōṭu pōṉṟu maṇ, maram, pittaḷai mutaliyavaṟṟai ceytu tōṟkaṭṭiya paṟaikaḷai maṇṭai yeṉṟatu. oruvakai uvamaiyāku peyar. maṇṭai yeṉpatu ikkālattil montai yeṉṟu tirintu av vaṭivā yuḷḷa maṭkalayattaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. tavalai eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭi vākiya tapēlā eṉṉum intuttāṉic col oru nīrppāttirat taiyum oru paṟaiyaiyum kuṟittal kāṇka. kōvil mēḷattaik kuṟikkum taval(tavul) eṉṉum peyarum itaṉ tiripupōlum.
palvakaip paṟaikaḷaiyum aṭittuk koṇṭu orē kulamāyirunta maṇṭaippāṇar piṟkālattut toḻil. karuvi, oḻukkam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭṭāl palvēṟu pirivāyp pirintu pōyiṉar.
tuṭiyaṉ pāṇaṉ paṟaiyaṉ kaṭampaṉ eṉ
ṟintāṉ kallatu kuṭiyumillai  (puṟam. 335)
eṉa, māṅkuṭikiḻār toḻiṟ kuṭimakkaḷai nālvakaippaṭuttuk kūṟi yuḷḷaṉar. tuṭi uṭukku.
pāṭṭiṟkuk kūttu tuṇait toḻilātaliṉ, pāṇar kūttum āṭivantaṉar. vayiriyar, ceyiriyar, mataṅkar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, viṟali eṉṉum peṇpāṟpeyarum kūttup paṟṟiyavē. kūttaraik kuṟikkum kaṇṇuḷar. kaṇṇuḷāḷar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum pāṇarkkuriyaṉa. kaṇṇuḷ eṉpatu kaṇṇai uḷḷē vaittāṟ pōl nuṇuki nōkkum nuṇviṉaikkūttu. cilappatikārattil (pa.169) kaṇṇuḷāḷar - mataṅkar, āvār perumpāṇar eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār kūṟiyiruppatai nōkkuka. viṟali eṉpavaḷ  viṟalpaṭa āṭupavaḷ. viṟalāvatu maṉattiṉ iyalpu puṟattē tōṉṟac ceyyum tiṟam. itu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ cattuvam eṉap paṭum. kaṇavaṉ pāṇaṉum maṉaivi viṟaliyumāyiruntu iruvarum icaintu aracariṭam ceṉṟu pāṭiyāṭuvatu peru vaḻakku.
pāṇarkkuc ciṟupāṉmai taiyal toḻilumuṇṭu. cilappati kārattil (intira viḻā. 32) tuṉṉakārar eṉṉum peyarkkup pāṇar eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār arumpatavuraikārar. tuṉṉam-taiyal.

11ām puṟappāṭṭil, pāṭiṉikkuc ciṟanta poṉṉaṇi kalattai yum pāṇaṉukku veḷḷinārāṟ ṟoṭutta poṟṟāmaraip pūvaiyum cēramāṉ pālaipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō aḷitta tākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
126ām puṟappāṭṭil, malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri pakaiva ruṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉatu neṟṟip paṭṭattiṟ poṉṉaik koṇṭu ceyta vāṭāta poṟṟāmaraip pūvaip pāṇaratu talaipoliyac cūṭṭiyatum, 203ām puṟappāṭṭil, cēramāṉ pāmuḷūreṟinta neytalaṅkāṉal iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi pāṇarkkup pakaivar araṇkaḷaip pōr ceytu aḻikku muṉpē koṭuttatum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.

kūttarum pāṇarum porunarum viṟaliyum
āṟṟiṭaik kāṭci uṟaḻat tōṉṟip
peṟṟa peruvaḷam peṟāark kaṟivuṟīic
ceṉṟu payaṉetirac coṉṉa pakkamum  (puṟat.36)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattil pāṇar vaṟumaiyum, avaruḷ oruvar tām paricu peṟṟa vaḷḷaliṭam atu peṟāta piṟarai ēvi āṟṟuppaṭuppatum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. caṅka nūlkaḷilum taṉippāṭalkaḷilum pāṇarāṟṟup paṭaikku utāraṇaṅkaḷ nirampavuḷa.
iṅṅaṉam paṇṭaikkālattē pāṇarkku vaṟumai tōṉṟiya taṟkum. atu piṉpu muṟṟip piṇappaṟai tavirap piṟa vaḻikaḷiṟ pāṇṭoḻil naṭattaviṭātu keṭuttamaikkum kāraṇam āriyavarṇāciramattāl pāṇar tāḻttappaṭṭatum āriyar tamiḻ icaiyaip payiṉṟatumē.
pāṇar tīṇṭātār allatu tāḻntōrākavē aracariṭattum peru makkaḷiṭattum aṇṭamuṭiyātu pōyiṟṟu. ivviḻivu tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, tiruppāṇāḻvār pōṉṟa aṭiyārkaḷaik kūṭa aṭuttamai avarkaḷ carittiraṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. pāṇar vāyppāṭṭaiyum yāḻaiyum ūkku vāriṉṟik kaiviṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl icttamiḻ aḻintatuṭaṉ eñciyuḷḷa orucila icaittamiḻc cūttiraṅkaṭkum kuṟiyīṭu kaṭkum uṇmaip poruḷ kāṇṭal aritāyviṭṭatu. ippōtu pāṇareṉappaṭuvār. māṭu tiṉṉāmaiyum piṇap paṟai yaṭiyāmaiyum paṟṟip paṟaiyariṉum caṟṟu uyarvāyirup piṉum, tam paṇṭait toḻilaiyum perumaiyaiyum iḻantava rāyē uḷḷaṉar.
paṇṭaik kālattil paṭṭattiyāṉaimēl ēṟi aracaruṭaiya viḷamparaṅkaḷaip paṟaiyaṟaintu nakarattārkku aṟivitta vaḷḷuvarum pāṇar allatu paṟaiyarē. cātāraṇap paṟaiyar potu makkaṭkum, vaḷḷuvar aracarkkum paṟaiyaṟaikiṟava rāyiruntaṉar. ituvē vaḷḷuvariṉ ēṟṟattiṟkuk kāraṇam.
iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭiṟ cila ciṟṟūrkaḷil paṟaiyar kōyil mēḷam eṉṉum maṇappaṟai payilvataiyum atai mēl vakuppārillaṅkaḷil iruvakai viṉaikaṭkum vācippataiyum kāṇalām. icaittoḻil naṭatta muṭiyāta pāṇarellām kuṭaimuṭaital, mīṉpiṭittal mutaliya piṟa toḻilkaḷai mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
āriyar (pirāmaṇar) mutaṉ mutalāy vāyppāṭṭum narappuk karuviyum payiṉṟu itupōtu tōṟkaruviyum payilkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum nākacaram eṉṉum tuḷaikkaruviyaiyum, taval pōṉṟa tōṟkaruvikaḷaiyum payilvatillai. avai tāḻntavai eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭutalāṉ.
11ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai pāṇarē tamiḻnāṭṭil icait talaimai vakittamai. nampiyāṇṭār nampiyālum mutalām irācarāca cōḻaṉālum tillaiyam palattiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭa tēvārat tiruppatikaṅkaṭku icai vakukkumāṟu. tirunīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar marapiṉaḷāṉa oru peṇ niyamikkap paṭṭamaiyāl viḷaṅkum.
āriyap pirāmaṇar icai payilak kūṭāteṉṟu ātiyil ōr vilakku iruntatu. maṉutarma cāttiram 4ām attiyāyam 15ām vitiyil pirāmaṇar pāṭṭup pāṭuvatu. kūttāṭuvatu............ ippaṭikkotta cāttiraviruttamāṉa karmat tiṉāl poruḷait tēṭik koḷḷak kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
vētattai ōtātu varippāṭṭaip pāṭi, vēta oḻukkattiṉiṉ ṟum tavaṟiyatāl cila pārppaṉar vilakkappaṭṭu ōr ūrukku veḷiyē pōyk kuṭiyiruntaṉar eṉṉum ceyti, cilappati kārattil,
varinavil koḷkai maṟainūl vaḻukkattup
purinūṉ mārpar uṟaipati  (puṟañcēri. 38- 9)
eṉṉum aṭikaḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉṉamē tamiḻarkku icaittamiḻ iruntatu. icaiyai moḻip pakutiyākkiṉatu tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟu emmoḻiyiṉumillai. āriya vētaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa cāmavētam icaiyōṭu kūṭiyatēṉum, avvicai piṟanāṭukaḷiṟpōl mantirattiṟkuriya aḷavu cāmāṉiyamāṉatēyaṉṟit tamiḻicai pōla virivāy ālāpittup pāṭappaṭuvataṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻicaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyē camaṟkirutattil icainūlkaḷ piṟkālat teḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ki.pi.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē mutaṉ mutalāy vaṭamoḻiyil icainū leḻuntateṉṟu tañcai āpirakām paṇṭitar tamatu karuṇāmirta cākarattiṟ kūṟiyuḷ ḷār. ākavē āriya vētattiṉiṉṟum intiya icai eḻunta teṉpatu aṟiyātār kūṟṟē. vēta voḻukkattiṟku māṟāṉa mēṉāṭṭu aṟuvaimuṟai maruttuvattai eṅṅaṉam āriyar piṟkālattup putitāyk kaṟṟut tērntaṉarō, aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻar icaiyaiyum muṟkālattup putitāyk kaṟṟut tērntaṉar. vaṭanūl kaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭāta pala tōṟkaruvikaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntaṉa. pala uyirkaḷiṉ tōlaiyum cavvaiyum ūṟa vaittuk kiḻittup palvakaip paṟaikaṭkuk kaṭṭuvatu pāṇarkkē ēṟkum. icai valla ōr vakuppār icaikkaruvikaḷ ceypavarāyu miruttal vēṇṭum. icai nūṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta kuṟiyīṭuka ḷellām iṉṟum tamiḻiluḷḷaṉa.
iṉi ikkālap pāṇaraip paṟṟi tarṭaṉ (Thurston) eṉpār tamatu teṉṉāṭṭuk kulamarapukaḷ (Castes and Tribes of Southern India) eṉṉum nūlil tokuttiruppataic curukkik kūṟuvām:
tamiḻap pāṇar mētiriyeṉavum paṭuvar. ivar tirunelvēli, maturaik kōṭṭaṅkaḷil taiyaṟkārarāyuḷḷaṉar. ivar vēḷāḷaraiyum pārppāraiyum purōkitarākak koḷvar. ivar  vīṭṭil ampaṭṭarum vaṇṇārum uṇṇār, āyiṉum. kōyil nuḻaiyum urimai ivarkkuṇṭu.
malaiyāḷap pāṇar mantiravātikaḷum pēyāṭikaḷumāyiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ivar mantira viṉaikaḷ palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭavai. ivaruḷ āṭavar tāḻaṅkuṭai muṭaivar:  peṇṭir maruttuvam pārppar. cilaviṭattu malaiyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam ivarkkuṇṭu. aṟuppukkālattil pāṇac ciṟuvar ciṟumiyar vīṭutōṟum ceṉṟu kuṭaiyuṭaṉ āṭi irappar. payirpaccai mītu atikāra muḷḷa pēykaḷ ivar vayappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. cila caṭaṅkukaḷil pāṇar tuṭiyiyakkuvar. paṟaikkut tōl kaṭṭuvatum ivar toḻilām.
pāṇaṉāṉa kuruppu eṉṉum mēlvakuppampaṭṭaṉ, tīyarukkum, iṟantōr āviyai avar iṟanta aṟaiyiṉiṉṟum oru caṭaṅkāl veḷippaṭuttuvāṉ. ivaṉ ceṟumarkku mēṟpaṭṭa tīṇṭātāṉ:  tāḻaṅkuṭaikku mūṅkil vēlai maṭṭum ceyvāṉ. tāḻai vēyvatu ivaṉ maṉaivi taṉ maṉaiviyillā viṭṭāl ayalvīṭṭup peṇṭiriṭam tāḻai vēyak koṭuppāṉ.
tīyar piṇañ cuṭumpōtu pāṇar 5 nāḷ iravu paṟaiyaṟaintu tīyāvikaḷai viraṭṭuvar.
pāṇarkku makkaḷ tāyamē. peṇṭir pala cakōtararai maṇappa tuṇṭu. teṉ malapāril, pāṇarkkuḷ tirureṅkaṉ, koṭa keṭṭi (kuṭaikaṭṭi), mīṉpiṭi, puḷḷuvaḷ eṉa nāṟ pirivuṇṭu, ivaruḷ puḷḷuvaḷ ēṉaiyarilum tāḻntavaṉ, pāṇar kalappu maṇamuḷḷa pala illaṅkaḷ allatu kiriyaṅ kaḷākap pirikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāḷi, parakuṭṭi, kariṅ kuṭṭi, kuḷikaṉ, kuṭṭiccāttāṉ eṉpaṉa ivar teyvaṅkaḷ, ivar uccavēli eṉṉum vakuppup pēykaḷai ōṭṭuvar.
oru pāṇaṉ taṉ āṭaiyil ōr iḻaiyeṭuttut taṉ maittuṉa ṉiṭam koṭuttu uṉ paricam muṭintatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl taṉ maṉaiviyai muṟṟum taḷḷiyatākum.
malaiyāḷap pāṇaruḷ, aññūṟṟāṉ, munnūṟṟāṉ eṉa iru uṭpirivukaḷuṇṭu, tiruvāṅkūrp pāṇarkkup paṇikkappaṭṭa muṇṭu, ivar tamiḻap pāṇariṉun tāḻntavar. ivar mēṟ kulat tārkku 36 aṭittūram vilakuvar; maṉṉāraiyum vēṭaraiyum 8 aṭit tūrattum pulaiyaraiyum paṟaiyaraiyum 32 aṭit tūrattum tammiṉiṉṟum vilakkuvar. ivar mayir viṉaiyum calavaiyum tāmē ceytu koḷvar; kammāḷariṭat tuṇpar; iḷamai maṇañ ceyvar; iṟantōraip putaippar, ivarkkuc cāvuttīṭṭu 16 nāḷ iyalpāy iṟantavarkku āṭi mātattilum, kolaiyuṇṭu allatu tuṉpanērcciyil iṟantavarkku āvaṇi mātattilum ūṉuṅkaḷḷum paṭaippar. itu veḷḷaṅkuḷi yeṉappaṭum. iṟantavarai niṉaivu kūrap pantal maṭam mutaliyaṉa amaippatuṇṭu.
īḻavar tamatu koṇṭāṭṭaṅkaḷil, pāṇarkku īrāḷ uṇṭi yaḷittut tam muṉṉōrkku avar muṉṉōr ceyta ōr naṉṟiyai niṉaivu kūrvar.
pālakkāṭṭil pāṇar talaivaṉukkuc cuprataṉ eṉṟu peyar. avaṉ iṟantāl aracaṉukku aṟivikkappaṭum. aracaṉ vāḷ, kēṭakam, īṭṭi, tuppākki, veṭimaruntu, veḷḷik kāppu, aṭṭikai eṉṉumivaṟṟai avaṉ makaṉukkāvatu iḻavu koṇṭāṭupavaṉukkāvatu aṉuppuvaṉ. makaṉ aṇikaḷaiyum, vēṟu cilar āyutaṅkaḷaiyum aṇintu koḷvar, piṇattai eṭukkumpōtu oṉṟum, kiṭattumpōtu oṉṟum, erittapiṉ oṉṟumāka 3 veṭi cuṭuvar, maṟunāḷ makaṉ taṉ kaiyāṟ ceyta ōr tāḻaṅkuṭaiyai aracaṉukkaḷippaṉ. aracaṉ avaṉukkuc cuprataṉ eṉṉum paṭṭamaḷippaṉ.
caṅkilikkaruppaṉ, pēcci, ūtara karuppaṉ, kāḷi, cōtala karuppaṉ, cōtala patrakāḷi, yaṭci, kantarvaṉa, aṉumāṉ eṉṟu āvikaḷaip pāṇar vayappaṭuttuvar.
pāṇar matam pēy vaṇakkam, mūkkaṉ, cāttaṉ, kappiri, malaṅ koṟatti (kuṟatti) eṉṉun teyvaṅkaḷaiyum ivar vaṇaṅkuvar, it teyvaṅkaḷukku marattaṭiyil kal naṭṭu, muḻukkāṭṭi, āṭu kōḻiyaṟuttuk kāykaṟi cōṟu paṭaippar. iṟantōraiyum vaṇaṅkuvar; amāvācaiyaṉṟāvatu muḻu nilāviṉ piṉ 11ām nāḷāvatu nōṉpiruppar.
pāṇar ellārum pārppār, nāyar, kammāḷar, īḻavariṭam uṇpar, pāṇarum kaṇiyarum toṭṭuk koṇṭāl oruvarai yoruvar tīṭṭuppaṭuttiyavarāvar; piṉpu kuḷittut tīṭṭaip pōkkuvar, pāṇar īḻavar arukil kuṭiyirukkalām. āṉāl, nāyar taraiyil kuṭiyirukka muṭiyātu. kammāḷar kiṇaṟṟil taṇṇīreṭukkavum, pārppaṉar kōyiliṉ puṟamatilaiyaṇṭa vum, pālakkāṭṭil pārppaṉar teruvaḻic cellavum ivarkku urimaiyillai. 1891ām āṇṭuk kuṭimatippil pāṇar paṟaiyaruḷ oru pirivār eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (centamiḻ celvi mēḻam 1939)
pātam
paḷ- paṭu, paṭutal= tāḻtal, viḻutal, porukaḷattil viḻuntiṟattal, iṟattal, paṭṭavaṉ kuṟi= naṭukal. paṭṭavaṉ kāṇi= kaḷattiṟantavaṉukku viṭap paṭṭa māṉiyam, paṭu= kuḷam, maṭu.
paṭu-paṭi, paṭital= tāḻtal, paṇital, paṇiviṭai ceytal.
paṭi-pati. patital= tāḻtal, iṟaṅkutal, ūṉṟutal, akaḻtal, aḻuntutal, uramākap patital, maṇi patittal, kaṟpāvutal, eḻututal,
pati= patikai, patikkum nāṟṟu, patintirukkum uṟaiviṭam, vīṭu, kōyil, nakar,
pati- pativu. pati- patiyam.
pati-patam= nilattiṟ patiyum pātam, kāl, (eṟipatattāṉiṭaṅ kāṭṭa puṟanā. 4), uṭampil muḻaṅkālaḷavu pōṉṟa kāṟpakuti, patintirukkum iṭam, patavi, pātac cuvaṭṭāl ēṟpaṭum pātai vaḻi.
patam- pātam= nilattiṟ patiyuṅ kālaṭi, pātattaik koṇṭa kāl, (pātak kāppiṉaḷ paintoṭi cilap. 14: 23), viḷakkut taṇṭiṉ paranta aṭippakuti, irukkai tāṅkuṅkāl, kāṟpaṅku, ceyyuḷaṭi. vāṅkarum pāta nāṉkum vakuttavāṉmīki (kamparā. nāṭṭup.1)
pātakkāppu, pātakkuṟaṭu, pātakaṭakam, pātakāṇikkai, pātaccāyai, pātacakkaram, pātacaram, pātacāyalam, pāta tāmarai, pātatūḷi, pātappaṭi, pātapūcai mutaliyaṉa pātat toṭarpāṉa kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ, ivai puṇarcciyil vali mikkum mikātum vaḻaṅkum,
pātam - pātai = pātam paṭuvataṉāl ēṟpaṭum vaḻi.
