pirāmaṇam
vētattiṉpiṉ, vētap poruḷai viḷakkum pirāmaṇam eṉṉum urainaṭai nūlkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pirāmaṇaṉukkuriyatu pirāmaṇam. piramaṉai yaṟintavaṉ pirāmaṇaṉ. piramaṉ (prahmaṉ) eṉṉum col paramaṉ eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripu. mā. vi. a. itaip pruh. (b) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirikkum. pruh eṉpatu peruku eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripu.
āraṇiyakamum upaniṭatamum
pirāmaṇattiṉpiṉ āraṇiyakam (āraṇyaka) eṉṉum upaniṭatat tōṟṟuvāy nūlkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āraṇiyattiliruntu ārāyntatu āraṇiyakam. āraṇiyam kāṭu. āraṇiyam eṉṉum col araṇam eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripu.  va.va.
akkālattil nāṭu ciṟitākavum kāṭu peritākavum iruntati ṉāl, ūraiyaṭuttē kāṭu allatu cōlaiyiruntatu. tamiḻa tiraviṭa muṉivar kāṭṭiluṟaivataik kaṇṭa vētap pirāmaṇar, tāmum avarpōl naṭittē, ūraiyaṭutta allatu taṭivaḻi (Trunk Road) mēluḷḷa cōlaikaḷilum makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum malaiyaṭivāraṅkaḷilum, kuṭumpattuṭaṉ vatintaṉar.
ciṟutalai navvip peruṅkaṇ māppiṇai
anti yantaṇa raruṅkaṭa ṉiṟukku
muttī viḷakkiṟ ṟuñcum
poṟkōṭ ṭimayamum potiyamum pōṉṟē.
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭup pakutiyai (2) nōkkuka.  va.va.
tamiḻ muṉivar muṟṟat tuṟantavar; āṇavam aṟṟavar;
pēypōṟ ṟirintu piṇampōṟ kiṭantiṭṭa piccaiyellām
nāypō larunti naripō luḻaṉṟunaṉ maṅkaiyarait
tāypōṟ karutit tamarpō levarukkun tāḻmaicolic
cēypō lirupparkaṇ ṭīruṇmai ñāṉan terintavarē.
kāṭē tirinteṉṉa kāṟṟē pucitteṉṉa kantaicuṟṟi
oṭē yeṭutteṉṉa uḷḷaṉ pilātava rōṅkuviṇṇōr
nāṭē yiṭaimaru tīcarkku meyyaṉpar nāriyarpāl
vīṭē yiruppiṉa meyññāṉa vīṭṭiṉpa mēvuvarē.
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭṭiṟ kilakkāṉavar.
vētap pirāmaṇa muṉivarō, tammaip piṟappiluyarntava reṉṟum vīṭupēṟum ataṟku vāyilāṉa tuṟavum pirāmaṇarkkē uriyaṉaveṉṟum karuti, āṇavam eṉṉum kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟu, kāma viruppam nīṅkumvarai maṉaiviyoṭu kūṭi vāḻntavar. ātalāl, avar uṇmaiyāṉa tuṟaviyarumallar; tuṟavu mutircciyāl meypporuḷ kaṇṭavarumallar. āraṇiyakattiṟkuppiṉ eḻunta upaniṭatam (upaniṣat) eṉṉum meypporuḷ nūl kaḷellām, ōttukkaḷ eṉṉum tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppē.  (va.va.)
uyarntōrk kuriya vōtti ṉāṉa.
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (977).
upaniṣak eṉṉum vaṭacol upa+ni+sat eṉṟu piriyum. upa = uṭaṉ, aruku. ni = kīḻ. sat = kuntu. ākavē, upaniṣat eṉṉum coṟku arukaṭiyamartal eṉpatu tiraṇṭa poruḷ; āciriyaṉ kīḻiruntu meypporuḷ kaṟṟal eṉpatu virinta poruḷ. āyiṉ, paramavōtiyāl aṟiyāmaiyai aṭakkutal eṉpatu āriyar kūṟum viḷakkap poruḷ (“Setting at the rest ignorance by revealing the knowledge of the Supreme Spirit”).
vēta muṉivar kāṭṭil vatintataṉāl, kaṭavuḷaippaṟṟiya vuṇmaikaḷait tāmē kaṇṭaṟintatupōṟ kāṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. avar muṉintatellām tamiḻaiyum tamiḻaṉaiyum tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭaiyumē. muṉital veṟuttal. muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ.
upaniṭataṅkaḷ 108 eṉpar.  (va.va.)
pirāmaṇaṉ nilat tēvaṉā?
katai nīla nari, tāṉ nīlat toṭṭikkuḷ viḻuntu nīla niṟam peṟṟa taṉālēyē, arimāvum varimāvum karimāvum pōṉṟa vaṉmā vilaṅkukaḷaiyum ēmāṟṟi aṭakki yāṇṭatu pōṉṟē, vētap pirāmaṇaṉum kuḷir nāṭṭu vāḻkkaiyāṟ peṟṟa taṉ veṇṇiṟattait tuṇaikkoṇṭu, tāṉ nilattēvaṉ eṉṟu tamiḻaraiyum tirāviṭaraiyum ēmāṟṟi aṭakkiyāṇṭāṉ. an nīla nariyiṉ ūḷaiyāl ataṉ iṉam aṟiyappaṭṭatu pōṉṟē, pirāmaṇaṉum āriya māntaṉ eṉpatu, mēlaiyāriyattiṟku iṉamāṉa avaṉ moḻiyiṉāl aṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ulaka varalāṟṟaṟivum moḻiyārāycciyumillāta paṇṭaik kālattil, paḻaṅkuṭip pētaimaiyum matap pittamum koṭai maṭamuṅ koṇṭa mūvēntaraiyum palvakaiyil vayappaṭutti, avar vāyilākap potu makkaḷiṭaip pirāmaṇiyattaip pukuttiṉar āriyar. annilaimaikaḷ iṉṟiṉmaiyāl, akkālattu mūṭa nampikkaikaṭkum iṉṟiṭamillai.
kaṭanta mūvarāyiram āṇṭāka, pirāmaṇar kalviyait tam kulat toḻilākak koṇṭiruntaṉāl, kalvik kēṟṟa maṉappāṉmai yum niṉaivāṟṟalum avarkku mika vaḷarntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, iv vaḷarcci kulavāri yaḷavilēyē yaṉṟit taṉip paṭṭavar aḷavil illai. ēṉeṉil, iṟaivaṉ  kalviyai ellāk kulattārkkum potu vurimaiyākki vaittiruppa toṭu, vevvēṟu kulat taṉi makkaṭkē ciṟanta kalvit tiṟamaiyai aḷittirukkiṉṟāṉ. orē kulattār kalviyait tam muṟṟiṟaimaiyākak koḷvatu, iyaṟkaikkum iṟaivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṟkum māṟāṉatē.
tiruvaḷḷuvar pōṉṟa aṟa nūlāciriyarō, kampariṉuñ ciṟanta pāvalarō, pirāmaṇarkkuḷ iruntatillai.
ikkālattum, vayavar (Sir) cantira cēkara veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ pōṉṟē, kōvaik kō. turaiccāmi nāyakkarum (G.D. Naidu) putuppuṉai vāṟṟalar. muṉṉavar aṟiñarkkup payaṉpaṭum karut tiyal ārāycci maṭṭum ceytavar; piṉṉavarō, aṉaivarkkum payaṉpaṭum kāṭciyiyaṟ karuvikaḷaiyum iṟumpūtukaḷaiyum ceytavar. āyiṉum, avaraip pōṟṟavillai.
kuṭantai irāmāṉucam pōṉṟē, ceṅkōṭṭaippar, (Dr) civa caṅkara nārāyaṇap piḷḷaiyum, mēṉāṭṭār pōṟṟu maḷavu kaṇitat tiṟavōr. āyiṉum, pirāmaṇarkkuḷḷa iṉavuṇarcci tamiḻaruk kiṉmaiyāl, avarukku viḷamparam illāmalē pōyiṟṟu.
mukavai māvaṭṭattaic cērnta oru tamiḻar, iru kaṇṇum teriyātavar, ettaṉaip pēreṇkaḷaiyum uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭu nālvakaik kaṇakkuñ ceyyavum, ettuṇaip pēreṇṇiṟkum nāṉmaṭi vēr kāṇavum, vallavarāyiruntār.
palkalaik kaḻaka āḷviṉait tiṟamaiyilum āṅkilap pēc cāṟṟalilum, par. (Dr.) vayavar (Sir) ā. ilakkumaṇacāmi mutaliyāriṉum mikkavar, pirāmaṇarkkuḷ irukkavē muṭiyātu. iṅṅaṉam ēṉait tuṟaikaḷilum ettaṉaiyō pēr uḷḷaṉar. tamiḻariṉ taṉṉalamum poṟāmaiyum iṉa veṟuppum, palar talai yeṭukka vāyppillāmaṟ ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pirāmaṇar kalvit tiṟamaiyiṟ ciṟattaṟku iṉṉoru karaṇiyamum uṇṭu. atu maṟaivāṉa iṉak kalappu.
makkaḷuḷ nāl vēṟu varaṇam pōṉṟē nālvakaik kalappuk kulamum uḷḷaṉaveṉṟu, maṉuviṉ kulavoḻukka nūl kūṟu kiṉṟatu. mēl varaṇa āṭavaṉukkum kīḻ varaṇap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ aṉu lōmaṉ. kīḻ varaṇa āṭavaṉukkum mēl varaṇap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ piratilōmaṉ. aṉulōma āṭavaṉukkum pirati lōmap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ antarāḷaṉ. pirati lōma āṭavaṉukkum aṉu lōmap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ virāttiyaṉ.
mēl mūvaraṇattārum tat tamakkuk kīḻppaṭṭa varaṇaṅkaḷilum allatu varaṇattilum peṇ koḷḷalāmeṉṟu, maṉu nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, kīḻ mūvaraṇattārum tattamakku mēṟpaṭṭa varaṇattiṟ peṇ koḷḷalā meṉṟu neṟiyīṭṭu muṟaiyāyk kūṟavillai. āyiṉum, av vaḻakkam toṉṟu toṭṭu iruntē vantatu. itai maṉunūlun kūṟukiṉṟatu.
pirāmaṇaṉukku kṣattiriya vaiciyatirīkaḷiṭattiṟ piṟantavar kaḷ, eppaṭi upanayana mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kuriyavarkaḷāyiruppatāl uyarn tirukkiṟārkaḷō, appaṭiyē vaiciyaṉukku kṣattiriya tirīyiṭattilum kṣattiriyaṉukkup pirāmaṇa tirīyiṭattilum piṟanta puttirarkaḷ, cūttiraṉukku vaiciya kṣattiriya pirāmaṇa jāti tirī yiṭattiṟ piṟantavarkaḷaik kāṭṭilum uyarnta varkaḷ.  (kE.(bkhÊ peyarppu) 10:28)
mūvēntarum pirāmaṇarkku muḻu aṭimaiyarāyp pōṉataṟku, pirāmaṇap peṇ nukarcciyum karaṇiyamā yiruntirukkalām.
paṇṭai muṟaippaṭi tamiḻilēyē tiruk kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceytu vanta kurukkaḷ eṉṉum tamiḻa vakuppār, āriya mantiraṅ kaḷaik kaṟṟuk koṇṭu camaṟkirutattil vaḻipāṭu naṭatti in nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ pirāmaṇarāka māṟi viṭṭatākavum collap paṭukiṉṟatu.
pirāmaṇar eṉṟum tam mēmpāṭṭai nilai niṟutti iṉpuṟṟu vāḻa virumpiyatāl, tam miṉa vuyarttattaip pēṇuvatiṟ kaṇṇuṅ karuttu māyiruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iṟaiyaṭiyā reṉṟu ciṟappittuc collap paṭupavar, ceyaṟ kariya ceyal ceyta teyvap paṟṟāḷarē.
vāḷāṉ makavarit tūṭṭaval lēṉallaṉ mātucoṉṉa
cūḷā liḷamai tuṟakkaval lēṉallaṉ toṇṭuceytu
nāḷāṟiṟ kaṇṇiṭan tappaval lēṉallaṉ nāṉiṉicceṉ
ṟaḷāva teppaṭi yōtiruk kāḷatti yappaṉukkē.  (tiruttillai, 3).
eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattār maṉam varuntip pāṭiyuḷḷār.
tirut toṇṭat tokai pāṭiya cuntara mūrtti nāyaṉār, tillaivā ḻantaṇartam aṭiyārkkum aṭiyēṉ eṉṟu toṭaṅki yirukkavē vēṇṭiya tillai. tillai vāḻantaṇar pirāmaṇarē yaṉṟit tamiḻ muṟaippaṭi antaṇaru millar; avaruḷ oruvarēṉum ceyaṟ kariya pattic ceyal ceytatu millai. mūvēntarum, ciṟappākac cōḻa vēntar, viṭṭa māṉi yaṅkaḷaiyum iṭṭa kāṇikkaikaḷaiyuṅ koṇṭu, orē kōyilil mūvāyiravar iruntu uṇṭu koḻuttuc cōmpēṟit taṉamāy vāḻntu vantavar. poṉṉampalap pūcakaraip pōṟṟuva teṉiṉ, veḷḷiyampalam ceppampalam mutaliya ēṉai yampalap pūcakaraiyum pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum. atu poruntākkāl, poṉṉampalap pūcakaraip pōṟṟutalum poruntātu.
kōvil vaḻipāṭu ataṟkut takutiyuṭaiya ellāk kulattārum ceyyalām, ceyvikkalām, tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻarē tamiḻil maṭṭum ceyvittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamē āriyar varumuṉ naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu.
nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu vanta āriya aṭimait taṉattiṉāl, aṭimait taṉattilēyē piṟantu aṭimaittaṉattilēyē vaḷarntu, aṭimait taṉamē elumpuṅkurutiyumāka ūṟippōṉa tamiḻarē palar tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭai etirkkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭaiyum tamiḻar pūcaka rāvataiyum pirāmaṇar vaṉmaiyāka etirkkiṉṟaṉar. civakōcariyāriṉ camaṟkiruta vaḻi pāṭṭiṉum, kaṇṇappaṉāriṉ tamiḻ vaḻipāṭē civaperu māṉukkuc ciṟantatum ukantatumāyiruntataiyaṟintum, iru cārārum uṇarkiṉṟilar. ēṉai nāṭukaḷiṉ naṭappaiyum ikkāla urimai vēṭkaiyaiyum nōkkukiṉṟilar.
āriya muṟaippaṭi nōkkiṉum, pirāmaṇar iṉṟu ūrkāval tuṟaiyilum paṭait tuṟaiyilum āḷviṉait tuṟaiyilum cērntu cattiriyarāyum, uḻavut toḻil ceytum uṇṭic cālai vaittum vāṇikam mēṟkoṇṭum vaiciyarāyum kait toḻilum ēvalum ceytu cūttirarāyum, māṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
muṭipurai
etu eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, pirāmaṇaṉ nilat tēvaṉallaṉ eṉpatum; ulakiyalilum mataviyalilum illaṟattilum tuṟavaṟattilum, tamiḻaṉukkillāta ēṟṟam avaṉukku immiyum illai yeṉpatum, itai uṇarātavaṉ ettuṇaik kaṟṟavaṉēṉum uruvattāl māntaṉum uḷḷattāl aḵṟiṇaiyumāvaṉeṉ patum; teritaru tēṟṟamām. āriyar veṇṇiṟam iṉṟu poṉṉiṟamum cenniṟamum pukarniṟamum karuniṟamumāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻar moḻiyārāycci yum varalāṟṟārāycciyum ceytu uṇmai yaṟintu kaṇ viḻittuk koṇṭaṉar.
pirāmaṇaṉukku uriyatu evvaṟam?
aṉpuṭaimai, viruntōmpal, iṉiyavai kūṟal, ceynnaṉṟi yaṟital, naṭuvu nilaimai, aṭakka muṭaimai, oḻukka muṭaimai, piṟaṉil viḻaiyāmai, poṟai yuṭaimai, aḻukkāṟāmai, veḵkāmai, puṟaṅ kūṟāmai, payaṉila collāmai, tīviṉai yaccam, oppura vaṟital, īkai eṉpaṉa ilvāḻkkai yaṟaṅkaḷākat tirukkuṟaḷ maṟaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmai yāṉatu viruntōmpal.
iruntōmpi yilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi
vēḷāṇmai ceytaṟ poruṭṭu.  (kuṟaḷ. 81).
viruntōmpum iyalpum īkaiyum pirāmaṇaṉukkiṉmaiyāl, illaṟam avaṉukkuriya taṉṟu.
pirāmaṇaṉ toḻilākac collappaṭum āṟaṉuḷ, ītal eṉpatu ōtuvittalil aṭaṅkutalāl, uṇmaiyil avaṉ toḻil aintē.
aruḷuṭaimai, pulāṉ maṟuttal, tavam, kaḷḷāmai, vāymai, vekuḷāmai, iṉṉā ceyyāmai, kollāmai, tuṟavu, meyyuṇartal, avā vaṟuttal eṉpaṉa tuṟavu neṟiyaṟaṅkaḷākat tirukkuṟaḷ maṟaiyiṟ collap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kolai vēḷvi ceytalālum, tuṟavu nilaiyilum akam piramam eṉṟum civōkam eṉṟum akaṅkarippatālum, tamiḻa matattai āriya mata meṉṟum taṉṉai nilat tēvaṉ eṉṟum taṉ ilakkiya moḻiyait tēva moḻi yeṉṟum colli intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷai ēmāṟṟuvatālum, pirāmaṇa ṉukkut tuṟavaṟamum uriyatākātu.
ākavē, pirāmaṇaṉukku nāṭum pēccu moḻiyum pōṉṟē aṟa vāḻkkaiyum illaiyām.
piḻā
piḻā - piṭa (t´))
piḻā - piḻavu - piṭavu - piṭaku - piṭakku = pūttaṭṭup pōṉṟa putta maṟainūl. piṭakkē yurai ceyvār  (tēvā. 245 : 10.)
piṭaku - piṭakam = 1. taṭṭukkūṭai.
2. puttamaṟai (tiripiṭakam). periyōṉ piṭaka neṟi  
(maṇi. 26 : 66)  (va.va : 209)
pīḻi, pīḻai. piḷḷutal = piṭuṅkutal. tuṉpañ ceytal, tollai koṭuttal. piyttup piṭuṅkutal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. piḷ - pīḷ - pīḻ - pīḻi - pīḻittal = varuttutal, tuṉpañ ceytal. pīḻi - pīṭi - pīṭ (va.) pīḻi - pīḻai - pīṭai - pīṭā (va.) pīṭittal - tuṉpañ ceytal. pīṭai tuṉpam.   (ti.ma : 748.)
piḷḷaiyār vaṇakkam
piḷḷaiyār vaṇakkam kaṭaic caṅka kālattiṟkup piṉṉar tāṉ tamiḻakat tiṟ pukuntatu. iṟaivaṉ ōṅkāra vaṭiviṉaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭatiṉālum, ōṅkārattiṉ varivaṭivam yāṉai vaṭivai ottiruppatālum, yāṉai vaṭivil oru teyvam āriyap pūcāriyāla putitākap paṭaikkappaṭṭu, civaneṟiyai muṉṉiṉum mikutiyāy āriyappaṭuttavum cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭṭaiyum avaṉukkut tantaiyākac collap paṭṭa civaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭṭaiyum kuṟaikkavum, tamiḻarukkuḷ maṟṟumoru matap pirivaiyuṇṭu paṇṇavum, pukuttappaṭṭatāka aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. civaṉ allatu tirumāl eṉṉum peyarāl ellām valla iṟaivaṉ ākiya muḻu mutaṟ kaṭavuḷē vaṇaṅkappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉai vaṇaṅku vārkku, avaṉukku makaṉ allatu marukaṉ muṟaippaṭṭa vēṟoru ciṟuteyvam vēṇṭiyatēyillai. mēlum, iṟaivaṉ paṭaippil talaiciṟanta māntaṉ vaṭivil iṟaivaṉai vaṇaṅku vatē pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaṭku iḻukkāyirukka, ōr aḵṟiṇai yuyiriyiṉ vaṭivil etteyvattaiyum vaṇaṅkuvatu, iv irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu uyartiṇaiyāḷaṉukku eḷḷaḷavum poruntāteṉpatu collāmalē peṟappaṭum.  (ta.ti. 7677).
piṟappu 
piṟappu iṟappu eṉṉum coṟkaḷ muṟaiyē pakarameyc cērkkaiyum ataṉ nīkkamum peṟṟiruttalāl uyir uṭampoṭu kūṭutalaiyum ataiviṭṭu nīṅkutalaiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar.
piṟappu eṉpatu piḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟum iṟappu eṉpatu iṟu eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟum piṟantiruppatālum piṇaṅku iṇaṅku eṉap piṟa etirc coṟkaḷum piṟappu iṟappu eṉpavai pōṉṟē amaintiruppatālum atupōlik kūṟṟeṉka.  (col. 33,34).
pukaḻvakai
oḷi - taṉmatippu
	peyar allatu pēr - nalla vaḻiyilum tīyavaḻiyilum paravum pēr.
icai - pulavar pāṭuvatāl uṇṭākum pukaḻ.
pukaḻ - iṟanta piṉṉum vaḻaṅkum uyartturai.
cīrtti allatu kīrtti - miku pukaḻ.  (col. 42).
pukā (uṇā)
uṇṇutal eṉpatu uṭkoḷḷutal; atāvatu, oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟait taṉṉuḷ iḻuttal allatu cērttal. vāyvaḻi vayiṟṟiṟkuḷ oṉṟai iṭutalum, nilam nīrai uṟiñcutalum, pātam cempañcuk kuḻampu niṟattaip peṟutalum, uṇṇutalē. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalatē makkaḷ pēcciṟ peru vaḻakkāṉatu. iṟuti yatu, cērtaliṉ allatu cērttaliṉ vakaikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
uṇṇutal (peruvaḻakku) = I (mutaṟporuḷ) : uṇavai uṭkoḷḷutal. II (vaḻipporuḷ) : 1. nukartal (aṉupavittal). taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa nī yuṇṭaveṉ ṉalaṉē (kuṟun. 236). 2. kavartal. avuṇaṉā ruyiraiyuṇṭa kūṟṟiṉai (tiv. tiruk kuṟun. 2). 3. icaivātal. ōcai yūṭṭiṉu muṇṇāta vāṟum (yāp. vi.pak. 97). 4. oru viṉaiyiṉ payaṉaiyaṭaital. kōvalaṉ poṟ kollaṉāṟ kolaiyuṇṭāṉ (kolluṇṭāṉ).
uṇṇutal eṉpatu aṭippaṭaip poruḷil uṭkoḷḷuta lātaliṉ, perumpālum uḷḷiṭattai allatu uṭpukuttalaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē uṇṇutal viṉaiccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu : uḷ - uṇ - uṇā - uṇa - uṇavu.
pukuttal = 1. uṭcella viṭutal. vāyil pukuppiṉum (tol. po. 149). 2. uṭceluttutal. 3. uṇṇutal. ipporuḷil ic col vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
puku - pukavu = 1. uṭcelkai. kaḻutu pukavayara (aiṅkuṟu. 314). 
2. uṇavu. paḻañcōṟṟup pukavarutti (puṟanā. 395).
puku - pukā = uṇavu. pukāak kālai (tol. po. 107).
pukā - puvā - puvvā = cōṟu, uṇavu (nāṭṭuppuṟa vaḻakku).
puka + aṭṭu (oṭṭu) = pukaṭṭu. pukaṭṭutal = 1. uṭceluttutal. 
2. ūṭṭutal. tāy kuḻavikkup pālūṭṭiṉāḷ. 3. aṟivuṟuttutal. allama tēvaṉ caritat tīmpāl ..... pukaṭṭiṉāṉāl (pirapuliṅ.tuti. 11), cevitiṟantu pukaṭṭa (tiruviḷai. viṭaiyilac. 4).
oṭṭu eṉṉum tuṇai viṉaiyai, tūṅkavoṭṭār, varavoṭṭār eṉṉum etirmaṟai viṉaikaḷiṟ kāṇka. oṭṭutal = poruntutal, icaital. oṭṭu - aṭṭu. e - ṭu : varaṭṭu (vara + aṭṭu), pōkaṭṭu (pōka aṭṭu). ivai orumai yēval. varaṭṭum, pōkaṭṭum eṉpaṉa paṉmai yēval.
pukaṭṭu - pōṭṭu (u. va.). kuḻantaikku maruntu pōṭṭu.
puka + viṭu = pukaṭu. o. nō : pōka viṭu - pōkaṭu - pōṭu. pukaṭutal = vīciyeṟital. itaṟku ūṭṭutaṟ poruḷillai.
puku - Gk. paku (phag) phagin, to eat, devour; phagema, food; phagas, phagon, glutton.
phage (phag), combining form meaning ‘eater’ as in xylophage.
phagedena, gangrene, L. phagedaena fr. Gk. phagedaina, cancer, lit. ‘voracity’ fr. phagein, to eat.
phago, combining form meaning ‘eating’ as in phagocyte.
phagous, combining form meaning ‘eating, feeding on’ as in creophagous. xylophagous Gk. phagos, ‘eater of’.
phagus, combining form meaning ‘eating’, as in sarco-phagas, L. fr. Gk. sarkophagos, orig. flesh-consuming (stone).
phagy, combining form meaning ‘eating of ’ (something specified), as in anthropophagy, geophagy.
phagia, same as ‘phagy’.	
ukaramutal akaramutalākat tirivatu perumpāṉmai. tamiḻil mūccoli (aspirate) illai. tamiḻ meykaḷ piṟa moḻikaḷil mūccoli meyyākat tirivatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu : kal - khal (pirākuvī).
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻccol tamiḻukku akappuṟamāṉa tiraviṭatti lēyē mūccoli peṟumpōtu. ataṟkup puṟamāṉa āriyattil ataip peṟuvatu iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉataṉṟu.
ph eṉṉum iṇaivari allatu kūṭṭeḻuttu, āṅkilattil ‘f’ eṉṟolippiṉum, kirēkkattil mūccoli kūṭiya pakaramē (pha) eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
puku eṉṉum tamiḻccol, kirēkkattil paku allatu paka eṉṟu tirintapiṉ, ataṟkiṉamāṉa intiya āriya muṉṉai moḻiyil (Pre - Indian East Aryan) puj (bhuj) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. 
o. nō : paku - va. paj. (bhaj).
camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiṉa vēta moḻiyilēyē it tiripu kāṇappaṭuvatāl, itu vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ puku muṉṉarē nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum. miṭaṟṟoli aṇṇa voliyākat tiriyumpōtu, eṭuppilā voli (voiceless sound) eṭuppilā voliyākavum eṭuppoli (voice sound) eṭuppoliyākavum tirivatē potuviyalpu.
e - ṭu :	ka - ca : kuṭikai - kuṭicai.
		g-j : L. ago - Skt. aj (to drive).
im muṟaippaṭi, puku eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa paku (phag) eṉṉum mēlaiyāriyak kirēkkac col kīḻaiyāriyattil puj (bhuj) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. eṭuppilāvoli eṭuppoliyākat tirivatum iyalpē.
e - ṭu :	E. acre - L. ager, Gk. agros.
		E. triple - E. treble.
bhuj, to eat, eat and drink, enjoy a meal, consume, enjoy, use, possess, make use of, utilize, exploit, suffer, experience, undergo, RV., AV., Up., MBh., KaÍv.
bhukta, enjoyed, eaten, made use of, possessed etc.
bhukti, eating, enjoyment, consuming, frution, possession, usufruct.
bhuktvaÍ having enjoyed or eaten or possessed.
bhuji, the granting of enjoyment, favour, RV.
bhujishya, granting food, useful, AV.
bhoktru, one who enjoys or eats, Maitr Up.
bhoÍga, enjoyment, eating, feeding on, RV.
bhoÍgin, enjoying, eating, having, wealthy; a king, a concubine.
bhoÍgya, to be enjoyed, to be used.
bhoÍja, bestowing enjoyment, bountiful, liberal, RV leading a life of enjoyment, BhP., a king.
bhoÍjaka, cating, being about to eat; giving to eat, nourishing.
bhoÍjana, feeding, giving to eat; the act of enjoying, using, RV., the act of eating; a meal, food.
bhoÍjaniya, to be eaten, eatable; to be fed, to be made to eat, Mn.; one to whom enjoyment is to be afforded or service to be done, Nir.; food (esp. what is not masticated, as opp. to khaÍdaniya) MBh.
bhoÍjin, enjoing, eating.
bhoÍjya, to be enjoyed or eaten, what is enjoyed or eaten, anything to be enjoyed or eaten, a festive dinner.
bhoÍktavya, to be enjoyed or eaten, to be used or employed; to be possessed or governed or ruled, to be utilized or exploited, to be fed.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutaliyait tokuttavarkaḷ, puku (uṇ) eṉṉum teṉcoṟkum puj (bhuj) eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpai aṟintē, pukavu, pukā eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷaiyum puj eṉṉum vaṭacolloṭu oppu nōkkak kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ teṉcollē vaṭacolliṉ mūlam eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatu avar karuttaṉṟu. vaṭamoḻi tēva moḻi eṉṉum aṭāvaṭittaṉa ēmāṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉcol lellām vaṭa colleṉṟu collāmaṟ colvatē, av voppunōkkak kuṟippiṉ nōkkam. tamiḻai vaṭamoḻit tiripāka allatu kiḷaiyākak kāṭṭavēṇṭumeṉpatē ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutalit tokuppāḷariṉ aṭippaṭaikkoḷkai yātalāl, vaṭamoḻikku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷai yellām vaṭacol leṉṟu kāṭṭuvatoṭu, uṇmaiyil toṭarpiṉṟi oru maruṅku oliyotta pōliyoppumaic coṟkaḷaiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu tirippatilum avaṟṟoṭu oppunōkku vatilum, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. e - ṭu : āriyaṉ - aiyaṉ, cyāmā - cāmai. vēntaṉ - o.nō : tēvēntiraṉ, maruntu - o.nō : amruta.
itaṉāl, teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻikku mūlamāyirukkalām eṉṉum ōr accam avar uḷḷattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭiruntateṉa uyttuṇarak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
puku eṉṉum teṉcol puj (bhuj) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku mūlamāyiruppatoṭu, tōṟṟamum vaḻimuṟait tiripuṅ kūṟum teḷivāṉa taṉvaralāṟuṅ koṇṭuḷḷatu. ivviyalpu ataṉ tirivāṉa āriyaccoṟkaṭkillai.
ul - uḷ - uḷu = marattait tuḷaikkum puḻu. uḷuttal = marattaip puḻu arittut tuḷaittal.
ul - pul = uṭṭuḷai, uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa nilaittiṇai, tāḷil uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa payirvakai, mūṅkil, puṟakkāḻuḷḷa teṉṉai paṉai mutaliyaṉa.
pul - puḷ - (puḷu) - puḻu = kāykaṟikaṉikaḷait tuḷaikkum uḷu.
puḻuttal = puḻu uṭpukutal allatu arittut tuḷaittal.
puḻu - puku. o. nō : toḻu - toku, maḻa - maka, kuḻai - kukai.
pukutal = uṭcellutal. eli vaḷaikkuṭ cellutalum makkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkuṭ pukutalum viṉaivakaiyil oṉṟē.
pukuttal = uṭceluttutal, uṇavai vāyvaḻi vayiṟṟiṟkuṭ celuttutal, uṇṇutal.
puku (uṇ) eṉṉum colliṉ kakaram, kirēkkattil ‘g’ eṉa eṭuppoliyāy māṟiṉum, miṭaṟṟoliyākavē iruttalai yum, ēṉaiyāriya moḻikaḷil ‘j’ (ja) eṉṉum aṇṇavoli yākat tiritalaiyum, nōkkutal vēṇṭum. itaṉāl, kirēkkac colliṉ muṉmaiyum vētaccolliṉ piṉmaiyum aṟiyappaṭum.
potuvāka, mēlaiyāriyattiluḷḷa eṭuppolik kakaram (g) kīḻaiyāriyat til aikaramākat tirikiṉṟatu.

puku - va. puj3 (bh) - i. vē.
pukutal = uṭcellutal, vāykkuṭ cellutal.

pukāak kālaip pukketirp paṭṭuḻi  (tol. 1053).

pukā - puvā - puvvā (koccai).
puku - puj. o. nō : paku - paj.

pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai yuruṭṭal viḷaiyāṭṭu (1)
itu oruvāṟu kaṇṇāmpotti pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ itaṟku oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum; atōṭu muntit toṭuvatu yār eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṟku, ellāraiyum varicaiyāyk kuṉiya vaittu, avarkaḷ niḻalil pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyai oruvar uruṭṭuvar. atu yār niḻaliṟpōy niṟkiṉṟatō, avar ēṉaiyōrait toṭutal vēṇṭum.
piṟarellām oṭi oḷintukoḷḷutaṟku iraṇṭoru nimaiyaṅ koṭukkappaṭum. toṭum piḷḷai toṭavarumpōtu, ellārum ōṭippōy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟukoḷvar. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ ceṉṟapiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ cellumuṉ toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷai aṭuttamuṟai toṭutal vēṇṭum. oruvarum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu toṭamuyaṉṟa piḷḷaiyē mīṇṭum toṭutal vēṇṭum.
oruvar toṭarntu mūvāṭṭai oruvaraiyuṅ toṭāviṭiṉ, avarmītu ēṉaiyōr ciṟitu ciṟitu kutiraiyēṟuvatuṇṭu. ataṉpiṉ, mīṇṭum pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai uruṭṭappaṭum.
pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyeṭuttal viḷaiyāṭṭu (2)
ituvum oḷintu viḷaiyāṭum viḷaiyāṭṭē.
āṭuvārellām uttikaṭṭi irukaṭciyākap pirintu koṇṭapiṉ, oru kaṭciyār oṭi oḷintukoḷvar. iṉṉoru kaṭciyār. kaṭaikaṭkuc ceṉṟu pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai (allatu veṟṟilaikkāmpu) eṭuttuvantu, oḷintavarait tēṭippiṭippar. yāraiyēṉum kaṇṭupiṭittapōtu, kaṭaikkuc ceṉṟu vantamaikku aṭaiyāḷamākap pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyaik kāṭṭalvēṇṭum. kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭavar muṉpu ellārum niṉṟa iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. ellāruṅ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭapiṉ viṉaimāṟi viḷaiyāṭuvar.
oḷintiruntavaraik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpōtu pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyaik kāṭṭāviṭiṉum, yāraiyēṉuṅ kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāviṭiṉum, muṉpu kaṇṭupiṭittavarē mīṇṭuṅ kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu pāṇṭināṭṭu viḷaiyāṭṭu. itaṉ cōḻanāṭṭu vakai varumāṟu:
āṭuvārellārum kaipōṭṭu ovvoruvarākap pirittu, iṟutiyilakap paṭṭuk koṇṭavar ēṉaiyāraip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṭikkavēṇṭiyavar caṟṟuk tolaiviluḷḷa oru kuṟippiṭṭa taḻaiyaik koṇṭuvanta piṉputāṉ piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṟkuḷ ēṉaiyarellām maṟaivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil oḷintukoḷvar. kaṇṭupiṭippavar oḷintiruppavaraik kaiyiṉāṟ piṭittukkoḷḷalām, allatu avar peyarai maṭṭum piṟarkkuk kēṭkumāṟu urakkac collalām. ivviraṇṭil etu ceyvateṉṟu muṉṉarē tīrmāṉikkap paṭṭirukkum. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar taḻaiyaik kāṭṭac collumpōtu, kaṇṭupiṭittavar kāṭṭal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ, maṟu muṟaiyum avarē kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. taḻai kāṭṭappaṭiṉ, kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar maṟumuṟai kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. ivviḷaiyāṭṭu kulīmtār eṉṉum urutup peyarāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itait taṉittamiḻil taḻaipaṟittal eṉalām.  (ta.nā.vi.)

puṭṭi - puṭṭil = ampaṟāt tūṇi. te. puṭṭika.
puṭṭi = kuṭuvaipōṉṟa paṟavaiyuṭampiṉ piṟpakuti.
puṭṭi - piṭṭi.
koḻutta kōḻippuṭṭiyai neykkuṭam eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.

(mā. vi. a, pra-ta (“Standing forth prominently” = eṭuppāy muṉniṟpatu) eṉpatu mūlamāyirukkalām eṉṟu karutukiṉṟatu. (va. va : 206).
puṭam

ma. puṭam. o.nō : kuḻai - kukai - kuvai. kuḻai - kuṭai - kuṭaivu.
puṭam - puṭampu = kukai. malaippuṭampun tēṭi yoḷivār (pañca. tirumuka. 1895).  (va. va : 206).

puṇai
mitavaiyum kaṭṭumaramum paricalum teppamum pōṉṟa kaṭattak karuvikaḷ. (ti.ma. 1134).
putu maṇippavaḷap puṉmaiyum puraimaiyum.
oru moḻiyiṉ vaḷattaik kāṭṭuvaṉa ataṉ coṟkaḷēyaṉṟi olikaḷalla. moḻi tōṉṟiyatu coṟkaḷākavēyaṉṟi eḻut tolikaḷāka valla. ilakkaṇam ēṟpaṭṭapōtē. coṟkaḷ eḻuttolikaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu :
kākā - kākkā - kākkai, kākā - (kāka) - kākam.
k + ā + k + ā eṉṟu tōṉṟavillai.
coṟkaḷē poruḷuṇarttum; eḻuttolikaḷalla. ōreḻuttuc collē yāyiṉum, poruḷaḷaviṟ collēyaṉṟi eḻuttoli yaṉṟu. palaveḻuttuc collait taṉittaṉi eḻuttukaḷākap pakuttuviṭiṉ, poruḷ tarātu.
tamiḻil ayaloli kalattal kūṭāteṉpatai yuṇarttavē.

allā vaccai varukka mutalī(ṟu)

potuveḻut toḻinta nālēḻun tiriyum  (naṉ.pata. 19).
eṉṟu pavaṇantiyārum, kūṟip pōntaṉar.
ākavē, coṟṟūymai pōṉṟē olittūymaiyum tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta paṇpām.
veḷināṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷkaḷellām centamiḻp peyar peṟṟaṉa. uruḷaikkiḻaṅku, ēḻilaikkiḻaṅku, karumpu, cāttukkuṭi, centāḻai (aṉṉāci), pukaiyilai, miḷakāy mutaliya nilaittiṇaip poruḷkaḷum, oṭṭakam, oṭṭakac civiṅki, kaḻutai, kōvēṟu kaḻutai (attiri), kutirai, varik kutirai, nīryāṉai mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum; tīkkōḻi, vāṉkōḻi mutaliya paṟavaikaḷum; mitivaṇṭi, pukaivaṇṭi, cūḻcciya vaṇṭi mutaliya ūrtikaḷum; kuṇṭukkuḻāy, vaittūṟṟi, maṇ ṇeṇṇey mutaliya palvakaip poruḷ kaḷum, veḷi nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vantavaiyē.
kutirai oṉṟē paṉṉiru vakaiyāka vakukkappaṭṭu vevvēṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. kuccukkiḻaṅku iṭantoṟum peyar vēṟupaṭṭup paṉṉiru coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, veḷināṭṭup poruḷkaṭkellām veḷināṭṭup peyarkaḷē yiruttal kūṭumeṉpatu poruḷaṟṟa uṟaḻāṭṭē.
piṟamoḻikaḷellām, tamiḻpōl colvaḷamum collākka vāyppum tūymai marapum uṭaiyaṉavalla. āṅkilar kaṭanta munnūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap putup puṉaivukaḷāl tam aṟivaip perukkik koṇṭaṉar. tam puttaṟivuk karuttukaḷaik kuṟikkat tam moḻiyiṟ colliṉmaiyāl, ilattīṉ kirēkka moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu ērāḷamāyk kaṭaṉkoṇṭu tam karuttiṟ kēṟpat tirittukkoṇṭaṉar.

iṅṅaṉam āṅkilar tam karuttaiyuṇarttap piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiyatu, paṅkīṭṭuk kālattil mikutiyāy arici kiṭaikkumiṭattiṟ paiyaiyum, uḻavaṉ tāṉ viḷaitta kūlattaic cantaikkuk koṇṭupōka vaṇṭiyaiyum, iraval peṟṟatokkum.
āṅkilattil maṭṭumaṉṟi ilattīṉ kirēkka moḻikaḷilum pala aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ tamiḻāyiruppatāl, iṟṟai āṅkila aṟiviyaṟ kammiyak kalaik kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṟ pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷaiyē aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. mēlum, āṅkilak kalaikkuṟiyīṭukaḷellām palvēṟu vakaiyil mika eḷiya muṟaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. ammuṟaikaḷaik kaiyāḷiṉ, ellāk kalaikkuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum tamiḻiṟ cevvaiyāka moḻi peyarttuk koḷḷalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭaracu caṭṭattuṟai tavira vēṟetilum kuṟiyīṭukaḷai moḻipeyarkka ituvarai ettakai muyaṟciyum ceyyavillai. kālañ ceṉṟa i.mu. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷai pōṉṟa taṉip paṭṭa tamiḻp pulavarum, kiṇṭi, kōvai, kāraikkuṭi aṇṇā malai nakar mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kallūri māṇavarumē, tam aḷaviṟanta tamiḻp paṟṟiṉāl, āṭcic coṟkaḷaiyum palvēṟu aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum tokuttum moḻipeyarttum uḷḷaṉar. aracu avaṟṟaiyellām tokuttu aḷavaip paṭutti, āṭcic coṟkaḷaip pōṉṟē piṟavaṟṟaiyum āṭcikkuk koṇṭuvarutal vēṇṭum. itaṟkākat takkāraik koṇṭa oru nilaiyāṉa kuḻuvaiyum aracu amarttutal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ mummoḻik kalavaiyāyiṉ, ennilai yaṭaiyumō, iṟaivaṉukkut tāṉ veḷiccam.
oppuyarvaṟṟa taṉṉantaṉit tūya tamiḻai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa niṟai pulavarkkum māṟṟavum citaikkavum atikāra millai. aṅṅaṉamirukka, tamiḻp pulamaiyum tamiḻārāycciyum tamiḻp paṟṟumillāta orucilar tamiḻai urutteriyātu māṟṟa urimaiyuṭaiyarō?
āṭpeyar, iṭappeyar mutaliya moḻipeyarkkak kūṭāta ciṟappuc coṟkaḷaiyum, tamiḻeḻuttilēyē eḻututal vēṇṭum. e-ṭu : cēkkacuppiyar, āppirikkā, piṟa coṟkaḷai yellām moḻipeyarttēyātal vēṇṭum.
pura
purattal = kāttal. pura - puram = kāppu. pura - puravu = kāppu. puravalaṉ = kāvalaṉ. purantā - puṟantā, puṟantarutal = pātukāppaḷittal, kāttal. purantara - purantaraṉ = kāvalaṉ, aracaṉ. purantaraṉ - purantara (va.) = intiraṉ, viṇṇōr kāvalaṉ.  (ti.ma. 749).
puraṭci
tamiḻ camaṟkirutap piṇippiṉiṉṟum, tamiḻaṉ āriya aṭimait taṉattiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai peṟumvarai, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum iṭamiṉmaiyāl,
	tamiḻāynta tamiḻaṉtāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil
	mutalamaiccāy varutal vēṇṭum.
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār puraṭcip pāvēntar. āyiṉ, atu pōtātu, tamiḻāynta taṟukaṇmait tamiḻaṉtāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mutalamaiccāy varutal vēṇṭum. pētaikkillai ūtiyam; pēṭikkillai paṭaikkalam; aṭimaikkillai viṭutalai; añcikkillai aṭaikkalam.
ulakavaralāṟu (World History), kumukaviyal paṇpāṭṭiyal māntaṉūl (Social and Cultural Anthropology), oppiyaṉ moḻi nūl (Comparative Philology) eṉṉum mūvaṟiviyal tamiḻ viṭu talaiyaic cārntiruppatāl, tamiḻiyakkattai moḻi veṟiyeṉṟō iṉaveṟiyeṉṟō evaruñ collamuṭiyātu.
puraṭcip pāvēntaṉ kaṉavu
tamiḻai mūlamākak koṇṭa mēlaiyāriya vakaiyāṉa kirēkkat tiṟku nerukkamāyiruntu, intiyāviṟku vantavuṭaṉ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōy vaṭatiraviṭa mākiya pirākirutattuṭaṉ iraṇṭaṟak kalantu vētamoḻiyāki, piṉṉart tamiḻai yaṇṭi ataṉāṟ peritum vaḷampaṭuttappaṭṭu araic ceyaṟkai ilakkiya naṭaimoḻi yāṉa camaṟkirutattai, ulaka aḷavil talaimaiyākkutaṟ poruṭṭu, oru putiya camaṟkiruta āṅkilap pērakara mutali uruvākkutaṟku, intiya naṭuvaṇaracum, marāṭṭira aracum, palkalaik kaḻaka nalkaik kuḻuvum, pūṉāp palkalaik kaḻakamum, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ amaippum (U.N.O.), iru kōṭikku mēl otukkiyuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilō, ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāṉa centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutali uruvākkavō, camaṟ kirutak kiḷaiyeṉṟum paṉmoḻik kalavaiyeṉṟum pirāmaṇap pulavar kāṭṭiyuḷḷa naccut taṉmai niṟainta ceṉṉaip pal kalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyait tiruttavō, iṟṟaiyaracu oru ciṟu tokaiyum otukkavillai.
tamiḻnāṭṭait tamiḻnāṭeṉṟu araciyalēṭṭiṟ kuṟittata ṉālum, araciṉar cālaik kaṭattap pēriyaṅkikaḷil (Govt. Road Transports Buses) ovvoru tirukkuṟaṭ palakaiyai āṇi yaṟaintu vaittataṉālum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum intiyi ṉiṉṟum viṭutalai peṟṟu vaḷarntu viṭātu.
tamiḻaṉ uṇmaiyāṉa iyalpai yaṟintu atai añcātu pōṟṟik kāppavaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭu āṭcit talaivaṉākum pōtē, tamiḻāynta tamiḻaṉtāṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ mutalamaic cāy varutal vēṇṭum. eṉṉum pāratitācaṉ kaṉavu niṟai vēṟiyatākum.
puram
puram - va. pura (i. vē.).
puram = uyarcci, uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam, mēṉmāṭam, kōpuram, kōpura nakar. (piṅ.). maturaiyantaṇ purattiṉkaṇ (tiruvālavā. 39 : 1). kōpuramuḷḷakōyil, kōyil, mūvarkkōtiya puramum (kallā. 24 : 23).  (va.va : 206 - 207).
purāṇam
purāṇam eṉpatu paḻaimai. ātalāl paḻañceytikaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟum nūl potuvākap purāṇam eṉappaṭum. māpurāṇam, pūtapurāṇam eṉpaṉa teṉṉāṭu vantu taṅkiya āriyar iyaṟṟiya paḻantamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ.
piṟkālattil, (1) ulakap paṭaippu, (2) ulaka aḻivum maṟu tōṟṟamum, (3) teyvaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻimarapu, (4) manukkaḷ āṭci, (5) katirava tiṅkaṭkula aracar varalāṟu ākiya aintaiyuṅ kūṟuvatu purāṇam eṉa ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭṭatu.
taruma cātiraṅkaḷ tamiḻarai aṭimaippaṭuttiṉaveṉiṉ, purāṇaṅkaḷ avarai maṭamaippaṭuttiṉa.
vaṭamoḻip purāṇaṅkaḷ muṉṉeḻuntavai 18; piṉṉeḻun tavai 18. piṉṉeḻuntavai upapurāṇam eṉappaṭum.
patiṉeṇ purāṇam
piramam, patumam, viṣṇu, civam, pākavatam, nāratīyam, mārk kaṇṭēyam, āknēyam, paviṣyam, piramavaivarttam, liṅkam, varākam, kāntam, vāmaṉam, kūrmam, maccam, karuṭam, piramāṇṭam.
upa purāṇam
caṉaṟkumāram, naraciṅkam, nanti, turuvācam, civatarumam, nāratīyam, kapilam, māṉavam, aucaṉacam, vaciṭṭaliṅkam, varuṇam, kāḷikam, cāmpēcam, aṅkiram, cauram, parācaram, mārīcam, pārkkavam.
purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ kālam ki.mu. 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvaraippaṭṭatākum.
itikācam
(iti - ha - āsa = ippaṭi uṇmaiyil iruntatu) 
irāmāyaṇam, makāpāratam eṉa itikācam iraṇṭu.
makāpāratak kālam ki.mu. tōrā. 1000.
makāpāratattoṭu toṭarpuṭaiyatu pakavatkītai. nālvakai varaṇattaiyum avaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟat tāḻvuṭaṉ iṟaivaṉē paṭaitta tāka, kaṇṇaṉ kūṟṟil vaittu valiyuṟuttap pakavatkītai payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. viyakkattakka aracar varalāṟu itikācam.  (va.va.)
puri (1)

vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻt tiricoṟkaḷ
puri - pur, wall, rampart, fortress, city, town, RV.
puri - puri, a town.
tamiḻiṟ puram eṉpatu kōpuramuḷḷa nakaraiyum, puri eṉpatu kōṭṭaiyuḷḷa nakaraiyum, kuṟikkum. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil iv vēṟupāṭiṉmaiyāl, pura, puraya eṉṉum coṟkaḷum kōṭṭai allata nakar eṉṉum potupporuḷē tarum.
puri eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa pari eṉṉum col, tamiḻiṟpōṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyil vaṭṭam eṉṉum aṭipporuḷaiyum muḻumai mikuti mutaliya vaḻip poruḷkaḷaiyum uṇartti, nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉoṭṭāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu : pari, parī - pari, pari, ind. round, around, about. round about, fully, abundantly, richly.
parinÊsa (parimca), the best part of, RV.
parikaÍsana, frequent coughing.
paritkup, to be very angry.
paricar, to move or walk about, go round.
paricaÍra, paricaÍraka, an assistant, attendant, or servant.
parijana, surrounding company of people, entourage.
pariniÍ, to lead about or round, RV., to lead a bride and bridegroom around a sacrificial fire, to marry.
parin´aya, marriage.
paridaÍ, to give, grant, bestow, surrender, RV.
paridaÍna, giving oneself up to the favour or protection of another.
paripri (gÇ¥U), to fill (v.t.), to become completely full (v.i.). paripuÍrana, the act of filling, perfecting, rendering complete.
paribhaÍs, to speak to, address. paribhaÍsaÍ, speech, discourse.
ParibhaÍsaÍ, speech, discourse.
paripac, to bring to maturity; Pass, to be cooked. paripakva, completely cooked or dressed.
parinirvaÍ, to be completely extinguished or emancipated, parinirvaÍn´a, complete extinction of individuality, entire cessation of rebirths.
parimaÍ, to measure round or about, RV. parimaÍn´a, measure of any kind.
parvrit (parivrut), to turn round, revolve, move in a circle or to and ro, roll for wheel or wander about, circumambiate, RV.
parivarta, revolving, revolution.
parivartana, causing to turn round.
parisudh, to bewailed off, become cleane or purified perfectly. parisuddha, cleaned, purified perfectly.
parisad, to sit round, RV., surrounding, an assembly, meeting, group, circle.
vaṭṭak karuttiṉiṉṟu toṟum karuttut tōṉṟuvataṟku, āṭṭai vaṭṭam kācu oṉṟukku ..... palicai eṉṉum kalveṭṭuk kūṟṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭām. vrukṣam parisiñcati (parisincati) (= marantoṟum nīr ūṟṟukiṉṟāṉ) eṉṉum vaṭamoḻic coṟṟoṭar itaiyottiruttal kāṇka.
paripuram eṉṉum collil cūḻtaṟ poruḷum, pari pulampiṉar (cilap. 10 : 226) eṉṉun toṭaril mikutip poruḷum, parimāṟutal eṉṉum collil tiruppal allatu māṟṟutal poruḷum varicaip poruḷum, tōṉṟutal kāṇka.

mēlaiyāriyac coṟkaḷ
kirēkkamum āṅkilamum
Gk. pref. peri, round about.
perianth, floral envelope.
pericardium, membranous sac enclosing the heart.
pericarp, seed-vessel, wall of ripened ovary of plant.
perichondrium, membrane enveloping cartilages (except at joints).
periclinal (geol.), sloping in all directions from central point.
perigynous (of stamen), situated around pistil or ovary.
periosteum, membrane enveloping the bones,
peripteral (of temple), surrounded by single row of pillars.
perisperm, mass of albumen outside embryo-sac in some seeds.
peristome (bot.), fringe of small teeth around mouth of capsule in mosses, (zool). parts around mouth in various invertebrates.
perity phlitis, inflammation of some part around the caceum, e.g. appendicitis.
pericranium, membrane enveloping skull.
periapt, thing worn about the person as charm, amulet.
perigee, that point in planet’s (esp. moon’s) orbit at which it is nearest to earth.
perihelion, that point in planet’s orbit at which it is nearest to sun.
perimeter, circumference, onthire of closed figure.
period, round of time marked by recurrence of astronomical coincidences.  Gk. peri, round. hodos, way.
periodical, magazine published at regular intervals, e.g. monthly.
peripatetic, a. & n. Aristotelian, so called from Aristotle’s custom of walking about in Lyecum while teaching.
periphery, bounding line, esp. of round surface, external boundary or surface. Gk. periphercia, circumference, peri round, phero, bear.
periphrasis, roundabout way of speaking, roundabout phrase, circumlocution.
periscope, kinds of tube and mirror apparatus by which one in a trench or in a submarine can see things above the parapet or water respectively. periscopic, enabling one to see distinctly for some distance round axis of vision.
perispomenon (Gk. gram). word with circumflex accent on last syllable.
peristaltic (pysiol). applied to the automatic muscular movement consisting of wave-like contractions in successive circles, by which contents of alimentary canal etc. are propelled along it.
peristyle, row of columns surrounding temple, court, cloister etc, space so surrounded.
peritoneum (anat.), double serous membrane lining cavity of abdomen.
tiruntiya vaḻakkilum koccai vaḻakkilum akara mutal ekara mutalākat tirivatu iyalpē.
e-ṭu : paru - peru, araṅkaṉ - raṅkaṉ - reṅkaṉ. im muṟaiyil pari eṉṉum tamiḻccol kirēkkattil peri eṉṟu tirintatu.
pari eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa cila coṟkaḷum tamiḻt tiri coṟkaḷāyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.

piṇi - piṇikai = kaccu (peṇṭir māroṭṭic caṭṭai).
puṇ - puṇai. puṇaittal = kaṭṭutal.
puṇai = 1. kaṭṭu, vilaṅku (cūṭā.). 2. teppam (kaṭṭumaram). nallāṇmai yeṉṉum puṇai (kuṟaḷ. 1134). 3. utavi. aṟampuṇaiyākalu muṇṭu (kalit. 144). 4. oppu. puṇaiyillā vevva nōy (kalit. 124). 5. īṭu. 6. āṭpiṇai. ivaṉukkup puṇai (S.I.I.V. 173).
puṇai - puṇaiyal = māṭṭup piṇaippu. puṇaiya laṭittal = piṇaitta kaṭāviṭṭuk katirai yuḻakkutal. puṇaiyal - puṇaical.
puṇai - piṇai. piṇaital = 1. cērtal, iṇaital. kaḻunīr piṇaintaṉṉa vāki (cīvaka. 491). 2. ceṟital. piṇaiyūpa meḻuntāṭa (maturaik. 27). 3. puṇartal. maṅkaiyar tammoṭum piṇaintu (tiruvāca. 41 : 6).
piṇai = 1. poruttu. itaiyuṅ kayiṟum piṇaiyu miriya (paripā. 10 : 54). 2. kaṭṭu. 3. iṇaikkappaṭukai. piṇaiyāra mārpam (pu.ve. 12 : 6). 4. pūmālai. veṟikoṇṭa mullaip piṇaimītu (periyapu. kaṇṇappa. 57). 5. poṟuppāṇmai. piṇaik koṭukkilum pōka voṭṭārē (tiv. periyāḻ. 4 : 5 : 2). 6. uṭaṉpāṭu (yāḻ. aka.). 7. kaḷaikaṇ, pēṇukai. peṭṭāṅ koḻukum piṇaiyili (nāṉmaṇi. 91).
piṇai - piṇaiccu = puṇarcci (piṅ.).
puṇaiyal - piṇaiyal = 1. oṉṟucērkkai. tāmarai yalariṇaip piṇaiyal (paripā. 2 : 53). 2. naṭaṉa iṇaikkai. piṇṭiyum piṇaiyalum (cilap. 3 : 18). 3. mālaivakai. pittikaip piṇaiyaṉ mālai (cīvaka. 2177). 4. pūmālai. tātuku piṇaiyal vīci (cīvaka. 463). 5. katiruḻakkuṅ kaṭāp piṇaippu. 6. puṇarcci. 
7. katavup poruttu.
puṇ - pūṇ. pūṇutal = 1. nukattiṟ kaṭṭappaṭutal. pūṇṭaṉa puraviyō (kamparā. irāvaṇaṉva. 38). 2. maṇañ ceytal. pūṇṭāṉ kaḻittaṟ karumaiyāl (nālaṭi. 56). 3. neruṅki yiṟukka mātal. avaṉukkup pal pūṇṭuviṭṭatu (u.va.). 4. vilaṅku māṭṭappaṭutal. puṉaipūṇum (kuṟaḷ. 836). 5. nukam māṭṭik koḷḷutal. paṭunukam pūṇāy pakaṭē (cilap. 27 : 228). 6. aṇital. pūṇpatuvum poṅkaravam (tiruvāca. 12 : 1). 7. mēṟkoḷḷutal. aṉpu pūṇṭaṉai (kamparā. vipīṭaṇaṉaṭai. 2). pōrttoḻil vēṭkai pūṇṭu (kamparā. paṭaittalai. 1).
ka. pūṇ, te. pūṇu.
pūṇ = 1. ulakkai taṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉaiyiṟ ceṟitta toṭi allatu vaḷaiyam. pūṇceṟinta talaiyai yuṭaiya pariya taṇṭukkōlai (puṟam. 243, urai). 2. yāṉaik kōṭṭiṉ kimpuri (piṅ). 3. aṇi. nuṇpū ṇākam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅki (maturaik. 569). 4. kavacam. pūṇaṇimārpa pōṟṟi (cīvaka. 264).
k., pūṇ, te. poṉṉu.
pūṇ - pūṇi = 1. nukam pūṇum erutu. pūṇi pūṇṭuḻap puṭcilampum tēvā. 647 : 3). kaṭampūrukku vaḻiyetuveṉiṉ iṭampūṇi yeṉṉāviṉ kaṉṟu eṉal (nēmi. col. 5, urai). 2. kaṉṟukāli. pūṇi mēykku miḷaṅkōvalar (tiv. periyāḻ. 3 : 16 : 7). 3. viṭaiyōrai (iṭaparāci). 4. oruvakaip paṟavai. perum pūṭ pūṇiyum pēḻvāyk kokkum (peruṅ. uñcaik. 51 : 69).
pūṇittal = 1. poruttuvittal. 2. tōṟṟuvittal. vicumpiṟ puttap putumatiyam pūṇittāṉ (māṟaṉalaṅ. 224). 3. mēṟkōḷ pūṇutal. eṉṉōṭē pūṇittu (īṭu, 10 : 8 : 5). 4. poruttik kuṟittal. (to allude to, to refer to).
pūṇ - pūṭkai = 1. mēṟkōḷ (piratikñai). pūṭkaiyillōṉ yākkai pōla (puṟam. 69). 2. koḷkai. 3. mēṟkōḷoṭu kūṭiya valimai. ōṭāp pūṭkaip piṇimukam vāḻtti (tirumuruku. 247).
pūṇ - pūṇṭu = paṟpōṉṟa paruppukaḷ allatu kaḷaikaḷ neruṅkip poruntiya pūṭuvakai. e-ṭu : 1. veṅkāyap pūṇṭu, veḷḷaip pūṇṭu. 2. ciṟṟeccam (vestige, trace). āṇpūṇṭu kaṇṭālum pēcāmaṟ kaṭṭiṭṭāḷ. (viṟaliviṭu. 822).
pūṇṭu - pūṭu = 1. veṅkāyam allatu veḷḷuḷḷi. 2. nilattoṭu paṭarnta ciṟuceṭi. pullākip pūṭāy (tiruvāca. 1 : 26).
o.nō : kūṇṭu - kūṭu.
pūṇ - pūṭci = 1. poruntukai, pūṇukai (catu.). 2. aṇi (kallā. 91 : 9, urai). 3. puṇarppu. pūtap pūṭci yuḷḷeḻum pōtamum (pirapōta. 31 : 36). 4. uyiroṭu pūṇṭa vuṭal (piṅ.). pulluyirkkum pūṭci puṇarttāy (tēvā. 1160 : 6). 5. koḷkai. 6. maṉavuṟuti. uṟuporu ḷuṇarum pūṭciyōy (kamparā. uyut. mantira. 66). 7. urimai (aka.ni.). 8. varivakai. e-ṭu : īḻam pūṭci.
pūṇ - pūṭṭu (pi.vi.). pūṭṭutal = 1. poruttutal. 2. kāḷai mutaliyavaṟṟai nukattiṟ piṇaittal. kaḷiṟupala pūṭṭi (patiṟ. 44 : 16). 3. porutti aṇital. paintār pūṭṭi (patiṟ. 42 : 10). 4. māṭṭutal. porucilaimēṟ caram pūṭṭāṉ (ēreḻu. 17). 5. toḻuvilaṭittal (W.). 6. vilaṅku māṭṭutal (W.). 7. nāṇēṟṟutal. pūṭṭucilai (cīvaka. 1788). 8. pūṭṭup pūṭṭutal. 9. iṟukak kaṭṭutal. pūṭṭiya kaiyaṉ (kamparā. kukap. 34). 10. maṉattai yiṇaittal. eṉpāṟ pūṭṭu naṇpu pūṇṭāṉ (pārata. vāraṇā. 37). 11. oppaṭaittup poṟuppēṟṟutal. tātai pūṭṭiya celvam (kamparā. vāliva. 81). 12 poruttik kūṟutal (W.). 13. vaḻakkut toṭuttal. koṭumaicāl vaḻakkup pūṭṭi (tiruviḷai. māmaṉā. 14).
ma. pūṭṭuka, ka. pūṭu.
pūṭṭu - pūṭṭakam = 1. pōli. e - ṭu : pūṭṭaka vēlai. 2. vīṇ perumai. eṉṉa pūṭṭakamō (perunto. 1275). 3. vañcakam. pūṭṭaka mōvitu (irāmanā. ayōt. 21). 4. marumam (irakaciyam). antac ceyti pūṭṭakamā yirukkiṟatu. (u.va.).
ka. pūṭṭaka, te. pūṭṭakamu.
pul - pol - poru. porutal = 1. poruntutal. poyporu muṭaṅkukai (cilap. 15 : 50). 2. toṭutal, muṭṭutal. viṇporu pukaḻ (puṟam. 11). viṇporu viyaṉkuṭai (puṟam. 35). 3. oppātal. malai yettaṉai yattaṉai... porukiṟpaṉa (kamparā. atikā. 150). 4. taṭavutal. vīṇai porutoḻilum (cīvaka. 1795). 5. tākkutal. porupuṉal tarūum (ciṟupāṇ. 118). 6. kaṭaital. oṟṟaikkiri mattaip porutu (tiruppu. 1). 7. vaṉmaiyāy vīcutal. kurūuppukai picirak kālpora (patiṟ. 15 : 6). 8. pōr ceytal. īraim patiṉmarum porutukaḷat toḻiya (puṟam. 2). 9. cūtāṭutal. pōtu pōkumā ṟiruntu porutum (pārata. cūtu. 169). 10. māṟupaṭutal. kaṇpora viḷaṅkuniṉ viṇporu viyaṉkuṭai (puṟam. 35).
poru = 1. oppu. poruvākap pukkiruppar (tēvā. 780 : 11). 2. uvamai (piṅ.). 3. etirttaṭai. āṟu camayaṅkaṭ kellām poruvāki niṉṟāṉavaṉ (tiv. tiruvāy. 9 : 4 : 8).
porumuka veḻiṉi = porunti varum iraṭṭait tirai. poruṅkatavu = iraṭṭaik katavu.
poru - poruvu. poruvutal = 1. poruntutal. 2. ottal.  uṉṉaip poruvu muṉivōr (kantapu. tirukkal. 55). 3. nērtal. pōṉṟaṉa viṉaiya taṉmai poruviyatu (kamparā. iraṇiya. 152). 4. urāytal. viṟkoṇāṇ poruvutōḷ (kamparā. nintaṉai. 52).
poruvu = oppu. poruvaṟṟā ḷeṉmakaḷ (tiv. tiruneṭun. 19).
oppupporuvu, uṟaḻporuvu eṉap poruvu iruvakai.
poruva = ōr uvamavurupu. pullap poruva (tol. uvama. 11).
poru - porun. porunutal = 1. poruntutal. uṇ porun (naṉ. 137). 2. uṭaṉpaṭutal (W.).
porun - poruntu. poruntutal = 1. neruṅkutal. poruntavan tuṟṟa pōril (kamparā. kumpakaruṇa. 13). 2. aṭaital. vantaṭi porunti muntai niṟpiṉ (puṟam. 10). 3. aḷavaḷāvutal (piṅ.). 4. puṇartal. maṇimēkalai... poruntiṉa ḷeṉṉum pāṉmaik kaṭṭurai (maṇimē. 23 : 46). 5. ilakkaṇa neṟippaṭutal. paṇporunta icaipāṭum (tēvā. 268 : 5). 6. iyalutal. pūta maintiṉum poruntiya vuruviṉāṟ puraḷāṉ (kamparā. iraṇiya. 18). 7. nikaḻtal. puṇṇiyam poruntiṟṟu (kamparā. kumpakaruṇa. 131). 8. niṟaivēṟutal (W.). 9. takutiyātal. 10. amaital. aṟaneṟi porunta (kamparā. vipīṭaṇa. 43). 11. maṉam icaital. koṇarkuvāyeṉap poruntiṉaṉ (kamparā. maruntu. 85).
ka. porutu.
poruntu - poruttu = 1. iṇaippu (W.). 2. mūṭṭu. 3. uṭalelumpu mūṭṭu. poruttelāṅ kaṭṭuviṭṭu (paṇaviṭu. 298). 4. oṉṟucērppu. poruttuṟu poruḷuṇ ṭāmō (kamparā. kumpakaruṇa. 157). 5. neṟṟimūlai maṇṭaiyōṭṭup poṭṭu.
poruttu - poruttam = 1. poruntukai. 2. takuti. poymmukaṅ kāṭṭik karattal poruttamaṉṟu (tirukkō. 356). 3. iṇakkam. avaṉukkum eṉakkum poruttamillai (u.va.). 4. maṇamakkaḷiṉ piṟappiya (jātaka) iṇakkam.
poruttu - poruttaṉai = 1. poruttam. 2. nērttikkaṭaṉ. (tuṉpa nilaiyil teyvattoṭu iṭṭa cūḷai niṟaivēṟṟutal). itaic ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali pirārttaṉai eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripākak kuṟittuḷḷatu.
poruttu - pottu.
pottutal = 1. poruttutal. 2. kalattal. neñcam potti (pu.ve. 11, āṇpāl 4). 3. tī mūṭṭutal. kaṉaiyeri potti (maṇimē. 2 : 42). 4. taittu mūṭṭutal. 5. taittal. ilaiyaip pottu (u.va.). 6. mālai kaṭṭutal. maṟukaṇṇiyum potti (īṭu, 1 : 3 : 1). 7. kaṟpaṉai ceytal. illāt teyvam potti (tēvā. 658 : 7). 8. tiṟappiṉ allatu kiḻiviṉ irupuṟattaiyum porutti mūṭutal. pollam pottutal (u.va.). 9. vāy kaṇ kātu mutaliyavaṟṟai mūṭutal. taṉcevit toḷaiyiru kaikaḷāṟ potti (kamparā. iraṇiya. 22). 10. viral maṭakki uḷḷaṅkaiyai mūṭutal. (u.va.). 11. maṟaittal. cempāl... kiḷarpaṭi pottiṉa (kamparā. karaṉvatai. 100). 12. putaittal. maṇṇiṭaik kaṭitu pottutal (kamparā. virātaṉvatai. 43).
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.). = niṟaital. vāṉmēṟ pottiṉa kuḻu (kamparā. karaṉvatai. 100).
f., ma. pottu.

pottakam - va. putaka, i. putak.
pūrjam paṭṭaiyiṉiṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu vaṭavar kūṟum poruṭkaraṇiyam poruntātu. putaka eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭivaiyē puttakam eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.

	mati - mātam - va. māsa - mācam.

pul2 (paruttaṟ karuttuvēr)
poruntaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu paruttaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟucērvatāl parumai uṇṭākum. cērē tiraṭci (tol. uri. 65).
pul - pal = pala. palakāṟ paṟavai = vaṇṭu. palkāṟ paṟavai kiḷaicet tōrkkum (perumpāṇ. 183).
palkāl = pala camaiyam, aṭikkaṭi, palkālun tōṉṟi (nālaṭi. 27).
pal - paṉmai = 1. oṉṟiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa tokaip parumai. tāṉaṟi poruḷvayiṟ paṉmai kūṟal (tol. kiḷavi. 23). 
2. tokuti. uyirppaṉmai (puṟam. 19). 3. oru taṉmaippaṭ ṭirāmai. paṉmaiyē pēcum paṭiṟaṉ ṟaṉṉai (tēvā. 674 : 5). 4. kuṟippiṉmai. paṉmaiyākavē pēciṉāṉ (u.va.). (W.).
pal - pallam = oru pēreṇ (tivā.). pal = mikunta. palvalip paṟavai = carapam. palvalip paṟavai paṟṟupu (peruṅ. ilāvāṇa. 11 : 54). pallam - pallavam = palamuṟai pāṭappaṭum kīrttaṉai yuṟuppu (W.).
pallavam - pallavi = tirumpat tirumpap palamuṟai pāṭappaṭum kīrttaṉai mutaluṟuppu. ka. pallavi.
pal - pallakku = civikaiyiṉum periya (tūkkic cellum) ūrti. tantap pallakkuñ civikaiyun tāṅki (toṇṭai. cata. 87). civikai (ciṟuttatu) x pallakku. civinta paḻam eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
ma. pallakku, ka. pallakki, pirā. pallaṅkka, ca. paryaṅkka.
ceṉṉaip pa.ka.ta. akaramutaliyil, ic col camaṟkirutatti ṉiṉṟu pirākiruta vaḻiyākat tamiḻukku vantateṉṟu talaikīḻākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
pallakkaik kuṟikkum paryaṅkka eṉṉum vaṭacol, palyaṅkka eṉṉum vaṭivum koḷḷum. atu teṉcol vaṭiviṟku nerukkamāṉa teṉpatu teḷivu.
paryaṅkka eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku mūla viḷakkam, māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta āṅkila akaramutaliyiṟ piṉvarumāṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
‘paryanc to turn about or round, revolve, R.V. X, 119, 5.
pary-anka a bed, couch, sofa, litter, palanquin, Kaush Up...” purital = vaḷaital. e- ṭu : valampuri, iṭampuri, puri - pari. pariti = vaṭṭamāṉa katiravaṉ. parical = vaṭṭamāṉa nīrkaṭattuk kūṭai. paricai = vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam.
aṅkutal = vaḷaital, cāytal, kōṇutal, vāṭṭañ cāṭṭamātal.
vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku - aṅku. vaṅku - vaṅki = neḷivaḷaiyal, vaḷainta katti (piccuvā). aṅku - aṅkaṇam = vāṭṭañ cāṭṭamāṉa cāykaṭai.
aṅkaṇattu ḷukka vamiḻtaṟṟāl  (kuṟaḷ. 720)
ūraṅ kaṇanīr uravunīr cērntakkāl  (nālaṭi. 175).
pari - va. pari, round, around, about, round about.
aṅku - va. añc, to blend, curve, incline, curl.
o.nō : poṅku - poñcu.
paryaṅkka eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkuk kūṟappaṭum poruṭ karaṇiyam, orumaruṅku tamiḻukkum poruntum.
pallaṅku - pallakku - pallākku.

pul3 (tiraṭṭaṟ karuttuvēr)
paruttaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu tiraṭṭaṟ karuttup piṟakkum.
poḻittal = tiraṭṭutal. poḻippu = tiraṭṭu, piṇṭam.
poḷi = tiraṇṭa varappu. poḷiyai veṭṭi iraṇṭu taḷaiyum oṉṟākkiṉāṉ (W.).
varampu kaṭṭutal, varampu tiraṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.
poḷi - poḻi = 1. kaṇu. poṟkuṭam poruntiya poḻiyamai maṇittūṇ (peruṅ. uñcaik. 48 : 87). 2. vayalvarampu, varappu.
pul - puḷ - piḷ - piḻampu = 1. tiraṭci. kaṭuntaḻaṟ piḻampaṉṉa (tiruvāca. 29 : 7). 2. vaṭivu. uṇmai yuṇarttip piḻampuṇarttap paṭātaṉa (iṟai. kaḷa. pa. 15). 3. uṭal. piḻampunaṉi yularttal (tol. puṟat. 20, urai.
piḷ - piṇṭu = 1. tiraṭci. 2. uṭampu. piṇṭālam vittiṉ (tiruman 3025).
o.nō : kaḷ - kaṇṭu, naḷ - naṇṭu, cuḷ - cuṇṭu, vaḷ - vaṇṭu.
piṇṭu - piṇṭam = 1. tiraḷai. 2. cōṟṟuttiraḷai. piṇṭa mēya peruñcōṟṟu nilaiyum (tol. puṟat. 8). 3. uṇṭai (piṅ.). 4. teṉpulat tārkkup paṭaikkum cōṟṟuṇṭai. 5. cataittiraḷ. uṟuppil piṇṭamum (puṟam. 28). 6. uṭampu. uṇṭi mutaṟṟē uṇaviṉ piṇṭam (puṟam. 18). 7. tokuti. parivutapa veṭukkum piṇṭa nacciṉ (puṟam. 184). 8. nūṟpirivup poruḷaip potuppaṭat tokuttuk kūṟum nūṟpā. piṇṭan tokaivakai kuṟiyē ceykai (naṉ. 20). 9. mūvuṟuppaṭaṅkiya nūl. mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya piṇṭattāṉum (tol. cey. 170).
o.nō : kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam, tuṇṭu - tuṇṭam.
te. piṇṭali, va. piṇṭa, ka. peṇṭṭe, heṇṭṭe, heṇṭe.
piṇṭakkaru = uṟuppaṟṟa cataittiraḷāṉa karu (embryo, foetus).
piṇṭak kāppu = cōṟu. piṇṭak kāppukkut tāḷam pōṭukiṟāṉ (u.va.).
piṇṭak karumam = teṉpulattārkkup piṇṭam paṭaikkum caṭaṅku.
piṇṭakaṉ = piṇṭam paṭaippōṉ.
piṇṭa nūṟpā = atikārap poruḷai allatu nūṟpirivup poruḷait tokuttuk kūṟum nūṟpā.
piṇṭatāṉam = piṇṭap paṭaippu.
piṇṭap poruḷ = karuttu (W.).
piṇṭap poḻippu = piṇṭavurai (ci.pō. pā. 1, pa. 26, cuvāminā).
piṇṭapākam = aiyam (piccai) (aka. ni.).
piṇṭam piṭittal = (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. uṇṭai tiraṭṭutal. 
2. uruccitaittu uruṇṭai yākkutal.
(ce. ku. vi.). karu uṇṭātal (u.va.).
piṇṭam viḻutal = kāy viḻutal (abortion).
piṇṭavurai = poḻippurai (W.).
piṇṭu - piṇṭi. o.nō : kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi, tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi.
piṇṭi = 1. vaṭivam (cilap. 3 : 26, urai). 2. kūṭṭam (W.). 3. ēḻām nāḷ (puṉarpūcam) (cūṭā.). 4. iṇaiyā viṉaikkai vakai (cilap. 3 : 18, urai).
piṇṭittal = (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). tiraḷai yākkutal. piṇṭittu vaitta vuṇṭiyai (tol. puṟat. 8, urai). 2. tokuttal.
(ce. ku. vi.). tiraḷutal. piṇṭittup perukiṟ ṟeṉpār (cīvaka. 1276).
piṇṭi - piṭi. o. nō : kaṇṭi - kaṭi, taṇṭi - taṭi.
piṭittal = (ce. kuṉṟāvi). 1.kaiyāl iṟukkit tiraṭṭutal allatu tiraḷaiyākkutal. poriviḷaṅkāy piṭikka vēṇṭum. (u.va.). 2. kaiyāl iṟukkit tiraṭṭip paṭimaiyamaittal. piḷḷaiyār piṭikkak kuraṅkāy muṭintatu (paḻa.). 3. kaiyāl iṟukkit tiraṭṭutal pōl oṉṟai iṟukap paṟṟutal. kuṭai piṭittāṉ (u.va.). 4. oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟaip paṟṟutal. pūṉai eliyaip piṭikkum, iṉṟaikkuk kuḷattil mīṉ piṭikkiṟārkaḷ. ōṭukiṟa māṭṭaip piṭittukkoṇṭu vantāṉ. ūrkāvalar kuṟṟavāḷiyaip piṭittukkoṇṭārkaḷ. intap piḷḷaiyaik koñcanēram piṭi (u.va.). 5. kōṇiyaip piṭittu vāṅkutal. tirunelvēlikku arici piṭikkap pōyirukkiṟārkaḷ. (u.va.). 6. kayiṟṟaip piṭittu vāṅkutal. kāṅkēyattiliruntu ōru cōṭi māṭu piṭittu vantirukkiṟāṉ (u.va.). 7. kalattil nīr koḷḷutal. kuḻāyil nīr piṭittuvā (u.va.). 8. kaḻaṅku viḷaiyāṭutal. taṭṭāṅ kal piṭittu viḷaiyāṭukiṟārkaḷ. oṟṟaiyā iraṭṭaiyā piṭikkalāmā? (u.va.). 9. poruttutal. nāṟkālik kālukku vāṅku piṭikka vēṇṭum (u.va.). 10. aḻuttit taṭavutal. uṭampu piṭikkiṟavaṉ iṅkē irukkiṟāṉā? 11. cuḷukkutal. kālil narampu piṭittukkoṇṭirukkiṟatu (u.va.). 12. iṟukkamāyiruttal. caṭṭai mutukiṟ piṭikkiṟatu (u.va.). 13. uḷḷaṭakkutal. antap peṭṭi ellār uṭuppaiyum piṭikkum (u.va.). 14. oṭṭikkoḷ ḷutal. pēṉ piṭitta talai (u.va.). 15. tāṅkutal. muṭṭukkaṭṭai cuvaraip piṭittuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu (u.va.). 16. oḷippaṭam eṭuttal. paṭam piṭikkum iṭam (u.va.). 17. paṟṟik koḷḷutal. yār cēlaiyiliruntō cāyam piṭittuviṭṭatu (u.va). 18. paṟṟi yerital. kuṭicaiyil tīppiṭittuviṭṭatu. 19. aṭittal. vitai piṭitta kāḷai. 20. kuṟaittal. campaḷattiṟ pattu rūpā piṭittuk koṇṭārkaḷ (u.va.). 21. niṟutti yaṭakkutal. callikkaṭṭil māṭu piṭippārkaḷ (u.va.). 22. aṭaital. perukiya celvanī piṭi (kamparā. kiḷaikāṇ. 23). 23. aṭain tēṟutal. maturai pōy vaṇṭiyaip piṭittāṉ. 24. collaip payaṉpaṭuttal. avaṉ coṉṉa collaip piṭittukkoṇṭāṉ. 25. akappaṭuttu tal. avaṉ piṭikoṭukka māṭṭāṉ (u.va.). 26. vacappaṭuttutal. avaṉaip piṭittāl itu eḷitāy muṭiyum (u.va.). 27. paṟṟuk kōṭākak koḷḷutal. añceḻuttiṉ puṇaipiṭittuk kiṭakkiṉṟēṉai (tiruvāca. 5 : 27). 28. pukalaṭaital. uṉṉai nāṉ piṭittēṉ koḷ (tiv. tiruvāy. 2 : 6 : 1). 29. mēṟ koḷḷutal. vicuva rūpam piṭittāṉē (irāmanā. cuntara. 19). 30. uṇarntu koḷḷutal. āciriyaṉ kaṟpittatai māṇavar piṭittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum (u.va.). 31. kuṟikkoḷḷutal. piṭittatu muṭikkavalla viccaiyar (pirapōta. 24 : 52). 32. kaṭaippiṭittal. perumāṉurai piṭittēm (kamparā. nikum. 143). 33. oṭṭāram paṇṇutal. orē piṭiyāyp piṭikkiṟāṉ (u.va.).
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. oṭṭikkoḷḷutal. aḻukkup piṭitta tuṇṭu. 2. tōṉṟutal. maḻaiyiṟ pācam piṭikkum (u.va.). 3. viruppamātal. eṉakkum avaṉukkum piṭikkātu (u.va.). 4. ēṟṟatātal. antavūrt taṇṇīr eṉakkup piṭikkavillai (u.va.). 5. celavātal. tirumaṇattiṟku āyiram urupā piṭikkum (u.va.). 6. nikaḻtal. malainakaril mālaitoṟum maḻai piṭikkum (u.va.).

piṇṭam eṉṉum teṉcol, vaṭamoḻi marapiṉpaṭi īṟukeṭṭup piṇṭa eṉa irukku vētattil niṉṟu, tiraḷai, uṇṭai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. ataṉiṉṟu, piṇṭatva, piṇṭak, piṇṭaṉa mutaliya taṉic coṟkaḷaiyum; piṇṭakanta, piṇṭakaraṇa, piṇṭatarkkaka mutaliya kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷaiyum, ēṟattāḻa eḻupatuvarai tirittuḷḷaṉar.
aṅṅaṉamē, piṇṭi eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭiviṉiṉṟu, piṇṭita, piṇṭiṉ, piṇṭī, mutaliya taṉiccoṟkaḷaiyum; piṇṭitamūlya, piṇṭitārtta, piṇṭīkaraṇa, sapiṇṭīkaraṇa, piṇṭīpāva (bh) mutaliya kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷaiyum; ēṟattāḻa muppatu varai tirintuḷḷaṉar.
āriyattiṟkut tamiḻoṭu allatu tiraviṭattoṭu toṭar pillaiyeṉṟum, camaṟkirutam ēṟattāḻa ainnūṟu coṟkaḷai maṭṭum tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa teṉṟum, kūṟum parō eṉṉum camaṟkirutap pērāciriyar kūṭa, piṇṭam eṉṉum col teṉcollē yeṉṟu tam camaṟkiruta moḻi (Sanskrit Language) eṉṉum nūliṟ piṉvarumāṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
“Pinda lump, clod’: Ka, petta, pette, pente, hente, hende, clod, lump of earth’, Te, pedda, pella ‘id’, pendali ‘a lump or mass’, Ka., Te, pindu to squeeze together”.
o. nō : piḻi. piḻital = uḷḷirukkum nīrpporuḷ vaṭiyumāṟu, oṉṟai iṟukkip piṭittal.
piṇṭam allatu piṇṭi eṉpatu vaṭacollāyiṉ, piṭi eṉṉum viṉaiccollum, ataṉ nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tiri coṟkaḷum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum vaṭacollām. piṭi eṉpatu, kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟiya aṭippaṭait tamiḻccoṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟātalālum, irukkuvētam mikap piṟkālattatātalālum, piṇṭam allatu piṇṭi eṉpatu teṉcollē yeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām.

“pet, n., & v.t. (-tt-). 1. Animal tamed & kept as favourite, or treated with fondness; darling, favourite, (often attrib); one’s ... aversion, what one specially dislikes; ... cock, small stop-cock for draining, letting out steam, etc; - name, one expressing fondness or familiarity.

ka. pari. pari - va. pari.
“Pari, ind. round, around, about, round about; fully, abundantly, richly (esp.) ibc. (where also pari) to express fullness (or high degree), R.V. & c. & c. as a prep. (with acc.) about (in space and time), R.V. , A.V., against opposite to, in the direction of, towards, to, ib. (of. Pam. i, 4, 90; also at the beginning of a comp. mfn. cf. ib. ii, 2, 18, Vartt. 4 Fat. and Pary adhyayana), beyond, more than, A.V., to the share of (with as or bhu, to fall to a person’s lot.). Pam i, 4, 90, successively, severally (e.g. vriksham pari sincati, he waters tree after tree), ib; (with abl.) from, away from, out of, R.V.; A.V.; SBr. (cf. Pam, I, 4.93); outside of, except, Pam, 1,4,88, Kat. (often repeated, ib. viii, 1,5; also at the beginning of or the end of am ind. Comp., ib. II, 1, 12); after the lapse of Mu. III, 119; MBh. XIII, 4672 (some read parisamvatsarat); in consequence or on account or for the sake of, R.V; A.V.; according to (esp. dharmanas pari, according to ordinance or in conformity with law or right),  R.V.; (cf. Zd. pairi, Gk. pari)”.
- A Sanskrit English Dictionary by Sir Monier Monier Williams, p. 591.
“peri, pref. = Gk. peri round, about, as : perianth, floral envelope;- carditis n., inflammation of - cardium; - cardium, membranous sac enclosing the heart, so - cardiac, - cardial, aa.; pericarp, seed-vessel, wall of ripened ovary of plant........”
- The Concise Oxford Dictionary

accoṭu tākkiya pāruṟ ṟiyaṅkiya
paṇṭaic cākāṭ ṭāḻcci colliya
varimaṇal ñemarak kaṟpaka naṭakkum
perumitap pakaṭṭukkut tuṟaiyu muṇṭō  (puṟam. 90).
nirampāta nīryāṟ ṟiṭumaṇalu ḷāḻntu
perumpāra vāṭavarpōṟ peypaṇṭan tāṅki
maruṅkoṟṟi mūkkūṉṟit tāṭavaḻntu vāṅki  (cīvaka. 2784).
uḻaippatu kāḷai.
kāḷai = 1. iḷaverutu. 2. kaṭṭiḷamaip paruvattiṉaṉ (tivā.). 3. āṇmakaṉ (tivā.). 4. pālainilat talaivaṉ (tivā.). 5. maṟavaṉ. uravuvēṟ kāḷaiyum (puṟam. 334).
mutaṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ avaṉ kālattiṟ paṇṭaiyaṉāyiruntirukka muṭiyātātalāl, paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉ kuṭippeyarait tirittu, paḻaimaiyāṉavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ nāṭu eṉpatu piṟkālattiṟ pāṇṭināṭeṉa maruviṟṟu.
kuṟippu : 1. vaḷaitaṟ karuttu, cāytal, kōṇal, maṭaṅkal, neḷital, vaṭṭamātal, uruṇṭaiyātal, uruḷtal, cuṟṟutal, cuḻalutal mutaliya iṉak karuttuka ḷellāvaṟṟaiyun taḻuvum.
2. vaṭṭak karuttiṉiṉṟu muḻumaik karuttup piṟakkum. e-ṭu : kaṭaṉai vaḷḷitāyk koṭuttut tīrttuviṭṭāṉ. vaḷḷitu = vaṭṭamāṉatu, muḻumaiyāṉatu.
E. roundly = in thorough - going manner.
im muṟaiyil, pari eṉṉum teṉcollum vaṭamoḻiyil muṉṉoṭṭāki (prefix), allatu muḻuniṟaivu muḻumaik karuttait tōṟṟuvikkum. e-ṭu : pūraṇam - paripūraṇam.
pul6 (tuḷaittaṟ karuttuvēr)
pullutal = tuḷaittal. iv viṉaiccol vaḻakkiṟantatu. 
pul = 1. uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa nilaittiṇaivakai. puṟakkāḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa (tol. marapu. 86). 2. ūṉuṇṇā vilaṅkuṇavāṉa tāḷuḷḷa nilaittiṇaivakai. pacumpuṟ ṟalaikāṇ paritu (kuṟaḷ. 16). 3. uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa paṉai. aṉṟil puṟcēkkai pukku (kallā. 38:10). 4. paṉai vaṭivāṉa nāḷ (aṉuṭam) (piṅ.). 5. uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa teṉṉai (tailava. taila.). 6. uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa mūṅkil. pullāṅkuḻal (u.va.) 7. puṟpōṉṟa kampam payir (piṅ.). pul, kampampul (u.va.). 8. pullarici. 9. ciṟumai. pulluḷai (kamparā. varaikkāṭci. 70). puṉmai = ciṟumai, ciṟutaṉmai. 10. iḻivu. puṉkōṭṭi (nālaṭi.255). pullaṉ = iḻintōṉ. kallāp pullarkku nallōr coṉṉa poruḷeṉa (kamparā.  tāṭakai. 72).

paḷḷaṉ - maḷḷaṉ = marutanilattil vāḻum uḻavaṉ. maḷḷa ruḻupaka ṭurappuvār (kamparā. nāṭa. 18).
paḷḷaṉ - paḷḷi = 1. paḷḷarkulap peṇ. 2. paḷḷaṉ maṉaivi. 
3. vaṉṉiyarkulam. 4. vaṉṉiyar kulac ciṟṟaracaṉ. rājākkaḷ pōkap  paḷḷikaḷ vantu pukurumā pōlē (tiv. iyaṟ. tiruvirut. 40, viyā. 235).
paḷḷaṉ - paḷḷatti = paḷḷarkulap peṇ.
paḷḷatti - paḷḷacci = paḷḷarkulap peṇ.
paḷḷi - paḷḷicci = vaṉṉiyarkulap peṇ.
paḷ - paḷḷu = paḷḷar vāḻkkaipaṟṟiya nāṭakap paṉuval.
kuṟippu : tuḷaittal eṉpatu, kuḻittal, kuṭaital, tōṇṭutal, tuḷaiyiṭutal, turuvutal eṉṟum; kuttutal, veṭṭutal, kiḷḷutal, kīṟutal, cīvutal, citaittal, aṟuttal, kiḻittal, piḷattal, uṭaittal, pakuttal (pirittal), tuṇṭittal, (tuṇṭupaṇṇutal), poṭittal eṉṟum; kulaittal, kalaittal eṉṟum; viṉaikkum karuvikkum ēṟṟavāṟu palatiṟappaṭum.
māḻaiyum (metal) kallum maramum pōṉṟa vaṉporuḷkaḷ karuvikaḷālum, kaṉikaḷum uṇṭi vakaikaḷum kaḷimaṇṇum pōṉṟa meṉporuḷkaḷ karuviyālum kaiyālum tuḷaikkap paṭum.
cila poruḷkaḷ, takka nilaimai variṉ, nilam veṭippatum kaṉipiḷappatum pōla, tāmākavē tuḷaiyuṇṇum.
kuḻittal kuṭaital pōṉṟaṉa āḻavāṭṭil tuḷaittal; veṭṭiṭutal, aṟuttal pōṉṟaṉa nīḷavāṭṭil tuḷaittal.
kuḻittaluṅ kīṟutaluṅ kuṭaitalum pōṉṟaṉa kuṟaiyat tuḷaittal; turuvutalum veṭṭutalum piḷattalum pōṉṟaṉa niṟaiya allatu muṟṟat tuḷaittal.
muṟṟat tuḷaittalāl pakuttal, pirittal, tuṇittal, poṭittal mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(piri - pirivu. o.nō : L. pars, partis, portionem)
paḷ - paḷḷi = 1. paṭukkai. paḷḷikoṇṭāṉ, paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam. 2. tūkkam. paḷḷipuk katupōlum parappunīrt taṇcērppa (kalit. 121). 3. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam (piṅ.) 4. paṭukkaiyaṟai.  5. paṭukkaiyaṟaiyuḷḷa vīṭu. 6. vīṭu. 7. vīṭu pōṉṟa iṭam, iṭam. colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa vāyiṉum (tol. eḻuttu. piṟap. 18), colliya paḷḷi eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ (mēṟpaṭi. 20). 8. taccaṉ paṇi ceyyum iṭam, paṭṭaṟai, taccaṉ viṉaipaṭu paḷḷi (kaḷavaḻi. 15). 9. aracaṉ vīṭākiya araṇmaṉai. paḷḷikkaṭṭil = ariyaṇai, niṉpaḷḷik kaṭṭiṟ kīḻē (tiv. tiruppā. 22). paḷḷikkaṭṭu = iḷavaraciyiṉ tirumaṇam. (nāñ.). paḷḷit tēvāram = araṇmaṉait teyva vaḻipāṭu (nāñ). 10. teyva vīṭākiya kōvil. kaḻāakkāl paḷḷiyuḷ vaittaṟṟāl (kuṟaḷ. 840). 11. camaṇa puttar kōvil. puttar nōṉpiyar paḷḷiyuḷḷuṟai (tiv. periyati. 2 : 1 : 5). 12. mukamatiyar kōvil, paḷḷivācal. 13. iṟanta aracariṉ niṉaivuk kuṟiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kallaṟai. (S.I.I. III, 24). paḷḷipaṭaip paṭalam (kamparā.). 14. muṉivar tavanilaiyam. mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyu ḷaṭaintatum (maṇimē. 18 : 8). 15. aṟaccālai , ayalāraiyum irappōraiyum uṇpikkum ūr maṭam. 16. maṭattilum kōvililum naṭattappeṟṟa kalvikkūṭam. paḷḷiyi lōti vantataṉ ciṟuvaṉ (tiv. periyati. 2 : 3 : 8). 17. araṇmaṉai allatu kōvil uḷḷa nakaram (piṅ.) 18. tāḻvāṉa kūrai vīṭukaḷuḷḷa iṭaiccēri. kāvum paḷḷiyum (malaipaṭu. 451). 19. iṭaiccēri pōṉṟa ciṟṟūr (piṅ.).
paḷḷam eṉpatu nilamaṭṭattiṉum tāḻvāṉa iṭam. niṟkum nilaiyiṉum paṭukkum nilai tāḻvāyiruppatāl, paṭukkai allatu paṭukkumiṭam paḷḷiyeṉṟum, paṭuttut tūṅkutal paḷḷi koḷḷutal eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
paḷḷimāṭam = aracar tuyilum māṭam. paḷḷimāṭa maṇṭapam (cīvaka. 146). paḷḷiyantulā = tuyilum pallakku. paḷḷiyantulā vēṟuvar (maturaip patiṟ. 19). paḷḷiyōṭam = paṭukkaiyamainta paṭaku.
pakalellām aṅkumiṅkum alaintu tirintu uṇavu tēṭiya mutaṟkāla anākarika māntaṉukkup pōṉṟē, ikkāla nākarika māntaṉukkum, pātukāppāka irāt taṅki iṉitāka uṟaṅkutaṟku vīṭu iṉṟiyamaiyātatāyiruttal kāṇka. paḷḷi koḷḷa utavāta amaippu uṇmaiyāṉa vīṭākātu.
pala vīṭukaḷ cērntatē ūrātaliṉ, vīṭṭup peyar ciṉaiyāku peyarāka ūraiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu:
kuṭi = vīṭu, ūr (kāraikkuṭi, maṉṉārkuṭi).
il = vīṭu, ūr (aḻumpil, kiṭaṅkil).
paḷḷi = vīṭu, ūr (tirukkāṭṭuppaḷḷi, tiruccirāppaḷḷi).
nakar = veṇcutai tīṟṟiya kārai vīṭu allatu māḷikai, attakaiya vīṭuḷḷa ūr.
ma. paḷḷi, te. palliya, palle, ka. haḷḷi, va. palli.
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻakara mutaliyil (Lexicon), paḷḷi eṉṉum tūya paḻanteṉcol palli eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripākak kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu maruṭkaikkuriya ceytiyākum.
paḷḷi - pāḷi - pāḻi = 1. makkaḷ tuyiliṭam. perumpāḻi cūḻnta viṭattaravai (tiv. iyaṟ. 1 : 20). 2. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam (piṅ.). 3. kōyil. aiyaṉ pāḻiyil āṉai pōrkkurittām aṉṟu (īṭu, 1 : 1 : 5). 4. muṉivar uṟaiviṭam (piṅ.). 5. iṭam. vāṉavar kōṉ pāḻi (tiv. iyaṟ. 2 : 13). 6. marutanilattūr (cūṭā.). 7. nakaram (piṅ.). o.nō : Gk. polis, city. 8. ciṟukuḷam. pūmi nāṭṭār.... ciṟukuḷattaip pāḻi eṉṟum.... vaḻaṅkupa (tol. col. 400, nac.urai).  x.neh : OE., MLG. pol, E. pool. 9. elivaḷai, elippāḻi (u.va.). 10. kukai (tivā.).
paḷ - paḷḷai = 1. kuḷḷam. 2. kuḷḷamāṉa paḷḷaiyāṭu. pa.ka. paṭṭe (dd).
paḷḷai - paḷḷaiyaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ, kuṟukip paruttavaṉ. (yāḻ. aka.).
paḷḷaiyaṉ - paḷḷacci = kuḷḷi. 
paḷḷaiyam = 1. paḷḷamāṉa uṇkalam (W.). 2. ciṟuteyvap paṭaiyal. uyarattai nōkkak kuḷḷam paḷḷamāyiruttal kāṇka. paḷḷam - tāḻmaṭṭam.
paḷ - paḷku, paḷkutal = patuṅkutal. paḷki nōkkupu payirttu naṭantāṉ (cūḷā. cuyam. 31).
paḷ - paṇ = 1. nīrnilai. paṇkulavat tālamicai naṭittu (kuṟṟā. tala. tirunatic. 12). 2. nīrnilai pōṉṟa vayal, vayaliṟ ceyyum vēlai, toḻil.
paṇ - paṇṇai1 = vilaṅku tuyiliṭam (piṅ.)
paṇṇai2 = 1. marutanilam. vaḷanīrp paṇṇaiyum vāviyum (cilap. 11 : 13). 2. vayal (piṅ.) paṇṇaicūḻn tilakun tirumuṉaip paḻaiya nāṭu (pārata. pāyi. 6). veḷḷanīrp paṇṇaiyum virinī rēriyum (cilap. 13 : 192). 3. nīrnilai (piṅ.). 4. marattiṟku aṭiyil nīr pāyccumāṟu cuṟṟivarat tōṇṭi vaikkum paḷḷam. marattiṟkup paṇṇai piṭikka vēṇṭum (u.va.). 5. nilakkiḻārē ceyyum payirttoḻil (C.G.). 6. vārakkuṭi. 7. neṟkuttumāṟu naṭuviṟ paḷḷañceytu nilattiṟ patikkappaṭṭa vaṭṭakkal. 8. pallāṅkuḻi viḷaiyāṭṭiṟku nilattil tōṇṭappaṭum kuḻi.
ka. paṇṇeya.
paṇṇaiyār = perunilakkiḻār. paṇṇaiyāḷ = vārak kuṭiyāḷ.
paṇṇaiyāḷi - paṇṇaiyāṭi - paṇṇāṭi = vārakkuṭi vaittu payir ceyyum perunilakkiḻār.
paṇṇai - paṇai1 = 1. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam (piṅ.). 2. yāṉai kutiraikaḷ taṅkumiṭam. paṇainilai muṉaiik kaḷiṟupaṭin tuṇṭeṉa (puṟam. 23), paṇaiyamai pāymāṉ (kalit. 57).
paṇai2 = 1. marutanilam. perun taṇpaṇai pāḻāka (puṟam. 16). 2. vayal. irumpaṇai tiraṅka (patiṟ. 43.12). 3. nīrnilai (piṅ.).
paṇ - paṇi. paṇital = 1. tāḻtal. paṇiyiya rattainiṉ kuṭaiyē (puṟam. 6), 2. cerukkiṉṟiyaṭaṅkutal. ellārkku naṉṟām paṇital (kuṟaḷ. 125). 3. iṟaṅkutal (W.). 4. tāḻvātal. 5. kuṟaital. 6. eḷimaiyātal. 7. vaṇaṅkutal. uṭaiyāṉ kaḻal paṇintilai (tiruvā. 4 : 35). paṇintavaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ (W.).
ma. paṇiyuka.
paṇittal = 1. tāḻttutal. vēntaṉ vēṟṟavarp paṇippa (pu.ve.4 : 9, koḷu). 2. kuṟaittal (W.). 3. mitittal, nantu māmaiyum paṇittu (cīvaka. 2109). paṇiviṭai = toṇṭu, kuṟṟēval. tiruppaṇi = teyvat toṇṭu.
paṇi - paṭi. paṭital = 1. aṭiyil taṅkutal. vaṇṭal paṭintirukkiṟatu (u.va.). 2. taṅkutal. paṟavai paṭivaṉa vīḻa (neṭunal. 10). 3. vayamātal. aṭiyāta māṭu paṭiyātu (paḻa.). 4. kīḻppaṭital. periyōrkkup paṭintu naṭattal vēṇṭum (u.va.). 5. kuḷittal. taṭaṅkaṭaliṟ paṭivām (tiruvāca. 38 : 9). 6. amuṅkutal. eṇṇey tēyttuc cīviṉāl mayir paṭiyum (u.va.). 7. taṇital, veḷḷam paṭintatu (u.va.). 8. kīḻē viḻuntu vaṇaṅkutal. cirantalattuṟap paṭintu (upatēcakā. civattu. 344).
paṇi ceyvōṉ = 1. vēlaikkāraṉ. 2. viḻākkālattil tāraiyum, iḻavunāḷil caṅkum ūtupavaṉ. kīḻppaṭital = coṉṉapaṭi ceytu paṇivāy naṭattal.
paṇi - pāṇi, pāṇittal = 1. kālat tāḻttal. pāṇinī niṉcūḷ (paripā. 8 : 56). 2. kāttiruttal. pāṇiyē meṉṟār (kalit. 102). 3. piṉvāṅkutal. camariṟ pāṇiyāṉ (kantapu. mūvāyirar. 59).
pāṇi = paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil taṅkum allatu paḷḷam nōkki ōṭum nīr. viṇṇiyal pāṇiyaṉ (patiṉo. poṉvaṉ. 30).
ka. paṇi, marā. pāṇi, i pāṇī.
nīraik kuṟikkum pāṇi eṉṉuñcol vaṭamoḻiyil illai. nīram eṉṉum tamiḻccol vaṭamoḻiyil nīra eṉṟu kaṭaik kuṟaintu vaḻaṅkumpōtu, pāṇi (nīr) eṉṉum col intiyil pāṇī eṉṟu tirintu vaḻaṅkuvatu iyaṟkaikku māṟaṉṟu.
paṇ - paṇṇal = 1. vayalait tiruttutal. 2. payir ceytal. paṇṇiya payiril puṇṇiyan teriyum (paḻa.). 3. vēlai ceytal. 4. cevvaiyā yamaittal. 5. aḻakupaṭuttutal. 6. aṇiya mākkutal (āyattañ ceytal). 7. icaiyeḻuvum eṇvakaiyuḷ oṉṟu. paṇṇal.... eṇvakaiyā licaiyeḻīi (cilap. 7 : 5-8).
paṇ - paṇṇu. paṇṇutal = 1. payir ceytal. 2. vēlai ceytal. 3. ceytal umpark kiṭantuṇṇap paṇṇappaṭum (nālaṭi. 37). 4. cevvaippaṭuttutal. 5. aḻakupaṭuttutal. paṭṭamo ṭilaṅkap paṇṇi (cūḷā. kalyā. 14). 6. aṇiyamākkutal. pūnutal yāṉaiyoṭu puṉaitēr paṇṇavum (puṟam. 12). 7. yāḻ narampil alaku (curuti) amaittal. 8. paṇṇiṟkēṟpa yāḻ narampamaittal. marutam paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ (malaipaṭu. 534). 9. camaittal. pālu mitavaiyum paṇṇātu peṟukuvir (malaipaṭu. 417). 10. uṇṭākkutal. pāṉai caṭṭi paṇṇukiṟavaṉ kuyavaṉ. (u.va.).
māntaṉ mutaṉmutaṟ kaṟṟukkoṇṭa kaittoḻila uṇavu viḷaikkum payirttoḻilātalāl paṇṇutal eṉṉum paṇṇai viṉaiyaik kuṟitta collē potuviṉaiyaik kuṟikkuñ collāyiṟṟu.
o. nō : kai = kaittoḻil, payirttoḻil.
iravār irappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
kaiceytūṇ mālai yavar.  (kuṟaḷ. 1035)
paṇ - paṇi = 1. kaittoḻil. paṇikkaḷari = toḻiṟcālai, paṭṭaṟai, kollaṉatu paṇikkaḷariyākiya kuṟiya koṭṭiliṭat taṉavāyiṉa (puṟam. 95, urai). 2. aḻaku paṇṇum aṇikalam (piṅ.). paṇiyelām paṇiya tāki (kantapu. tatīciyut. 106). 
3. vēlaippāṭu. paṇipaḻut tamainta pūṇ (kamparā. ilaṅkai kē. 12). 4. piḻaipput toḻil.
ma. paṇi. paṇittaṭṭār = poṟkollar. paṇittaṭṭār paṇipaṇṇu miṭaṅkaḷil (cilap. 6 : 135, urai).
paṇi - paṇikku = viṉaittiṟamai.
paṇikkuc celuttutal = vēlaiyāḷkaḷiṉ vēlaiyai mēṟpārppavaṉ (W.).
paṇikkuc collutal = vēlai ceyyum muṟaiyaik kāṭṭutal (W.). 2. piṟar vēlaiyiṟ kuṟṟaṅkūṟutal. kaṭṭat teriyā viṭṭālum kaṭṭiṉa vīṭṭiṟkup paṇikkuc colvāṉ. (u.va.).
paṇikku - paṇikkam = viṉaittiruttam, toḻiṟṟiṟamai (W.).
paṇikkam - paṇikkaṉ = 1. viṉaittiṟavōṉ. 2. āciriyaṉ (piṅ.). 3. paṭaikkalam payiṟṟuvōṉ. paṇpuṭaiyāṉoru paṇikkaṉ tōṉṟi (tiruvālavā. 35 : 1). 4. kūttup payiṟṟuvōṉ, āṭalāciriyaṉ. 5. talaimaik koṟṟaṉ (W.). 6. yāṉaip pākaṉ (W.). 7. nañcu maruttuvaṉ.
kaiyaik kuṟikkum pāṇi eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkiṉum, ataṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭappaṭum paṇ eṉṉuñ colliṟku, paṇṭamāṟṟutal, paṇaiyam vaittal, pēram pēcutal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷē uḷa. avai poruntu vaṉavalla.
entap poruḷaiyum toḻilaiyuñ ceytaṟkuk kai iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṟuppātalāl, ceytalaik kuṟikkum colliṉiṉṟu kaippeyar amaivatē iyaṟkaikkup poruttamām. paṇ (paṇṇu) - pāṇ - pāṇi = (paṇṇi).
taṇṭapāṇi = taṇṭam (taṇṭu + am) ēntiya kaiyaṉ, murukaṉ. taṇṭam eṉṉum collum tavaṟāka vaṭacolleṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
o.nō : cey - te. cēyi (c), ka. key. keyyi, ta. kai. cey - key - kai.
kai yeṉṉum peyar, cey eṉṉum vaṭivil kumarināṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
karu - va. kru - kara = kai. karuttal ceytal. ivviṉaiccol kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiṟku muṉpē vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. karumam, karuvi, karaṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷai nōkkuka.
paṇṇutal eṉṉuñ col, alaku, paṇ eṉṉum iru vakaiyil icaiyamaittalaik kuṟikka āḷappaṭṭataṉāl, paṇ eṉṉuñ col icai vakaiyaiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
paṇṇalāvatu:
valakkaip peruviral kuralkoḷac ciṟuviral
vilakkiṉ ṟiḷivaḻi kēṭṭum
iṇaivaḻi yārām tiṇaikoḷa muṭippatu
viḷaipparu marapiṟ paṇṇa lākum  (cilap. 7 : ka, paḻaṅkuṟippurai).
paṇṇal - pāṭa niṉainta paṇṇukku iṇai kiḷai pakai naṭpāṉa narampukaḷ peyarun taṉmai māttirai yaṟintu vīkkutal (cīvaka. 657, nac. urai).
o. nō : cey - ceyyuḷ.
vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ  (naṉ.268).
ceyyuḷ icaippāviṟku iṉamāṉatē. pāṭṭu, pāṇi, tēvapāṇi, peruntēvapāṇi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, ceyyuṭkum icaippāviṟkum potuvāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
paṇ = 1. icaivakai. paṇṇeṉṉām pāṭaṟ kiyai piṉṟēl  (kuṟaḷ. 573). 2. eḻumural (cura). icaivakai. paṇṇun tiṟaṉum (peruṅ. vattava. 3 : 56). niṟainta narampu nikaḻum paṇṇeṉal (piṅ. 6 : 324). 3. icaippāṭṭu (piṅ.). 4. narappuk karuvikaḷāṉa yāḻvakaikaḷ. paṇkeḻu melviralāl (cīvaka. 220).
paṇ - pāṇ = pāṭṭu. pūṇiṉāṉṟaṉ pāṇvalaic ceṉṟu paṭṭāḷ (cīvaka. 2040).
pāṇ - pāṇaṉ = icaiyum icaittamiḻum vaḷartta paṇṭait tamiḻa vakuppāṉ. kūttarum pāṇarum (tol. puṟat. 36).
pāṇaṉ - pāṇāṉ = icait toḻiliḻantu taiyal vēlai ceyyum tamiḻa vakuppāṉ.
pāṇaṉ - pāṇ = pāṇar kulam.
pāṇ - pāṇ = pāṇar kulam.

kālṭuvelār tam tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṉ iṟutip pakutiyil, colliyal uṟavukaḷ (Glossarial Affinities) eṉṉum talaippiṉ kīḻ,
“bhaj, to share.
bhajga, a portion. I am doubtful whether to regard these words as derived from the Tamil pag-u, to divide, to share, or to suppose both the Sanskrit and the Tamil to be derived from a common and earlier source. Probably the former supposition is in this case the more correct. At all events the Tamil - Malayalam pag-u is a pase, underived Drividian root. A noun former from it, signifying a share, is pang-u (ng for g, as is after the case); and a collateral root is pag-ir, meaning also to share. The Sanskrit word pangu means lame, and is altogether unconnected with the Tamil one. Other derived nouns are pagal, a division daylight; pal (=pagal), a portion; and padi (pagudi). half” - (Dravidian Comparative Grammar, p. 573). eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.

tirukkōvilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum paṇṇaiyāriṭattum vēlai ceyvārkkellām, campaḷamāka nellai aḷantu koṭuppatē paṇṭai nāḷ vaḻakkam. atu paṭiyaḷattal eṉṟu collappaṭum. ataṉāl, vēlaiyilamartti vāḻkkaikku vēṇṭum campaḷam koṭuppārk kellām, paṭiyaḷappār eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ellā vuyirkaṭkum uṇavaip pakuttaḷippavaṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttil iṟaivaṉum paṭiyaḷappāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. namakkup paṭiyaḷappār nāriyōr pākar (taṉippā. 1, 121 : 5).
ellāmvalla oru taṉi iṟaivaṉukkuriya pakavaṉ eṉṉum peyar, ciṟuteyvaṅkaṭkum ciṟanta makkaṭkum nāḷaṭaivil vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar tam tirukkuṟaṭ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttil, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷaik kuṟikka ātipakavaṉ eṉṟu aṭai koṭuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. āti eṉpatu mutal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum vaṭanāṭṭuc col. atu muntu tiraviṭamākiya pirākirutac collāyirukkalām. ātipakavaṉ eṉṟatu, ātiyeṉṉum pulaicciyai maṇanta oru pirāmaṇaṉai eṉiṉ, āti caṅkarāccāriyār eṉpatum ātiyeṉṉum pulaicciyai maṇanta caṅkarāccāri yārai eṉṟa kūṟal vēṇṭum. atu poruḷaṉmai aṟika.
kārkālam, muracukaṭṭil, kōvūrkiḻār eṉpaṉa pōla, ātipakavaṉ eṉpatum valimikātu vaḻaṅkiya orucila puṇarc coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. ataṉāl, āti yeṉpatu vaṭacol (camaṟkirutac col) eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatillai. mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilum āti eṉṉuñ colliṉmaiyiṉ, atu vaṭacollākātu.

tiripāki (muppāki) = mūveḻuttukaḷ koṇṭa oru col, ataṉ mutaleḻuttaiyum iṟuti yeḻuttaiyuñ cērkka maṟṟoru collākiyum, iṭaiyeḻuttaiyum kaṭai yeḻuttaiyum cērkka maṟṟumoru collākiyum vantu, vevvēṟu poruḷ tarumāṟu pāṭappaṭum collaṇi (taṇṭi).
itu muppākiyeṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. tiri eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi eṇṇup peyar, muppuriyākat tirikkappaṭum nūl allatu kayiṟu eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyirukkalām. ēka eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi mutaleṇṇup peyar, okka eṉṉum teluṅkuc colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
okka - ekka - ēka.
vaṭamoḻiyil ekarak kuṟiliṉmaiyāl, ekka eṉpatu ēka (ēkka) eṉa nīṇṭatu.

iṅṅaṉam iruvēṟu vakaiyil vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutap paṭuvatāl, patti eṉpatu iruvēṟu collō eṉṟu aiyuṟaṟka. pakti eṉṉum colliṟkum pākam eṉṉum colliṟkum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paj (bhaj) eṉṉum orē mūlaṅkāṭṭuvataiyum, paku eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟē tirintuḷḷa pakkam eṉṉum collai avar pakṣa eṉṟu tirippataiyum, nōkkuka.
iṅṅaṉam, orē mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷa palvēṟu tiri coṟkaḷai, eḻuttumāṟṟi vevvēṟu mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirinta coṟkaḷākak kāṭṭuvatu, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaḻakkam.
muttam eṉṉum teṉcollai mukta eṉṟum tirittatu pōṉṟē, patti eṉṟa teṉcollaiyum pakti eṉṟu tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.

4. poruḷkaḷ vaittaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu aṭiyiṟ parappiya  ōlai allatu vaikkōl. 5. teyvaṅkaṭkup paṭaikkum paṭaiyal (nāñ). 6. ciṟṟūrt teyvaṅkaṭkuc ceyyum orunāḷ viḻā.

puḻaṅku - puḻakkam = 1. kaiyāṭci, puḻakkattiṟkuk kalaṅkaḷillai. 2. paṇa naṭamāṭṭam. iv vāṇṭu paṇappuḻakkam mikak kuṟaintu viṭṭatu (u.va.).
paḻaṅku - vaḻaṅku. vaḻaṅkutal = (ce. ku. vi). 1. toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭaipeṟṟu varutal. vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum (tol. kuṟṟi. 78). 2. payiṟci peṟutal (W.). 3. nilai peṟutal. vāṉiṉ ṟulakam vaḻaṅki varutalāṉ (kuṟaḷ. 11). 
4. iyaṅkutal. vaḷivaḻaṅkum mallaṉmā ñālam kari (kuṟaḷ. 245). munnīr vaḻaṅkum nāvāy (puṟam. 13). 5. ulāvutal. cilampil vaḻaṅka lāṉāp puli (kalit. 52). 6. naṭamāṭutal. pēy makaḷ vaḻaṅkum perumpāḻākum (patiṟ. 22 : 37). vaḻaṅkāp poḻutu nī kaṉṟumēyp pāypōl (kalit. 112). 7. acaintāṭutal. maḻakaḷiṟu kantu cērpu.... vaḻaṅka (puṟam. 22). 8. ēṟṟatātal. vāykku vaḻaṅkāta kaṟi (W.).
(ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. kaiyāḷutal. tamiḻp pulavar taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum (u.va.). 2. collutal, yāṉō tēṟēṉ avar poyvaḻaṅ kalarē (kuṟun. 21). 3. koṭuttal. vaḻaṅkat tavāap peruvaḷa ṉeyti (perum pāṇ. 26). 4. viṭuttal cāpa nōṉñāṇ vaṭukkoḷa vaḻaṅka vum (puṟam. 14). 5. pakirntaḷittal.
ka. paḻaku (g).
vaḻaṅku - vaḻakku (pi.vi.). vaḻakkutal = pōkkutal. vaḻakkumāṟu koṇṭu (kalit. 101).
vaḻaṅku - vaḻakku (pe.) = 1. iyaṅkukai. vaiyai yaṉṉa vaḻakkuṭai vāyil (maturaik. 159). vaḻakkoḻiyā vāyil (pu. ve. 10, mullaip. 4). 2. ulaka vaḻakku ceyyuḷ vaḻakku eṉṉum iruvakai vaḻakkiṭam. vaḻakkeṉap paṭuva tuyarntōr mēṟṟē (tol. marapu. 93). 2. ulaka vaḻakku. vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ (tol. paṉam. ci. pā.). 3. iyalpu vaḻakku takuti vaḻakku eṉṉum iruvakaic col vaḻakkukaḷ.
ilakkaṇa muṭaiya tilakkaṇap pōli
marūuveṉ ṟākum mūvakai yiyalpum
iṭakka raṭakkal maṅkalam kuḻūukkuṟi
eṉumut takutiyō ṭāṟām vaḻakkiyal  (naṉ. 207).
4. paḻakkavoḻukkam. vāymaiyuṭaiyār vaḻakku (tirikaṭu. 37). 5. neṟi. aṉpuṟ ṟamarnta vaḻakkeṉpa (kuṟaḷ. 75). 6. nayaṉmai (nīti). vaḻakkaṉṟu kaṇṭāy (tiv. iyaṟ. 2 : 19). 7. caccaravu, urimaip piṇakku. 8. caccaravupaṟṟiya muṟaiyīṭu. vaḻakkurai kātai (cilap. 20). 9. koṭai. uṭaiyāṉ vaḻakkiṉitu (iṉi. nāṟ. 3).

vaḻaṅku - vaḻakkam = 1. paḻakkam. 2. nāḷtoṟum ceyvatu. avar kālai eḻuntavuṭaṉ kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkuvatu vaḻakkam (u.va.).  3. īkai. tirukkai vaḻakkam (kampaṉ). 4. paṭaikkalattaik kaiyāḷutal.
ka. paḻake (b).

pāḻ = 1. aḻivu. narakakkuḻi palavāyiṉa pāḻpaṭṭatu (caṭakōparan. 5). 2. keṭuti (W.). 3. vaṟaṭpāḻ, veḷḷap pāḻ, kuṭippāḻ eṉṉum muppāḻāṉa iḻappu. 4. iḻivu (W.).
