2. aruḷ. (W.).
paḷ - paṇṭu = paḻam. bj., ka. paṇ.
paṇṭu (paḻam) eṉṉum col iṟṟait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkā viṭiṉum, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. paḷ eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu paṇṭu eṉṉum collē mutalil tōṉṟiyirukkum. ḻakara vuyirmey ṇakara vuyirmeyyākat tiriyiṉum ḻakaramey taṉikkuṟilaṭuttu vaḻaṅkāmaiyiṉ piṟaveḻuttāyt tirivatillai. paḻamai, paḻi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ paṇṭu eṉṉum vaṭivuṅ koṇṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, paḻam eṉṉum collum av vaṭivukoṇṭu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal iyalpē.
pulavar perumitam
cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷiyiṭamiruntu uṟaiyūr pukunta iḷantattaṉ eṉṉum pulavaṉai, oṟṟu vantāṉeṉṟu kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi kollap pukunta viṭattu, kōvūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar,
vaḷḷiyōrp paṭarntu puḷḷiṟ pōki
neṭiya veṉṉātu curampala kaṭantu
vaṭiyā nāviṉ vallāṅkup pāṭip
peṟṟatu makiḻntu cuṟṟa marutti
ōmpā tuṇṭu kūmpātu vīci
varicaikku varuntumip paricil vāḻkkai
piṟarkkut tītaṟin taṉṟō viṉṟē, tiṟappaṭa
naṇṇār nāṇa aṇṇān tēki
āṅkiṉi toḻuki ṉalla tōṅkupukaḻ
maṇṇāḷ celva meytiya
nummō raṉṉa cemmalu muṭaittē  (puṟam. 47)
eṉṟu pāṭi, appulavaṉai uyyak koṇṭār.
mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ muraṇpaṭṭa pōtu,
maṉṉavaṉum nīyō vaḷanāṭum uṉṉatō
uṉṉaiyaṟin tōtamiḻai yōtiṉēṉ - eṉṉai
viraintēṟṟuk koḷḷāta vēntuṇṭō vuṇṭō
kuraṅkēṟṟuk koḷḷāta kompu.
eṉṟu kampar pāṭip pirintār. ellā nāṭṭilum pulavar ip paṭittāṉiruppar. atu maṇṭaik kaṉam aṉṟu. pulavar vaṟumaiyaip payaṉpaṭutti avarai yaṭakkiyāḷat tuṇivatu tāṉ celvaṉiṉ allatu amaiccaṉiṉ maṇṭaik kaṉam. cila vaṇikap pulavar vaṇaṅkip paṇivatu pōl ellārum paṇiyār.
pulippaṟṟāli
pulippaṟṟāliyeṉpatu kuṟiñci nilac ciṟār kaḻuttilum peṇṭir kaḻuttilum aṇiyappeṟum pulippal mālai.  (ta.ti.82).
puliyum āṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu
pala piḷḷaikaḷ vaṭṭamāyk kaikōttu niṟka, oru piḷḷai uḷḷum maṟṟoru piḷḷai veḷiyum niṟpar. uḷniṟkum piḷḷai āṭākavum, veḷi niṟkum piḷḷai puliyākavum, pāvikkappeṟuvar. pulikkum vaṭṭamāy niṟkum piḷḷaikaṭkum piṉvarumāṟu uraiyāṭṭu nikaḻum:
puli : caṅkili puṅkili katavait tiṟa.
piḷḷaikaḷ : nāṉ māṭṭēṉ vēṅkaippuli.
puli : varalāmā? varakkūṭātā?
piḷḷaikaḷ : varakkūṭātu.
piḷḷaikaḷ vaḻimaṟuttapiṉ, puli yārēṉum iru piḷḷai kaṭkiṭaiyil nuḻaiyap pārkkum. piḷḷaikaḷ iṭam viṭuvatillai. palamuṟai aṅkumiṅkum cuṟṟip pārtta piṉ, puli tiṭumeṉṟu ōriṭattil valintu pukum. uṭaṉē āṭu veḷiyē viṭappeṟum. puli āṭṭaip piṭikka veḷiyēṟum. āṭu mīṇṭum uḷḷē viṭappeṟum. iṅṅaṉam māṟimāṟi iraṇṭorumuṟai nikaḻntapiṉ, iṟutiyil puli āṭṭaip piṭittukkoḷḷum.
iv viḷaiyāṭṭiṉ tōṟṟam veḷippaṭai. vaṭṭamāy niṟkum piḷḷaikaḷ āṭṭut toḻuvattai nikarppar.
puḷḷikuttal
puḷḷikkāraṉ, mukattil kariyaip pūcutal karikkōṭiṭutal mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷ, ūrkku māṟāṉa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷai ūrār cempuḷḷi karumpuḷḷi kuttiyum, mukattil kariyaip pūciyum karikkōṭiṭṭum avamāṉappaṭuttiyatai niṉaivuṟuttum.  (col. 25).
puṟaṅkūṟṟu vakai
puṟaṇi - mukattil pukaḻntum puṟattil paḻittum pēcutal;
kuṇṭuṇi - palar kūṭi oruvaraip paḻittu nakaiyāṭutal; 
kuṟaḷai - oruvarkku māṟāka iṉṉoruvariṭam puṟaṅkūṟik kalaka mūṭṭutal;
kōḷ - mēlatikāriyiṭam oruvaraip paṟṟit tītākac collutal; 
ōmal - oruvaraip paḻittu ūrār kūṭip pēcum pēccu;
uvalai - oruvariṉ ākkam poṟātu piṟar kūṟum kūṟṟu; 
ōmal - uvalai iraṇṭum paṭarkkaiyilaṉṟi muṉṉilaiyilum nikaḻum; āyiṉum, puṟaṅkūṟṟiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(col. 60).
puṟaṅkūṟutal
puṟam, mutuku allatu piṉpakkam. oruvar illāta iṭam avarukkut teriyāta mutukuppakkam pōṉṟiruttalāl puṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. puṟattiṟ kūṟuvatu puṟaṅ kūṟutal. atu puṟam eṉavum paṭum.  (ti.ma. 118).
puṟamaṇam
oru kulap pirivār tam piriviṟkuḷ maṇavātu vēṟoru pirivil maṇattal.  (ta.ti. 12).
puṟanāṉūṟum moḻiyum
1. muṉṉurai
tamiḻaiyum ataṉ ilakkiyattaiyum cevvaiyāyaṟiya vēṇṭiṉ, tamiḻiṉ varalāṟṟaṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭap paṭum. im muṟai ēṉai moḻikaṭkum ēṟkum. paḻaiya āṅkila ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷillām muṉṉuraiyāka āṅkilac carittiram kūṟappaṭutalai nōkkuka. 
2. tamiḻiṉ kālap pakuti
tamiḻiṉ kālam kīḻkkāṇumāṟu mupperum pakutiyiṉatākum.
(1)	mutaṟkālam ki.mu. cumār 10,000 - 3,000.
(2)	iṭaikkālam allatu kaḻakakkālam - ki.mu. cumār 3,000 - 
ki.pi. 200.
(3)	taṟkālam - ki.pi. 200 mutal.
ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum pala ciṟu pakutikaḷākap pāku paṭṭuḷḷatu. kaḻakak kālam (1) talaik kaḻakakkālam, (2) iṭaik kaḻakakkālam; (3) kaṭaik kaḻakakkālam eṉa muppakutippaṭum. ivaṟṟukku iṭaiyiṭai iṭaiyīṭu miruntatu. iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷuraippaṭi, mukkaḻakamum nilaittirunta kālam muṟaiyē nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟi yāṇṭum mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟi yāṇṭum āyirat teṇṇūṟṟaim patiṟṟu yāṇṭumāka mottam oṉpatiṉāyirattut toḷā yirattut toṇṇūṟi yāṇṭākum. kumarināṭṭiṉ toṉmutu paḻaimaiyai nōkkuvārkkuk kaḻakakkāla neṭumaiyaip paṟṟiya aiyuṟavum mayakkamum aṟavē aṟumeṉpatu tiṇṇam.
puṟanāṉūṟṟil oru cila (2, 358, 362, 366) ceyyuḷkaḷait tavira, piṟavaṟṟaiyellām pāṭiṉārum avarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭārum kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiṉarātaliṉ, puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ kālam kaṭaikkaḻakak kālamākum (ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu).
3. nūlvakai
talaik kaḻakattārum iṭaik kaḻakattārum iyaṟṟiṉa nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa pala tuṟait taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷellām iṟantu paṭṭaṉa. itupōtu kaḻaka nūlkaḷeṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭupavai kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷē. avaṟṟuḷḷum, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ katai nūlkaḷ kalai nūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉa cērkkappaṭavillai. patiṉeṉ mēṟkaṇakkeṉṟum patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkeṉṟum irupāṟ paṭṭuk kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷeṉṟu vaḻaṅkum muppattāṟum, perumpālum, kātal vāḻkkai, maṟam, pōr, araca vāḻttu, nalloḻukkam. varalāṟṟut tuṇukku ākiya āṟuporuḷ paṟṟiyavē. mēṟkaṇakkil, eṭṭu nūlkaḷē tokai nūlkaḷeṉap pirittuk kūṟappaṭiṉum, uṇmaiyil ellām tokai nūlē. eṭṭuttokai pōṉṟē pattup pāṭṭum pala pulavarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭatākum. pattuppāṭṭiluḷḷa pāṭṭukaḷellām mika nīṇṭiruttalālēyē vēṟākat tokukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṭiyiṉ ciṟappē pāṭṭeṉap paṭumē eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭikaḷ koṇṭu mika nīṇṭirunta taṉic ceyyuḷai (perumpālum akavalai)p pāṭṭeṉṟu ciṟappāka muṉṉōr vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkiṉuḷḷum, nālaṭiyār tokainūlām; ēṉaiyavai taṉi nūlkaḷ. kaṭaik kaḻakattār pāṭiya pāṭṭukaḷi lum nūlkaḷilum palavaṟṟait tokuttapōtu, pattuppāṭṭum eṭṭut tokaiyumākiya patiṉeṭṭuk koppākak kīḻk kaṇakkaiyum patiṉeṭṭākat tokut tirukkiṉṟaṉar. it tokai irupuṟavoppup (Symmetry) paṟṟiyatu.
aḵteṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, puṟanāṉūṟu oru tokainūl allatu taṉippāṭaṟṟiraṭṭē. puṟapporuḷaip paṟṟiya nāṉūṟu ceyyuḷkaḷaik koṇṭatu puṟanāṉūṟu. iṉṉā nāṟpatu iṉiyavai nāṟpatu mutaliya nāṟpatu pākkaḷaik koṇṭa paṉuvalkaḷaiyum, naṟṟiṇai nāṉūṟu, kuṟuntokai nāṉūṟu, nālaṭi nāṉūṟu, paḻamoḻi nāṉūṟu mutaliya nāṉūṟu pākkaḷaik koṇṭa paṉuvalkaḷaiyum, nālāyirakkōvai nālāyirat tivyappirapantam (teyvappaṉuval) mutaliya nālāyiram pākkaḷaik koṇṭa paṉuvalkaḷaiyum nōkkumiṭattu, nāleṉṉum eṇ ētōvoru kāraṇampaṟṟi nūṟceyyuṭ ṭokaikkuc ciṟantatākak koḷḷappaṭṭa teṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. atu nālvakaip pāvō nālvakaik kulamō paṟṟi yirukkalām.
4. naṭai
paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷellām taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum potuk koḷkaikkēṟpa, puṟanāṉūṟum taṉit tamiḻē. āṅkāṅku iraṇṭoru vaṭacoṟkaḷ aruki vantiruppatiṉāl, puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṉ taṉittamiḻt taṉmai kuṉṟiviṭātu. in nūlil vantirukkum vaṭacoṟkaḷellām irupatu allatu irupat taintē. ivaṟṟuḷḷum oru cila teṉcollō vaṭa collō eṉa aiyuṟaṟkuriyaṉa.
iraṇṭoru vaṭacoṟkaḷ iṅkumaṅkumāka aruki vantiruppatu koṇṭu. puṟanāṉūṟu muḻumaiyuṅ kalapput tamiḻeṉpārāyiṉ, piṟamoḻic col kalavāta moḻiyē ulakattilillai yeṉṟum, āriyattilum cuṭṭum viṉāvum mūviṭap peyarum pōṉṟa aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷellām vērnilaiyil tamiḻāyiruttaliṉ, tamiḻ taṉittu vaḻaṅkiṉum āriyam taṉittu vaḻaṅkal kūṭāmaiyeṉṟum kūṟi viṭukka.
puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷkaḷiṟ cila citaintuṅ kuṟaintu miruppatāl, in nūṟ ceyyuḷkaḷiṉ aṭicciṟumai perumai yum coṟṟokaiyum aṟivataṟkillai. āyiṉum, itu pōtuḷḷapaṭi aṭiyuñ collum eṇṇip pārppiṉ, oru ceyyuḷiṉ carācari aṭittokai patiṉeṭṭeṉpatum, ōr aṭiyiṉ carācaric coṟṟokai āṟeṉpatum teriya varum. ākavē, oru ceyyuḷiṉ carācaric coṟṟokai ēṟattāḻa nūṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
5. puṟanāṉūṟṟāl viḷakkappaṭum colliyal (Etymological) neṟimuṟaikaḷum uṇmaikaḷum
1.	cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷ mutaṟkālattil uyir mutala.
	e-ṭu :	ēm - ēmam - cēmam = kāval, pātukāval (1, 3). cēmavaccu (102, cēmam - kṣēma (va.). o.nō : ēṇ - cēṇ.
2.	cuṭṭuviṉāc coṟkaḷ mutaṟkālattil neṭiṉ mutala.
	e-ṭu :	āṅku = acai (2), appaṭi (24) : atupōla (35, 106).
	īṅkaṉam = ivvāṟu (208), ītu (208).
	ūṅku = muṉ (79, 88, 141).
	ēṉōr = yāvar (343), yāṅkaṉam = evvāṟu (8, 49).
	yāṅku = eppaṭi (191, 245).
3.	cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ mutaṟkālattil peyarc collāyu miruntaṉa. e-ṭu : attaka = ataṟkuttaka (10).
4.	olikkuṟippallāc coṟkaḷellām mutaṟkālattil ōracaiya.
	e-ṭu :	ār = uṇ (24), ārakkāl (283), ātti (338), āl = āṭu (116).
	uṇ (160), uḷ = ūkkam (396), ūm = ūmai (28) ūr (83).
5.	mutaṟkālattil oru vaṭivē pala colvakaikkup potuvāyiruntatu.
e-ṭu : cey = ceykai (199), pari = celavu (14, 146), aṟivārā = aṟiyavārā (92), nakutakkaṉar = cirikkattakkār (72), iṉaikūu = varuntik kūppiṭum kūppīṭu (44), iṉai yum kūvu eṉka. vaṇaṅkiṟai = vaḷainta muṉkai (32).
6.	ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē (tol.).
e-ṭu :	
	cuḷ - cuṭu - cuṭar, 
	kaḷ - kaṇ,
	eyyil - eyil,
	kaṇ - kaṇṇi,
	ol - oṉ - oṉṉār,
	maṟu - maṟai.
	kuḷ - kuṭa - kuṭai.
	vaḷ - vari - varicai,
	aṇ - aṭu - aṭar - aṭavi,
	iru - eru - erumai,
	a - aṉ - aṉai,
	māṟu - māṟē. 
	iru - iṉu - eṉu - ēṉai - yāṉai,
	pul - puḷ - puṇar,
	vē - vēku - vēkam,
	vēku - vekuḷ - vekuḷi,
	iṟu - iṟai - iṟaiñcu,
	nal - nalku, oḷ - oḷi - oḷir - oḷiṟu.
	maru - mār - mārpu,
	kaḷ - kaḷi - kaḷiṟu,
	iru - irā - iravu.
	koḷ - koṭu - koṭi, koḷ - koṇpu - kompu.
	kuṭi - kuṭimpu - kuṭumpi - kuṭumi,
	pul - poḷ - pōḻ - pōḻtu - poḻutu - poḻutam,
	pul - pol - poru - porun - poruntu.
	poru - poruḷ,
	vai - vacai,
	kaḷ - karu - karum - karumpu,
	kil - kiḷ - kēḷ - kēṇ - kēṇi.
	kēḷ - kēḻal,
	paṇ - pāṇ - pāṇaṉ,
	kiḷ - kēḷ,
	i - iṉ - iṉi,
	puri - puricai,
	vaḷ - vāḷ - vāḷai,
	tī - tīmai,
	pul - puṉmai,
	vaḷ - vaṭku - vaṭkār.
acainilaikaḷ : attai = atai,
	ittai = itai, mātō = peṇṇē, maṉṉō = aracē,
	mati = aṟivākum, aḷavākum, pōtum,
	ōr = uṇar. ōrum - uṇarum, āyum.
	kuraittu = perumaiyuṭaiyatu.
	amma = ammaiyē. til = uḷḷam (?) 
	kol = ō (?) ō - vō - kō - kōl - kol.
7.	moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā (tol.)
i-ḷ : coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷ pārtta allatu kēṭṭa maṭṭil tōṉṟā e-tu.
	in nūṟpāviṟkup palkalaikkaḻaka akarātip patip pāḷar, Tolkappiyar says that the origin of words beyond ascertainment” eṉṟa viḷakkaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu mikamika iraṅkattakkatu.
8.	kumari nāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ pala iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl vēr maṟainta coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ tāṅki nillā.
9.	oru poruṭkup palakāraṇampaṟṟip pala col tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu :	kaṭal = vaḷaintatu, periyatu; munnīr = mūṉṟu nīrai yuṭaiyatu; yāṉai = kariyatu. kaimmāṉ = kaiyai yuṭaiya vilaṅku. vēḻam = karumpaiyum mūṅkilaiyum tiṉpatu. (kaḻai = mūṅkil (158), kaḻaikkarumpu (137), kaḻaitiṉ yāṉaiyār - oru pulavar (204)).
10.	orē kāraṇampaṟṟip pala poruṭku allatu karuttukkup palvēṟu col tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu :	iṟu - iṟaṅkutal = vaḷaital (98), iṟaiñcuka = vaṇaṅkuka (6).
	am - ampu = vāṇam (vaḷaivatu), āmpi = kāḷāṉ.
	kuḷ - kuṭai = vaḷainta kavikai, koṭumai = vaḷaivu (39).
	tiri = tiriyāc cuṟṟam (2), tiruki = kuṟuki (27), tiruki - tikiri (32), puri - puri = muṟukku (135), puricai = matil (17), vaḷ - vaḷai. vaṇar - vaḷaivu (455). vaṇaṅku = vaḷainta (32), muḷ - muṭam (307).
11.	oru karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟu karuttut tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu :	kamaḻa - parakka (50), vīciya - citaṟiya (23).
	kuṟumpu = ciṟṟaraṇ, kuṟumparacar, kuṟumparacar ceyyum tīmai. cemmai = civappu, nērmai.
12.	parupporuṭ karuttiṉiṉṟu nuṇporuṭ karuttum, nuṇporuṭ karuttiṉiṉṟu parupporuṭ karuttun tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu :	ataṉ talai = ataṟku mēlē (19),
	kavalai = kavartta vaḻi (3), maṉakkavalai.
	muḷ - muy - muyal - mayal - māl - mālai,
	tul - tulām = oppu, tarācu (39).
13.	oruporuṭ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu otta karuttut tōṉṟalām.
e-ṭu :	poru = poruntu. pōr cey; camam - oppu. pōr (14) uṟu = poruntu. uṟuvar = etirntavar (11),
	uṟaḻtal = māṟupaṭutal (22).
	uṭal = uṭaṉiruppatu. uṭalutal = pakaittal (25).
	uṭalunar = māṟupaṭuvōr.
	akal = viri, nīṅku; paṭar = viri, cel. acai = iyaṅku. varuntu; cali = acai, varuntu, kaḷai.
14.	poruḷ vēṟupaṭac col vēṟupaṭum.
e-ṭu :	maṇ - maṇal. talaiyaṉ - talaivaṉ, vaḻipaṭu - vaḻippaṭu. paṭiya - paṭikka. tēyntāṉ - tēyttāṉ.
	ī - vī - vīḻ - viḻai - viḻā - viḻavu.
	vīḻ - viḻu - viḻum - viḻuppam. viḻumiya = ciṟanta.
	vīḻ - viḻu. vīḻ - vīḻtu - viḻutu.
	viḻu - viḻal. vī - viḻunta malar. kāṉa vūkiṉ kaḻaṉṟuku mutuvī (307). vī kamaḻ vīḻumpū nāṟukiṉṟa (36, urai).
	viḻu - viku - vikuti - vikruti (va.). o. nō, toḻu - toku.
	vītal = viḻutal, muṭital, iṟattal.
	viku + tam = vikutam - vikitam = viḻukkāṭu.
	vikutam = vītam.
	vīḻ - vīḷ - viḷ - virum - virumpu.
	virum - viruntu = virumpiyiṭum uṇavu. virumpi yūṭṭappaṭum puttāḷ, putumai.
	viḷ - veḷ - vemmai - veyyōṉ.
	vēḷ - vēṇ - vēṇṭu - vēṇṭum - vēṇṭām - vēṇṭā.
	vēḷ - vēṭṭam - vēṭṭai - vēṭṭuvaṉ - vēṭuvaṉ - vēṭaṉ - vēṭu.
	vēḷ - vēṭkai.
	vēḷ - vēḷvi, vēḷāṇmai, vēḷāḷaṇ, vēḷāṉ, vēḷ, vēḷam.
	veḷ - peḷ - peṇ - pēṇ - pēṭu, pēṭaṉ, pēṭi.

	iṉi, piṟaku (back), pukai (Smoke), paiyuḷ (Pain); poṟi (Gk. pur. fire), vaṭṭi (vat) veḷu (pale) mutaliya aiyuṟavāṉa coṟ pōlikaḷum, vāṭu (fade), vēṇṭu (want) mutaliya pōli yoppumaic coṟkaḷum ettuṇaiyō uḷaveṉṟaṟika.
	āṅkilam paṇṭaikkālattē tamiḻoṭu kalantirukku māyiṉ, mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟkaḷellām āṅkilattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻukku vantavaiyākak karutappaṭumaṉṟō?
attōṭu, boy, nation mutaliya coṟkaḷkūṭa, paiyaṉ nāṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷākat tirintatākak koḷḷappaṭumaṉṟō!  iṅṅaṉamē vaṭacoṟpōl tōṉṟum teṉ coṟkaḷu meṉka.
8.	vaṭacoṟ pōlikaḷ
	antaṇaṉ :  am + taṇ + aṉ = antaṇaṉ. aḻakiya kuḷirnta aruḷaiyuṭaiyavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. ākavē tuṟavi allatu muṉivaṉ peyarām.
antaṇa reṉpō raṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmai pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ  (kuṟaḷ)
	eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉpatu paḻantamiḻp pakuppu.
	iṉi, vētaṅkaḷiṉ antattai aṇavuvōṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum, antaṇaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻccollē. aṇavu = poruntu. aṇ- aṇa- aṇavu.
anti: al - antu - antam - antaram = muṭivu. al - alku = muṭi, aḻi. al - aṟu. antu - anti= pakaṟ kālattiṉ muṭivu. neṭiṟcuraṅkaḷai antarakkōl eṉpatu icaittamiḻ vaḻakku.
amaraṉ : a + maraṉ = amaraṉ. maṭi- mari - maraṉ. al - a. etirmaṟai muṉṉoṭṭu. muṭi - maṭi. iṉi, amariṉāl tēvarulakai yaṭaintavaṉ eṉiṉum poruntum.
amayam : camai + am = camaiyam = oṉṟaṟkuc camainta vēḷai, vēḷai, camaiyam - camayam = āṉmā vīṭṭaiyaṭaitaṟkuc camaiyum neṟi. camai - amai. amai +am = amaiyam - amayam. camaital = pakkuvamātal. camaittal = cōṟṟaip pakkuvamākkal. amai - amaivu, amaippu, amaiti.
amiḻtu : avi - aviḻ - aviḻtu - aviḻtam = cōṟu. cōṟṟup pāl, pāl, maruntu (aṉṉappāl, irumaruntu eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka) aviḻtu - amiḻtu - amutu. aviḻtam - amiḻtam - amutam. amutu = cōṟu. amutam = cōṟu, pāl, nīr.
aracar : a-ari- aram- aracu. aram - araṉ = aḻippavaṉ, tēvaṉ, civaṉ, aramakaḷir = tēva makaḷir, aracu + aṉ = aracaṉ = tēvaṉaip pōṉṟavaṉ, talaivaṉ, aracaṉ - araicaṉ - araiyaṉ - araiyam. aracu - araicu.  araiyaṉ - rāyaṉ - E.roy, aracu - E. arch; aracaṉ - Gk. archon. aracu = aracu pōṉṟa maram ; pūvaracu = pūvaiyuṭaiya aracu, āṟṟaracu = āṟṟaṅkaraiyaracu.
avalam : a+valam = avalam a. etirmaṟai muṉṉoṭṭu, val-valam = valimai. avalam = tuṉpuṟaṟkētuvāṉa valimaiyaṟṟa nilai, tuṉpam.
avi : avi = avikkappaṭṭa vuṇavu, uṇavu.
avai : avi - avir = viḷaṅku. avi - avai = kaṟṟaṭaṅkiyōr. allatu aṟivāl viḷaṅkuvōr kuḻu, o.nō: E.galaxy = the Milky - Way, any splendid assemblage. avai +am = avaiyam.
āyutam : āy = kaḷai, oṭi, veṭṭu. āy + tam = āytam - āyutam = veṭṭum karuvi. karuvi. paṭai.
ilakkam (kuṟi) : el-ilaku- ilakku. ilakkam = viḷakkam, viḷaṅkum iṭam, iṭam, kuṟi, viḷakkattiṟ keṭuttuk kāṭṭu. ellē yilakkam eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam = eṭuttuk kāṭṭu, kuṟi cf. E. illustrate - L. illustratum, to light up. nūṟṟokutiyaik kuṟikkum ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ col vēṟu. aḵtu ilakku (= eḻutu) eṉpataṉaṭiyāyp piṟantatu.
urakam : ura = poruntu, taḻuvu, uram = mārpu. o.nō: maru - marumam - mār - mārpu, maruvu = kala, taḻuvu, uram- urakam = mārpiṉāl ūrvatu, pāmpu.
uru :  uru = (vi). tōṉṟu. (pe) tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, vaṭivattaiyu ṭaiya oru poruḷ, uṭampu, oru vaṭivattiṟkuḷ eḻutappaṭum mantiram, mantiram pōṉṟa pāṭṭu, uru - uruvu - uruvam, uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumai vaṭivam. uruppaṭi = oru taṉip poruḷ. uruppaṭu urukkulai mutaliya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅku talaiyum. urupu eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭāy iruttalaiyum nōkkuka.
ulakam : cuḷ - cul - culam - culavu- ulavu= vaḷai, cuṟṟu, culam - ulam - ulaku - ulakam = vaṭṭamā yiruppatu allatu cuḻalvatu allatu katiravaṉaic cuṟṟi varuvatu. ulam varuvōr (puṟam, 51) = cuḻalvōr. cyh = efiu tykhf¢ R‰¿ tUjš, x.neh.: koḷ - kōḷ = Planet. koḷ = vaḷai. cuṟṟu. koṭpu = cuṟṟukai. koḷ - koṭu- kōṭu- kōṇu.
ēmam : ē-ēm-ēmam = pātukāppu, iṉpam. ē= uyarcci. ē peṟṟākum, eṉpatu tolkāppiyam, vilaṅkukaḷālum pakaivarālum veḷḷattālum tuṉpam nērumpōtu, uyarnta iṭam pātukāppiṟkētuvāṉatu. pātukāppāl iṉpamuṇṭākum. ēmam - cēmam, o.nō: ēṇ- cēṇ. cēmavaccu (102).
ēṉāti : ēṉai (yāṉai) + ari = ēṉāri - ēṉāti = yāṉaiyaik koṉṟavaṉ. o.nō: kōṭu + ari = kōṭari. parukkai - patukkai.
kaṭumpu : kuṭam - kuṭi - kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu. kuṭumpu - kuṭumpam. kuṭi = uṭaṉuḷḷatu. uṭampu pōṉṟa vīṭu, vīṭṭu vāḻkkai, vīṭṭiluḷḷa maṉaivi, maṉaivi makkaḷākiya kuṭumpam, kuṭumpa marapu, kulam, oru kulam vāḻiṭam, ūr, kaṭumpu = kuṭumpam, cuṟṟam.
kaṇaṉ : kala - kaḷa- kaḷam - kaṇam - kaṇaṉ = kūṭṭam.
kantaṉ : kal - kantu - kantam - kantaṉ = āṉmākkaṭkuk kaṟṟūṇ pōṟ paṟṟukkōṭāṉavaṉ, murukaṉ, kantaḻi = paṟṟuk kōṭaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ.
	kantapurāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaḷapaṭi, kantaṉ imayamalaic caravaṇap poykaiyiṟ piṟantavaṉallaṉ, imayamalai tōṉṟiyataṟku muṉpē kumari nāṭṭil kuṟiñcit teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭavaṉ. mutaṟkālattil murukaṉuñ civaṉum oruvaṉē. tiṇaimayakka mēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, murukaṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavukaḷākavum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāṉ.
kammiyaṉ : kaṭu-karu, kaṭu-kaṭi- kari. kaṭuttal = mikutal. karuttal = tōṉṟutal, karittal = mikutal. mikutaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟum ceytaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. x.neh.:  E.make, from L.magnus, great. alam + kari = alaṅkari = aḻaku cey. karu kari eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ ceytaṟ poruḷil vaḻaṅkiya nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭaik kālattil virivāṉa akarātikaḷ eḻutappaṭāmaiyāl, kumarināṭṭuc coṟkaḷil palavaṟṟaik kāṭṭuvataṟku oru vaḻiyumillai. kari + aṇam = karaṇam = ceykai, ceyyum caṭaṅku, maṇaccaṭaṅku, ceyyuṅkaruvi, o.nō:  vari+ aṇam = varaṇam. karu + vi = karuvi. karu + mam = karumam. x.neh.: paru + mam = parumam. karaṇam - kāraṇam. kari + iyam = kariyam - kāriyam. o.nō: kaṇṭam - kāṇṭam. karu - kru (va.). o.nō: tura- tru (va.). karumam = ceykai, toḻil, viṉaippayaṉ, ceyappaṭuporuḷ. karumam eṉpatu mutalāvatu uḻavut toḻilaiyum, piṉpu kait toḻilaiyuṅ kuṟittatu. karumam - kammam - kam. kammavār - teluṅkuḻavar. kamakkāraṉ - uḻavaṉ. kammi = toḻilāḷi. kammāḷaṉ = aivakaik kollaruḷ oruvaṉ. kammiyam = kaittoḻil, kammāḷat toḻil, kammiyanūl = ciṟpanūl, kammiyaṉ = toḻilāḷi, kammāḷaṉ, neypavaṉ. karumakaṉ - karumāṉ - karumaṉ = kollaṉ, karumakaṉ eṉṉum peyaril karu eṉpatu toḻilaiyāvatu irumpaiyāvatu kuṟikkum.
	māriyallatu kāriyamillai yātalāl, karutta mēkam karuvi eṉappaṭṭatu eṉṟu koḷvatu oṉṟu.
karakam : kuṭam - kuṭakam - kurakam - karakam = vaḷainta allatu kuvinta nīrk kalam, o.nō:  muyaṅku - mayaṅku, muyal - mayal, kuṭam - kaṭam, kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu, muṭi - maṭi.
karu : karu - karuppu - karuppam - garbha. mēkam cūl koṇṭapōtu karuttiruttalāl, cūl karuveṉappaṭṭatu.
kavari : iḵtoru vilaṅkup peyarātalāṉum, vēṟu peyar tamiḻilitaṟkiṉmaiyāṉum, itu tamiḻc colleṉṟē terikiṉṟatu. kavari - cavari - cavaram - camaram - cāmaram.
kātal : kā + tal = kātal. kā eṉṉum col viruppu eṉṉum poruḷil kumari nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. kavar - kā. kavarvu viruppākum (tol. col. uri. 64)
kāmar : kā + mam = kāmam -  kām. kāmam - kāmar. kām + maru = kāmaru - kāmar eṉṟumām.
kāyam : kaḷ - kaḷam - kayam = karuppu. kayam - kāyam = kariya vāṉ. kariya iruḷai iruṭṭukkayam allatu iruṭṭuk kacam eṉpatu ulakavaḻakku. tolkāppiyattil, kāyam eṉṉum vaṭivē ākāyattaik kuṟikka vantuḷḷatu. karuppeṉṉum poruḷilēyē kayam eṉṉum col iruḷ, kuḷam, yāṉai eṉpaṉavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum.
kālam :  kāl = cintu, nīḷ (vi): nēram (pe.).
vārtal pōkal oḻukal mūṉṟum
nērpum neṭumaiyuñ ceyyum poruḷa  (tol. col. 800)
eṉpataṉōṭu oppu nōkkuka.
	nīṇṭu cellum poruḷkaḷellām tamiḻiṟ kāleṉap paṭum. kāl + am = kālam - kālaṉ. kāl + ai = kālai. ceytakkāl - kāla viṉaiyecca vikuti.
kāḷāmpi :	kāḷam + āmpi = kāḷāmpi. kaḷ - kaḷam - kāḷam = karuppu. kaḷ -kaḷavu = karuppu, maṟaivu. kaḷam - kaḷaṅku - kaḷaṅkam = karuppu. kaṟai, kuṟṟam, kaḷ - kaḷḷam = karuppu, karavu, tiruṭṭu. kaḷḷam - kaḷḷaṉ. kāḷam - kāḷi = karuppi. māyōḷ. kaḷ - karu - kār.
kuṭi : mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kuṭam : cuḷ - kuḷ - kuḷa - kuḻa - kuṭa - kuṭam = vaḷainta allatu kuvinta kalam. kuṭam - kuṭavaṉ - kucavaṉ - kuyavaṉ. kuṭam - kucam - kuyam = vaḷaivu. kuṭam - kaṭam (va.).
kuṇakku : kuṭam - kuṇam - kuṇakku = vaḷaivu. katiravaṉ mutaliya vaḷaiyuṅ kiḻakku. iṉi, koḷ - koṇṭal - kuṇakku - kuṇam eṉiṉumām.
kumari : kum - kom - kommai = tiraṭci. kum - kumam - kumar - kumari (i. peṇpāl vikuti) = tiraṇṭavaḷ, iḷampeṇ, kaṉṉi, kaṉṉiyākak karutappaṭum kāḷi. cf. E. Virgin from Gk. orgao to swell. kumar + aṉ = kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ, murukaṉ. kumari vaṇakkam āriyar varumuṉpē tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruntatu.
kurūu : kuruvuṅ keḻuvu niṟaṉā kummē  (tol. col. 301). eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. kuruti, kurutik kāntaḷ (ceṅkāntaḷ), kurutivāram (cevvāykkiḻamai) mutaliya collaiyum vaḻakkaiyum nōkkumiṭattu, kuru eṉpatu cenniṟam eṉa aṟiyappaṭum. civaperumāṉ niṟam cemmai yeṉṟum avaṉ āciriyar vaṭivil āṉmākkaḷai āṭ koḷvāṉeṉṟum nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatālum, āciriyaṉukkuk kuru kuravaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷuṇmai yālum, kuru eṉṉum niṟaṅkuṟitta collē kuravaṉ eṉṟu tirintu āciriyaṉ, periyōṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭateṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kuru eṉṉum collum āciriyaṉaik kuṟittalāl, atu paṇpup peyarākumpōtu perumai eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. perumai eṉpatu parupporuḷum nuṇ poruḷumākiya vaṭivunilai yiraṇṭaṟkum potuvātaliṉ, kaṉam eṉṉuñ col matippaiyum niṟaiyaiyuṅ kuṟittāṟpōlak kuru eṉṉuñ collum perumaiyaiyum niṟaiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkumeṉka.
	kuru - Skt. guru. L. gravis E. grave, weighty, kurūu aḷapeṭai.
kūṭakāram :	 kūṭu - kūṭam. ār = āram - vaṭṭam. kūṭavāram - kūṭakāram, kūṭāram. o.nō : koṭṭāram.
kōṉ : kō = pacu. kō + aṉ = kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kōṉāṉ. kōvaṉ = iṭaiyaṉ. ākkaḷai iṭaiyaṉ kāppatu pōl makkaḷaik kākkum aracaṉ, uyirkaḷākiya ākkaḷaik kākkum kaṭavuḷ (civaṉ). ivai āriyak karuttukaḷalla. ivaṟṟait tamiḻariṭattiṉiṉṟē āriyar kaṇṭaṟintaṉar. paṇṭait tamiḻar celvattai māṭeṉ ṟamaiyālum, iyaṟpeyar ciṉaippeyar ciṉaimutaṟpeyar muṟaippeyar eṉṉum nālvakaiyil makkaḷ peyaraiyē māṭukaṭ kiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyatālum, avarkaḷ māṭukaḷai makkaḷaip pōṟ pēṇiyamai viḷaṅkum.
	kōṉ - kō = aracaṉ.
kōṭi : kōṭu - kōṭi = kaṭaici, kaṭaici yeṇ.
caṭai : caṭacaṭaveṉṟiruppatu caṭai. caṭacaṭaveṉpatu oru viṟappuk kuṟippu. caṭaiyaṉ caṭaimuṉi eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻp peyarkaḷ.
camar : camam - camar - camaram, camar - amar. camam = oppu. kalappu, pōr. o. nō : poru - pōr.
cāṭi : cāl - cāṭi.
curam : cuḷ - cur -curam = cūṭu, kāyccal, pālainilam.
cūr : cur - curam. cūram - cūraṉ - cūr, cur - cūr = neruppā luṇṭākum accam.
ñamaṉ : camaṉ - (yamaṉ) - namaṉ - ñamaṉ = naṭunilaiyuḷḷa kūṟṟuvaṉ. yamaṉ - emaṉ.
ñāyiṟu : naya - nāyaṉ - nāyiṟu - ñāyiṟu = kōḷkaḷukkut talaivaṉ. nāyaṉ = virumpappaṭattakka talaivaṉ. o.nō: nampu - nampaṉ. nampu mēvu nacaiyā kummē. eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
tavam : tapu  + am = tapam - tavam = keṭuttal, nīkkutal, paṟṟaṟuttal, nīttār, tuṟavu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷai nōkkuka, tavir, tavattal (nīṅkutal) eṉpaṉa tavam eṉpatōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya coṟkaḷ.
ticai : tikku - tikai - ticai = tikaippiṟkiṭamāṉa ellai. tikkuttikkeṉṟirukkiṟatu. tikkumukkāṭal mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka. ticaittal = tikaittal. ticaiccol eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭu.
tiru : tiruveṉṉuñ col teṉcollē. tirumakaḷ, tirumaṉai, tirumāl tiruvil tiruvaraṅkam mutaliya paḻaimaiyāṉa coṟkaḷai nōkkuka. tiru - srī = cī.
tī : tēy - tē - tī = uracutalāluṇṭākum neruppu.
tulām : tul - tuṉ = poruntu, poruttit tai, tul - tulai = poruttam, oppu, iru puṟamum otta tarācu, tul - tulā - tulām - tulāṉ = tarācu, ōr eṭai, tulā + kōl = tulākkōl, tulā = tulākkōl, pōṉṟa ēṟṟam, kaittulā, āḷēṟun tulā eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakku. tuṉ - tuṟu = poruntu, neruṅku.

aṇintaṉṟu : aṇintu + aṉṟu = aṇintaṉṟu = aṇintatu. aṉ + atu = aṉṉatu = attakaiyatu, atu. aṉ +tu (atu)= aṉṟu = atu

	1. muṉṉilaiyorumai nikaḻkāla viṉaimuṟṟu
	2. muṉṉilai yorumai etirkāla viṉaimuṟṟu
	3. ēval orumai viṉaimuṟṟu.

	o.nō :  ceyal = ceyka. iruṭīra veṇṇic ceyal.

irīi :  iri = iruntu, irī = iritti :  irīi (irutti) aḷapeṭai, uṭīi, koḷīi eṉpaṉavum iṅṅaṉamē. īṟṟuyir nīḷutalum piṟaviṉaiyākum vakai pōlum!
	o.nō:  uṟu - uṟū = uṟuttu, uṟūu, aḷapeṭai, iṉi, iri - irīi eṉa aḷapeṭaiyē piṟaviṉaiyākum vakai eṉṟumām.

kaṇṭiciṉ :  (kaṇṭēṉ) :  kaṇṭu + īyēṉ = kaṇṭīyēṉ - kaṇṭīyiṉ - kaṇṭīciṉ - kaṇṭiciṉ. īyēṉ = īntēṉ, iṭṭēṉ (tuṇaiviṉai).  o.nō:  pōyēṉ = pōṉēṉ.

kaḷaimē (kaḷaivāy): kaḷaiyum + ē= kaḷaiyumē - kaḷaimē. kaḷaiyum pālvaḻuvamaiti, ceyyumē - ceymmē ē ārvap poruḷ vikuti, ēṉ vikuti ē eṉak kuṟainta teṉiṉumām. moḻimō eṉpatil ō ipporuḷpaṭa vantatu.

pū (1)
pū - va. pū (bh) - i. vē.
pul - puḷ - puḻu - puku. pukutal = tōṉṟutal.
puku - pū. pūttal = 1. tōṉṟutal. pūttiḻi matamalai (kamparā. kumpakar. 315). 2. paṭaittal. ñālamellām pūttōṉē (pārata kiruṭṭiṇa. 12). 3. peṟṟeṭuttal. oru tiruvaip pūttaṉaḷ (piramōt. 8 : 15). 4. iruttal. pūttaliṟ pūvāmai naṉṟu (nīti.6).
puku - poku - pokil = arumpu. pokilpiṭit talaruñ cantap potumpar (iraku. kucaṉayō. 63).
pokil - pōkil = arumpu (piṅ.).
poku - pō - pōttu = vaḷār, ciṟu putukkiḷai.
pōttu veṭittal eṉpatu marapu.
malaraik kuṟikkum pū eṉṉum col vēṟu. atu pol eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatu.
pol - poli - polivu. pol - pūl - pū.
pūl eṉṉum vaṭivam iṉṟu intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.  (va.va : 207 - 208).
pū (2)
pū : pūtam = peruveḷiyil tōṉṟiya nālvakai allatu aivakaip poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum  (tol. 242).
pālvarai teyvam viṉaiyē pūtam
pālpirin ticaiyā vuyartiṇai mēṉa.  (tol. 541).
pū : pū, (va. bhu), iccolliṉiṉṟē puvana, pūmi, paviṣya, pāvana. mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟāc coṟkaḷum, anupava, anupūti, utpava, svayampū mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa coṟkaḷumāka nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ tōṉṟumeṉa aṟika.  (ti.ma : 749).
pūkkāmpu vakai
kāmpu ciṟiyatu;
tāḷ allatu taṇṭu, parumaiyum, meṉmaiyumuḷḷatu.
nāḷam, uruṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷatu.  (col. 67).
pūk kutirai viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oru pūp peyaraic colli, oruvaṉmē loruvaṉ kutiraiyēṟi viḷaiyāṭuvatu pūkkutirai.
āṭuvār tokai : potuvāka, aivarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟattilum teruvilum piṟa veḷiyiṭaṅkaḷilum itai āṭalām.
āṭu muṟai : ētēṉumoru tērnteṭuppu vakaiyil, tavaṟi viṭṭa oruvaṉ, aṇṇāvipōl niṟkum oruvaṉiṭam maṟaivāka oru pūppeyaraic colliviṭṭuk, kuṉintu niṟka vēṇṭum. piṟar ovvoruvaṉāy avaṉ mēṟ kutiraiyēṟumuṉ, ovvoru pūppeyarai aṇṇāviyiṭam veḷippaṭaiyāyc colla vēṇṭum. yārēṉum oruvaṉ coṉṉa pū kuṉintavaṉ coṉṉatā yiruppiṉ piṉpu avaṉ aṇṇāviyiṭam oru pūp peyarai maṟaivākac colliviṭṭut tāṉ kuṉiya vēṇṭum. muṉpu kuṉintu niṉṟavaṉ, piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu muṉ coṉṉavāṟu viḷaiyāṭuvāṉ. kuṉintavaṉ collāta vēṟu pūp peyar collik kutiraiyēṟiṉavaṉ, oru nimaiyattiṟku iṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum. kuṉintavaṉ coṉṉa pū varumvaraiyum, ovvoruva ṉākavum māṟi māṟiyum ēṟi yiṟaṅkik koṇṭē yiruppar.  (ta.nā.vi.)
pūcci viḷaiyāṭṭu
pūcci eṉpatu āḷniḻal. nilavoḷiyiṟ pūcci teriyumpōtu oruvarait toṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu, pūcci allatu pūcci viḷai yāṭṭu. āṭuvāruḷ ētēṉumoru vakaiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa oruvar, nilavoḷiyiṭattil niṟka, ēṉaiyarellām arukēyuḷḷa ōr iruṇṭa iṭattil niṉṟukoḷvar. iruṇṭa iṭattil niṟpavar oḷiyiṭattiṟku variṉ, avarait toṭalām; illāviṭiṉ toṭal kūṭātu. oḷiyiṭattil toṭappaṭṭavar piṉpu piṟarait toṭutal vēṇṭum.  (ta.nā.vi.)
pūcu
pūcu - va. pūj, pūcai - va. pūjā
pūcutal = kaḻuvutal. nīruṇṭār nīrāṉvāy pūcupa (nāṉ maṇik.35).
mukampūcutal eṉpatu teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakku.
pūcu - pūcai = teyvap paṭimaiyai nīrāṭṭuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭu.
pūcu - pūcuṉai. ciṟappoṭu pūcuṉai cellātu (kuṟaḷ. 18).
pūcai - pūcaiyāri - pūcāri. talaiyāri eṉṉuñ colliṟpōl āri eṉpatu ōr īṟu.
pūcai
pūcutal = kaḻuvutal. teyvap paṭimaiyai nīrāl tuppuravākkutal. 
pūcu - pūci. pūcittal = pūccāttiyum tēṅkāy paḻam mutaliyaṉa paṭaittum vaḻipaṭutal. uḻavu eṉpatu payirt toḻiliṉ piṉ viṉaikaḷaiyum kuṟittal pōl pūcittal eṉpatum vaḻipāṭṭiṉ piṉviṉaikaḷaiyum kuṟittatu.
pūci - pūcai. o.nō : ācu (paṟṟu) - āci (aḷavu) - ācai (avā).
pūci - pūcaṉai, pūcaṉam. ai, aṉai, aṉam eṉpaṉa tamiḻ īṟukaḷē.
pūcai - pūcāri. āri - talaiyāri eṉpatil pōl ōr īṟu.
pūcāccāri (pūcai + āccāri) eṉṉum vaḻakku vaṭamoḻiyilum illai.
pū cey eṉpatu pūcai eṉṟāyiṟṟeṉpatu poruntātu.
pūci - pūj (va.). pū - pūjā (va). pūcaṉam - pūjaṉa (va.).
pūcaṉai - pūjaṉā (va.).
vēta āriyarkku vēḷḷi vēṭṭalēyaṉṟip paṭimappūcaiyum kōvil vaḻipāṭum illai. pūj eṉṉum collum vētattil illai. pāratam mutaliya piṟkāla vaṭapaṉuvalkaḷilēyē atu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.	(ti.ma. 47).
pūtam
pūtam : pūttal = tōṉṟutal.
avaiyañci meyvitirppār kalviyuṅ kallār
avaiyañcā vākulac cellum - navaiyañci
īttuṇṇār celvamu nalkūrntā riṉṉalamum
pūttaliṟ pūvāmai naṉṟu.  (nītineṟi. 5).
ippāviluḷḷa pūttaṟ col vēṟu; malartalaik kuṟikkum pūttaṟ col vēṟu. malartalaik kuṟippatu polivai yuṇarttum pol eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintatākum.  (ti.ma. 749).
pūtam1 - va. pūta (bh) - i.vē.
pūttal = tōṉṟutal. pū - pūtu - pūtam = tōṉṟiyatu, aimpūtaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum  (tol. 242).
pūtam2 - va. pūta (bh)
ūtāṅkuḻalāl neruppūtuvataip pūttup pūtteṉa ūtukiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. kāṟṟūtuvatāl turuttiyum ūttāmpaiyum puṭaikkum.
ūtutal = vīṅkutal, paruttal.
ūtu - pūtu - pūtal - pūtali. pūtalittal = paruttal, taṭittal.
pūtu - pūtam = paruttatu, parutta pēy.
pūtakkāl = yāṉaikkāl. pūtakkaṇṇāṭi = perukkikkāṭṭum. kaṇṇāṭi.  (va.va.)
iṉi, pottu - potu - putu - pūtu - pūtam eṉṟumām. putā = perunārai. pottu - pottai = parumiḷakāy.
putal - putar = aṭar ceṭi. putai = aṭarkāṭu, ampukkaṭṭu.
putai - pūtai = ampuk kaṭṭu. pūti = potu. potu - potumpu - potumpar.
mā. vi. a. vum ce. pa. ka. ka. ta. a. vum iruvēṟu pūtac collaiyum oṉṟākak kūṟiyiruppatu tavaṟām.  (va.va : 208 - 209).
pūti
pūti - va. pūti (bh)
puḻuti - pūti = 1. puḻuti (cūṭā). 2. cāmpal. 3. tirunīṟu (piṅ.). pūtiyaṇi poṉṉiṟattar (tēvā. 592 : 2). 
o.nō : koḻutu - kōtu, poḻutu - pōtu.  (va.va : 209).
pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm viḷaiyāṭṭu
potuvākap patiṉmarukkuk kuṟaiyāta pala piḷḷaikaḷ uttikaṭṭic camamāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintu, or akaṉṟa teruvilēṉum ūrppoṭṭa lilēṉum iru kaṭcikkum potuvāka oru naṭukkōṭu kīṟi, ataṟku ippālum appālum niṉṟukoḷvar. piṉpu irukaṭciyārum kaṭcivāriyākak kaikōttu muṉpiṉṉākap piṉvarumāṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, māṟimāṟi naṭukkōṭu varai ceṉṟu mīḷvar.
mutaṟ kaṭciyār : pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm. pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm, inta nāḷilē.
iraṇṭāṅ kaṭciyār : yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr? yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr? inta nāḷilē.
mu : kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm,
	kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm, innāḷilē.
i : entappū vēṇṭum?
	entappū vēṇṭum? inta nāḷilē.
mu : mallikaippū vēṇṭum,
	mallikaippū vēṇṭum, inta nāḷilē.
iṉṉapū vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli mutaṟkaṭciyār pāṭi muṭin tatum, irukaṭciyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru piḷḷai muṉceṉṟu naṭukkōṭṭai yaṭuttavuṭaṉ, iruvarum oruttiyai yorutti piṭittiḻuppar. kōṭṭiṟkappāl iḻuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭa piḷḷai etirkkaṭciyaic cērum. piṉpu muṉpōṟ pāṭi, vēṟiruvar iḻuppar. iṅṅaṉam ivviruvarāy ellārum iḻukkappaṭṭapiṉ, mikutiyākap piḷḷaikaḷ cērntirukkiṟa kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar.
pūmaṭal vakai
pū : vāḻai maṭal;
maṭal : tāḻai, vāḻai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ maṭal;
pāḷai : teṉṉai, paṉai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ maṭal.	(col. 66).

arumpu : pūviṉ tōṟṟanilai;
pōtu : pū viriyat toṭaṅkum nilai;

cemmal : pū vāṭiṉa nilai.  (col. 67).
pūṉaippeyarkaḷ
(1) pūcai
pūṉai eṉpatu pūcai eṉṉum colliṉ tiripē. pūcutal = kaḻuvutal, cilaikaḻuvutal. nellai māvaṭṭattiṉ orupakutiyil, mukam pūcutal eṉpatu iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkāyirukkiṉṟatu. pūcikkoḷiṉum irumpiṉkaṇ mācoṭṭum (nāṉmaṇi. 94).
pūṉai aṭikkaṭi taṉ eccilai muṉṉaṅkālāl toṭṭu mukattaip pūcikkoḷvatāl, atu pūcaiyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu. vevvāy vekukiṉaip pūcaiyeṉṟalum (tol. mara. 69). vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa mārjāla eṉṉum pūṉaip peyarum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyatē. meḻuku - va. mruj (mrj) = pūcu, kaḻuvu. mruj - mārj - mārjāra - mārjāla.
pūṉaiyaip pūcupūcu eṉṟaḻaippatu peṇṭir vaḻakkam. pūcai - pūccai (nāñcilnāṭṭu vaḻakku) pūccai - ma. pūcca. pūcai - pūṉai. ca - ña, pōlittiripu. o.nō : picai - piṉai.
pūṉaikkaṇ = pūṉaikkaṇ pōṉṟa niṟamuḷḷa oḷikkal (vaiṭūriyam). āṅkilattilum itu cat’s eye eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttalai nōkkuka. vaiṭūriyam eṉṉum vaṭacollum ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyatē.
pūṉai - pūñai o. nō : aṉṉai - aññai. iṉi, pūcai - pūñai eṉṟalumām. o. nō : kuṭicai - kuṭiñai. cilampi ciccili pūñai kirumi (tattuvap. 19).
āṅkilac col (The Oxford English Dictionary and Klein’s Comp. Ety. Dy. of the E. Language).
E. puss, pus, pusse. (A word common to several Teutonic languages, usually as a call-name for the cat).
Du. poes, LG. puus, puuskatte, puus-man, Sw. dial. pus, katte-pus, Norw. puse, puus, Lith. puz, puiz, lr. and Gael. f. us.
Alb piso, Ruman pisicaÍ.
‘Puss in the corner’, a game of children; Pussy - wants - a - corner, an American name of the game.
E. pussy, a pet name for a cat. Y dim suffix.
Pussy - cat, a nursery word for a cat.
Pussyfoot, to tread stealthily like a cat.
Pussy - willow, the American glaucous willow, in reference to its silky catkins.
Puss - Etymology unknown eṉpatu O.E.D.; Imitative of the spitting of a cat eṉpatu K. C. E. D. E. L.
2. piḷḷai (pūṉaippiḷḷai)
piḷḷaiyillātavarkaḷ, cila uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟaip piḷḷaipōl vīṭṭil vaittu vaḷarppatu paṇṭai vaḻakkam. ataṉāl, irukkum piḷḷai mūṉṟu, ōṭum piḷḷai mūṉṟu, paṟakkum piḷḷai mūṉṟu. eṉṟu oru paḻamoḻi yoḻuntatu. ōṭumpiḷḷai mūṉṟaṉuḷ, aṇiṟpiḷḷai kīrippiḷḷai eṉpaṉa iraṇṭu. mūṉṟām piḷḷai pūṉaippiḷḷai yāyiruk kalām.
piḷḷai kuḻavi kaṉṟē pōtteṉak
koḷḷavum amaiyum ōraṟi vuyirkkē.  (tol. mara. 24).
nāyē paṉṟi pulimuyal nāṉkum
āyuṅ kālaik kuruḷai yeṉpa.  (tol. mara. 8).
nariyum aṟṟē nāṭiṉar koḷiṉē.  (tol. mara. 9).
piḷḷaip peyarum piḻaippāṇ ṭillai
koḷḷuṅ kālai nāyalaṅ kaṭaiyē.  (tol. mara. 11)
kōṭuvāḻ kuraṅkum kuṭṭi kūṟupa.  (tol. mara. 13).
mukavum piḷḷaiyum paṟaḻum pārppum
avaiyum aṉṉa appā lāṉa.  (tol. mara. 14).
pārppum piḷḷaiyum paṟappavaṟ ṟiḷamai.  (tol. mara. 4).
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, palvēṟu uyiriṉaṅkaḷilum ciṟcila uyirikaḷ piḷḷai yeṉṟum iḷamaip peyar peṟṟiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
vīṭṭup pūṉaikkuṭṭi pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu perum pāṉmai. ātalāl, pūṉaippiḷḷaiyeṉpatu, peruvaḻakkup paṟṟi, piḷḷaiyeṉṉum potup peyarālēyē kuṟikkappaṭ ṭiruppatu iyalpu. piḷḷaipōl vaḷarkkap paṭuvatu, peritāṉa piṉpum appeyar peṟum. aṇiṟpiḷḷai kīrippiḷḷai mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.  (kuṭṭi eṉpatu āṭṭuk kuṭṭiyaiyum, kaṉṟukkuṭṭi eṉpatu māṭṭuk kaṉṟaiyum, ciṟappākak kuṟittalaiyum nōkkuka).
piḷḷai yeṉṉuñcol teluṅkiṟ pilliyeṉṟu tiriyum. 
o.nō : taḷḷai (tāy) - talli. pilli - in. pillī (billi). pilli yeṉpatu ilattīṉil felis allatu feles eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. uyartiṇaiyil irupāṟkum potuvāṉa piḷḷai yeṉṉum iḷamaippeyar, ilattīṉil filius eṉṟu makaṉaiyum, filia eṉṟu makaḷaiyum kuṟittu vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. piḷḷai eṉpatu, vaṭārkkāṭṭu āmpūr vaṭṭattil āṇpiḷḷaiyaiyum, pāṇṭināṭṭup pakutiyiṟ peṇpiḷḷaiyaiyum, ciṟappākak kuṟippatu, vaḻakku vēṟupāṭu paṟṟiyatē.
pūcai allatu pūṉai yeṉpatu vīṭṭup pūṉaiyaiyē. kāṭṭup pūṉai veruku eṉappaṭum. veruvattakka tōṟṟamum valimaiyum uṭaiyatu veruku. veruvutal - añacutal. veruku vīṭṭil vaḷarkkappaṭṭa piṉ pūcaiyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu. vevvāy verukiṉaip pūcai yeṉṟalum eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka. vīṭṭuppūṉai ilattīṉil felis domes tiea eṉappaṭum.
Feline (adj) = catlike. Felid = one of the Felidoe or cat-tribe.
(3) viṭarakaṉ
viṭutal = piḷattal, viṭu - viṭar = piḷavu, piḷappu, viṭaravaṉ = (pakaliṟ kaṇpiḷavuḷḷa) pūṉai (yāḻ. aka.). viṭaravaṉ - viṭarakaṉ - viṭarakam - viṭarukam - viṭarūkam = pūṉai(nāmatīpa. 203)
viṭarakam - viṭārakam - va. viṭāraka = pūṉai.
viṭārakam - viṭālakam = pūṉai.
viṭālakam - viṭālam = pūṉai (caṅ. aka.).
viṭālam - va. viṭāla - vaiṭāla = pūṉai.
viṭārakam - viṭāram - va. viṭāra - vaiṭūrya = pūṉaikkaṇ pōṉṟa oḷikkal.
vaiṭūrya - ta. vaiṭūryam.
tamiḻiṟ pūṉaikkaṇ eṉṟum āṅkilattil cat’s eye eṉṟum uṟuppait teḷivākak kuṟippiṭṭa uvamaiyākupeyar tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. camaṟkirutac collō pūṉaippeyariṉiṉṟē tirinta tiricollākavuḷḷatu. va. viṭāraka - piṭāraka (b), va. viṭāla - piṭāla (b). vakaramutal vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pakaramutalākat tirital iyalpē. o. nō : valam - va. pala (bala).
(4) ka. kotti = pūṉai.
ma. koṟṟi, kuṟiññi, (kuṟuññi) = peṇpūṉai.
tamiḻil iccol  iṟantupaṭṭatu. paḻañcēra nāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ (vaṭacoṟ ceṟinta) tiripē malaiyāḷamātalāl, koṟṟiyeṉ patait tamiḻākak koḷḷalām.
kuṟu, kuṟukuṟu, koṟukoṟu (ma. kuṟuṭṭuka - kuruṭṭuka, kuṟummuka; ka. kor, kora, korakora) mutaliya olikkuṟippukkaḷai yotta pūṉaiyaṭittoṇṭai yolik (purr) kuṟippiṉiṉṟu kuṟuññi (- kuṟiññi) eṉṉum pūṉaippeyar tōṉṟiyirukka lām. itaṟku koṟṟi yeṉṉuñ colloṭu toṭarpillai.
tamiḻiṟ kuruṭaṉaik kuṟikkak kottai eṉṟoru col uḷatu. kottaikku mūṅkar vaḻikāṭṭuvittu (tēvā. 1040 : 2), iṉṉāṉ eṉpatu, kaṇṇāṟ kottai, kālāṉ mūṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa. (tol. vēṟ. 11, iḷam. urai). kottiyeṉṉuṅ kaṉṉaṭaccol kottaiyeṉṉum tamiḻccollaip peritum ottuḷatu. pūṉai pakalil muḻukkaṇṇum teriyāmal araik kuruṭāyiruppataṉāl, ataṟkuk kottaiyeṉṉum peyar ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. appeyar oru tiraviṭa moḻiyiṟ kottiyeṉṟu tirintiruppatum, uttikkum iyaṟkaikkum poruttamāṉatē. koṟṟiyeṉpatu kottiyeṉpataṉ tiripāyirukkalām.
pūṉaikku mēlē moḻikaḷellāvaṟṟilumperuvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkum peyar, palvēṟu vakaikaḷil tirintiruppiṉum kotti eṉṉum collaiyē ottuḷḷatu.
Gk. katta, kattos; mod. Gk. gata; L. catta, catus, caÍtus, cattus, Late Latin. cattus, catta.
It. gatto; Sp., Pg. gato; Cat. gak; Pr. Cat; ONF. cat; F. chat (fem. gatta, gata, cata, cate, chate, chatte);
E. cat; OE., ME. cat, catt, catte., ON. Kott-r, kattus; keta, fem; Sw. katt, katta; Da kat; MLG. katte; MDu. katte, kat; D. kat; OHG. kazza, chazza, chataro; MHG., Mod. G. katza; MHG. katero, kater; mod. G. and Du. kater = he cat.
OIr. cat (masc); Gael. cat com. W. kath; Welsh and Cornish. cath; Breton kaz; Vannes kac’h m. OCo. kat.
Nubian kadis; Arab qitt, tomcat, qitta, cat.
Slavonic kot; OSlav. kot’ka; Bulg. kotka; Slovenish kot m; Russ. kot m. kotchka, koshka; Pol. kot (koczur m.); Boh. kot m. kotta f.; Sorabian kotka; Lith. kate; Finnish katti.
Derivatives.
Chatoyant, of a changeable colour, shining like the eyes of a cat.
caterpillar fr. LL. catta pilose = hairy cat.
caterwaul fr. LG. caterwanlen = to cry like cats.
meerkat fr. MDu. meer catte = sea cat.
catteny = place where cats are bred.
kitten, catkin, cattish, catishly, catishness, catling, catty, cattily, catliness etc.
airōppāvil ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai pūṉaiyirunta tillaiyeṉṟum, atu ekipatu nāṭṭiliruntu aṅkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭateṉṟum, cat eṉṉum ataṉ peyar ekipatiyac colleṉṟum, mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṉṟār. pūṉai nīṇṭa kālamāka ekipatu nāṭṭil vaḷarkkappaṭṭu vantataṉālum, ōr ekipatiyap peṇ teyvam pūṉaittalai koṇṭiruntata ṉālum, airōppāviṟku ekipatu aṇmaiyiliruppataṉālum, avar kūṟṟup poruttamākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, kumari nāṭē māntaṉ piṟantakamātalālum, tamiḻakattiṟku ekipati ṉōṭu nīṇṭa kālamāka nīr vāṇikamum nilavāṇikamum iruntu vantataṉālum, veruku tamiḻakak kuṟiñcinilak karup poruḷāta lālum, pūṉai tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟē ekipatuviṟkuc ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉāl ataṉ peyarum tamiḻc collāyē yiruttal vēṇṭum.
mākkacu mullar pūṉaip peyaraiyum pūṉaik kaṇṇaiyum paṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatiṉ (On the Name of the Cat and the Cat’s eye) cila pakutikaḷ varumāṟu:-
“Our domestic cat came to us from Egypt, where it had been tamed by a long process of kindness, or,  it may be, of worship. If no classical writer, Greek or Roman, do we find the cat as a domestic animal before the third century A.D. It is first mentioned by Caesarius, the physician, brother of Gregory, the theologian of Nazianzus, who died 369 A.D. He speaks of  ‘Kattai endrumo’, About the same time Palladius ............ writes “contra ... ponunt” .... it is clear that when Palladius wrote (fourth century A.D.) tame mustelae were still more common than cats, whether called cati or catti.
“Evagrius scholasticus (Hist. Eccl. 17, 23), about 600 A.D., speaks of katta as the common name of ‘ailouros’, here meant therefore, for cat....
“And Isidorus, his contemporary, expresses himself in the same sense when saying (12, 2, 38). ‘hune ............ catum ............ vocant.
“If we admit, in the absence of evidence to the contrary effect, that the tame cat came from Eqypt to Greece and Italy in the fourth century A.D., and that the shrewd little animal was called by the Romans catus, everything else becomes intelligible.
“In the ruins of Pompeii, where the bones of horses, dogs and goats have been found, no bones of cats have hitherto been discovered.....
“In the language of Romania no traces exist of the word catus, probably because at the time when that Romanic dialect became settled in Dacia, catus did not yet exist as a Latin name for cat.
The Romans did not transfer the name of Mustela to the cat, but by a kind of popular etymology, changed cattus into catus, and these two names, katta and catus, found their way afterwards into nearly all the languages of Europe”.
“We now come to the question, whether cat was known at an early time in India. The two principal words in Sanskrit for cat are ‘maÍrjaÍra’ and ‘vidaÍla’.
“MaÍrjaÍra means the cleaner, the cat being well known for its cleanliness.
“The second name for cat in Sanskrit is vidaÍla or bidaÍla....”
“It is difficult to analyse this word. I thought at first that it might be connected with vidaÍla (bidaÍla, in the At, AÍr. III. 1, 2, 6) which means cut in half, split in the middle, which would be a very appropriate term for a caÍt’s eye. But this would leave the lingual of  unaccounted for. In the UnaÍdi-Sutras (I. 117) it is derived from vid, to shout with the suffix aÍla. This suffix shows a certain analogy with aliga in marjaliya, another name for cat....”
“VaiduÍrya and VaiduÍrya, the very form that would best correspond to the Greek ailouros means in Sanskrit the cat’s eye. The cat is called man´i vaidurya lokana, ie. having eyes like the Vaid´uÍrya jewel. It is true that so ancient a grammarian as PaÍn´ini (IV. 3, 84) derives Vaid´uÍrya from viduÍra, ‘very distant,’ and that accordingly it is often spelt with a dental d. But this seems an after-thought. The transition of Vaid´aÍrya into Vaid´uÍrya is not impossible, even in Sanskrit, if we remember such parallel forms as dura and daviyas, sthuÍla, stahviÍyas, & c.....”
“It was objected by KaÍtyayana that PaÍn´ini rule (PaÍn. IV. 3, 84), according to which Vaid´uÍrya is formed from ViduÍra, must be wrong, because the Vaid´uÍrya jewel does not come from ViduÍra, but from BaÍlavaÍya, and is only cut or polished at ViduÍra....”  (What Can India Teach Us? - pp. 261 - 268.)
itiṉiṉṟu vaiṭūrya eṉpatu vitūra (mikat tolaivāṉa) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil pāṇiṉiyāl tirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu tavaṟu eṉpatu, teḷivākum. vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷaiyellām, poruntap poyttalāka vēṟu mūlaṅkāṭṭi maṟaippatu, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaḻakkamē.
viṭāraka eṉpatu piṭāraka (b) eṉṟum viṭāla eṉpatu piṭāla (b) eṉṟum, pakara mutaṟ collākat tirikkappaṭṭiruppatē vaṭacolliṉ piṉmaiyaik kāṭṭum.
piṭāla (bid´aÍla) eṉṉum colliṉkīḻ, “also written vid´aÍla, of doubtful origin” eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu tam camaṟkiruta āṅkila akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  (ta.ta.nā.co.)
peṭṭakam (1)
peṭṭakam - pēṭṭaka
piḷḷutal = piḷattal, virital, akalutal.
piḷ - piḻā = 1. vāyakaṉṟa ōlaikkuṭṭāṉ. (nellai vaḻakku). malarvāyp piḻāviṟ pulara vāṟṟi (perumpāṇ.276). 2. iṟaikūṭai. ōṅkunīrp piḻāvum (cilap. 10 : 111).  (va.va : 209).
peṭṭakam(2)
peṭṭakam - pēṭṭaka
peḷ - pēḻ = akaṉṟa. pēḻvāy = akaṉṟavāy. pēḻvāy...... pēymakaḷ (tirumuka. 47).
pēḻ - pēḻai = 1. peṭṭi. aruṅkalap pēḻai (cīvaka. 557).
2. kūṭai. 3. marakkalam (Arc).  (va.va : 210).
peṭṭi
peṭṭi - pēṭṭ
vaṭamoḻiyil ekaramiṉmaiyāṟ peṭṭi pēṭṭi eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
peṭṭi - peṭṭakam = 1. peṭṭi. āṅkilaṅku maḷapparum peṭṭakam (tiruvālavā. 27 : 22). 2. kaṭṭuppeṭṭi.  (va.va : 209).
peyar
peyar eṉṉum col mutalāvatu ōr āṉai viḷittaṟkō oru poruḷaic cuṭṭuvataṟkō iṭappaṭṭa collaip potuppaṭak kuṟippatākum. atu piṉṉar ākupeyarāy oru peyarāl viḷikkappaṭum, āḷaiyum kuṟikkum.
iruvar mūvar mutaliya eṇṇaṭi uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷai iraṇṭu pēr mūṉṟu pēr eṉṟu colvatu ulakavaḻakku. pēr eṉpatu peyar eṉpataṉ marūu.  (col. 35).
peyar viṉai
peyar viṉai iṭai uri eṉṉum nālvakaic coṟkaḷuḷ mutaliraṇṭē talaimaiyāṉavai.
peyar eṉpatu āḷaiyum viṉai eṉpatu  āḷiṉ ceyalaiyum kuṟippaṉa.  (col. 34).
periya purāṇam
piṟappāṟ ciṟappillai : ellāvakuppārum vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriyar; illaṟattālum vīṭu peṟalām eṉṉum tamiḻk koḷkai kaḷaiyum kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol vālaṟivaṉ, naṟṟāḷ toḻāar eṉiṉ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkaruttaiyum; aṟivu, aṉpu, toṇṭu īkam (tiyākam) eṉpaṉavē vīṭupēṟṟu nālvāyil eṉpataiyum naṭaimuṟaic ceytiyāl viḷakkik kāṭṭuvatum nāṭṭuvatum periyapurāṇam oṉṟē.  (ta. i. va. 81).
periyōraic cuṭṭāmai
periyōraik kaiyiṉāṟ cuṭṭuvatu avamatippeṉṟu karuti aṅṅaṉam ceyvatillai. cuṭṭa nērnta viṭattuk kaiyāṟ cuṭṭātu collāṟ cuṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. aṅṅaṉam collāṟ cuṭṭum pōtu avarkaḷ ivarkaḷ eṉṉum paṭarkkaic collālaṉṟi, nīr nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum muṉṉilaic collāṟ cuṭṭak kūṭāteṉṟu koṇṭu, tāṅkaḷ eṉṉum paṭarkkaic collāṟ cuṭṭuvar. atuvum nāḷaṭaivil muṉṉilait taṉmaiyaṭaintu viṭṭateṉṟum, mikap periyōrkku accol pōtāteṉṟum karuti, maṭattampirāṉārai aṅkuttaiyeṉṉum paṭarkkaic collāl muṉṉilaip paṭuttik kūṟuvatu civamaṭa marapu.
aṅkuttai (aṅkuṟṟai) yeṉpatu avviṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum col. tampirāṉoṭu uraiyāṭupavar nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil iccollai vaḻaṅkuvar.  (col. 113).
peru
peru (ku) - va. prah (b) - i. vē.
pal - paru - peru - peruku.
peruṅkatai - pruhat katā.
peruvuṭaiyār = pruhat īsvara  (va.va : 210).
peruntiṇai
āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya iruvarukkum kātal illātiruppatu allatu oruvaraiyoruvar valintu koḷvatu. ....... poruntāk kāmam eṉṉum collap peṟum.
peruntiṇai perumpakuti. makkaḷ maṇa muṟaikaḷuṭ perum pālāṉavaṟṟait taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟpatāl, poruntāk kāmam peruntiṇaiyeṉappaṭṭatu. kavarvu maṇaṅkaḷum, iyaṟ kaikku māṟāṉa ellāp puṇarcci vakaikaḷum peruntiṇaiyē. kātaloṭu peruntāmaiyum neṟiyoṭu poruntāmaiyum paṟṟi, poruntiṇai poruntākkāmam eṉappaṭṭatu.  (ta.ti.16).
perun tēva matam
aintiṇait teyva vaṇakkaṅkaḷuḷ, iraṇṭē matamāka vaḷarcci yaṭaintaṉa. cēyōṉ vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu civa matamum, māyōṉ vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu tirumāl matamum, tōṉṟiṉa. viṇṇulaka vēntaṉ koḷkaiyiṉpiṉ eṅkum niṟainta iṟaivaṉ koḷkai ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, tīyiṉ kūṟākac civaṉ eṉṟum nīriṉ kūṟāka māl (māyōṉ) eṉṟum, peyariṭṭu iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
(1) civamatam
iṟaivaṉ peyar
cēyōṉ, civaṉ eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum, orē mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟic civantavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷaik koṇṭaṉa.
cul - cuḷ. cul - culli = aṭuppu, aṭukkaḷai. cuḷ - cuḷḷai = kalamuñ ceṅkalum cuṭum aṭuppup pōṉṟa kāḷavāy.
cul - cel - cēl = cenniṟak keṇṭai mīṉ.
cēlviḻi = cēl mīṉ pōlum cevvari paranta peṇṇiṉ kaṇ.
neruppiṉ niṟam civappātalāl, neruppiṉ peyar cenniṟattaik kuṟittatu.
o. nō : eri = neruppu, civappu. erimalar = 1. civanta murukka malar. erimalarp pavaḷac cevvāy (cīvaka. 602). 2. centāmarai. celva ṉerimalarc cēvaṭiyai (cīvaka. 2741).
cel - ceḷ - ceṭṭu - ceṭṭi = 1. civanta aṭiyaiyuṭaiya veṭcic ceṭi. ceṅkāl veṭci (tirumuru. 21). 2. murukaṉ.
ceḷ - cey - ceyyavaṉ = 1. civantavaṉ. 2. katiravaṉ. 3. cevvāy. ceyyaṉ = murukaṉ.
ceyyāḷ (ceyyavaḷ) = ceṅkōlat tirumakaḷ.
ceyyāṉ = civantavaṉ, cempūrāṉ.
cey - cēy = civappu, cevvāy, murukaṉ.
cēy - cēyatu - cēytu = civantatu. cēytu - cētu.
cētu + ā = cētā. cētu + āmpal = cētāmpal.
cēy - cēyaṉ - cēyāṉ = cenniṟattāṉ.
cēyavaṉ = cevvāy, murukaṉ.
cēyōṉ = murukaṉ, civaṉ.
cēy - cē. cēttal = civattal.
cē = civappu, cēṅkōṭṭai.
cē - cēttu = civappu.
cēttu - cēntu = civappu, tī, acōku.
cēntu - cēntaṉ = civantavaṉāṉa murukaṉ.
cēntaṉ - cēntu.
o.nō : vēntaṉ - vēntu, murukaṉ - muruku.
cēntu + il = cēntil - centil.
cēntu + ūr = cēntūr - centūr (tiruccentūr).
cey - ceymmai - cemmai - cevvai.
cem - cev - ceva - cevappu.
ceva - civa - civappu - cikappu.
civa - cival - civalai.
civa - civam - civaṉ = civantavaṉ, neruppiṉ kūṟāka niṉṟu ulakattai iyakkum iṟaivaṉ.
tīvaṇṇaṉ, antivaṇṇaṉ, aḻalvaṇṇaṉ, māṇikkak kūttaṉ mutaliya civaṉ peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
civaṉ mālai
kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya koṉṟai mālai. koṉṟai vēyntōṉ - koṉṟai vēntaṉ.
civaṉūrti
kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya (veṇ) kāḷai. ataṉ veṇṇiṟam tūymai kuṟittatu.
civaṉ paṭaikkalam
muk kavarc cūlam. ataṉāṟ civaṉukkuc cūli eṉṟu oru peyar. kaṇicciyum (maḻuvum) civaṉ paṭai. ataṉāl avaṉukkuk kaṇicciyāṉ (maḻuvāḷi) eṉṟum peyar.
civaṉirukkai
vīṭṭulakamum veḷḷimalaiyum tirukkōvilkaḷum toṇṭaruḷḷamum.
civaṉ kuṇam
taṉvayattaṉātal, tūya vuṭampiṉaṉātal, iyaṟkaiyuṇar viṉaṉātal, muṟṟu muṇartal, iyalpākavē kaṭṭukkaḷiṉ (pācaṅkaḷiṉ) nīṅkutal, pēraruḷuṭaimai, muṭivilāṟṟa luṭaimai, varampiliṉpa muṭaimai eṉṉum eṭṭu.
civaṉ toḻil
paṭaippu kāppu aḻippu eṉṉum mūṉṟu.
civaṉ vaṭivam 
ai vakai.
(1)	koṉṟai mālai yaṇintu cūla mēntik kāḷai yūrntu cellum cemmēṉiyaṉ.
(2)	ammaiyappaṉ
ellā vuyirkaṭkum tāytantai pōṉṟavaṉ. valappuṟam tantaikkūṟum iṭappuṟam tāykkūṟum koṇṭataṉāl, maṅkai paṅkaṉ allatu mātoru pākaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭupavaṉ.
	tantaikkūṟṟup peyar	tāykkūṟṟup peyar
	civaṉ	civai
	iṟaivaṉ	iṟaivi
	tēvaṉ	tēvi
	paraṉ	parai
	appaṉ	ammai
	aiyaṉ	aiyai
	malaimakaṉ	malaimakaḷ
	cūli	cūliṉi
malaimakaṉ malaimakaḷ eṉṉum irupeyarum, malaivāḻ teyvam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvaṉa.
ammaiyappaṉ vaṭivam, ilaṅkam (liṅkam) eṉṉum uru vaṭivilum, ōm eṉṉum oli vaṭivilum, piḷḷaiyār cuḻiyeṉṉum ukara vari vaṭivilum, kuṟikkap peṟum.
ilakku = kuṟi. ilakku - ilakkam - ilaṅkam.
ammaiyappaṉ vaṭivu, iṟaivaṉiṉ uṇmai vaṭivaik kāṇa muṭiyāta illaṟavāṇarāṉa potu makkaṭkē. uyarnta aṟivupaṭaitta cittarum muṉivarum, iṟaivaṉiṉ āṟṟalaiyē peṇ kūṟāka uruvakippar.
ilaṅka vaṭivu nilaiyil, iṟaivaṉāṟṟalaik kuṟikkum aṭittaḷattiṟku āvuṭaiyāḷ eṉṟum, mēl niṟkum ilaṅkattiṟku āvuṭaiyappaṉ eṉṟum, peyar. āvuṭaiyāḷ eṉpatu āvuṭaiyammai, āvuṭaiyācci eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
(3) kuravaṉ
takutiyuḷḷavarkku, antaṇa (aruḷ muṉivaṉ) vaṭivil vantu uyaraṟi vuṟuttum parama āciriyaṉ.
muṉivaṉ kōlattiṟ caṭaiyuṭaimaiyāl, civaṉ caṭaiyaṉ, caṭaiyappaṉ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟāṉ. puram = uyarnilaik kaṭṭiṭam, aḵtuḷḷavūr. puram - param = mēlulakam, vīṭṭulakam. param - paramaṉ = mēlōṉ, iṟaivaṉ. param - varam - varaṉ 
(kuṟaḷ. 24).
(4) eṇ vaṭivaṉ (aṭṭamūrtti)
nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, veḷi, katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, ātaṉ (āṉmā) eṉṉum eṇ poruḷ vaṭiviṉaṉ. eṅkum niṟaintiruppatu paṟṟi eṇṭicaiyum civaṉukku eṇ kaiyākac collappaṭum. ataṉāl eṇṭōḷaṉ (piṅ.) eṉṟu peyar. katiravaṉ tiṅkaḷ tī eṉṉum muc cuṭarum civaṉukku mukkaṇṇākak kūṟappaṭum. ataṉāl avaṉukku mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu oru peyar.
(5) naṭavaracaṉ
uyirāṉatu niṉaivu colvu ceyal eṉṉum muttoḻiṟ paṭumāṟu, uṭampiṉ naṭuvuḷ toṅki niṉṟu iyaṅkum neñcat tuṭippai naṭamāka vuruvakittu, atu pōṉṟu ellā uyiriṉaṅkaḷum (paṭaippu kāppu aḻippu eṉṉum) tōṉṟal vāḻtal maṟaital ākiya muttoḻiṟpaṭumāṟu, pērulakap paraveḷiyuṭampiṉ naṭuvil niṉṟu iṟaivaṉ neñcam iyaṅkuvatākak kōṭittu (pāvittu), avaṉ mut toḻilaiyum iṉpa naṭamāka vuruvakittu, avaṉai naṭaruḷ talaivaṉākki, naṭavaracaṉ (naṭanāyakaṉ, āṭavallāṉ) eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar. ivvuruvakam, aṇṭattiṟ kottatu piṇṭattiṟkum. eṉṉum uṇmaiyai, piṇṭattiṟ kottu aṇṭattiṟkum eṉak kāṭṭiya vāṟām.
kumarināṭirunta paṇṭaik kālattil, kumarimalaikkum paṉi malaikkum naṭuviṭattilirunta tillai nakaraip pāṇṭiyaṉ pārukku neñcat tāvākak koṇṭu, aṅku naṭavaracaṉ tirup paṭimai niṟka ampalam amaittāṉ. naṭavaracap paṭimaikaḷ niṟkum kōvilkaḷellām, ampala meṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. ampalam āṭaraṅku. ampalak kūttaṉ, maṉṟāṭi eṉpaṉa tillaic civaṉ peyarkaḷ. pēram palam ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, āṭampalam ciṟṟampalam eṉappaṭṭatu. appeyarē iṉṟu citamparam eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ciṟṟampalam poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭapiṉ, poṉṉampalam eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉṉarē maṇiyampalam veḷḷiyampalam ceppampalam mutaliyavai tōṉṟiṉa.
kaṭpulaṉākak kāṇum naṭavaracaṉ paṭimai, akak karaṇa vaḷarcci yaṭaiyāta potu makkaṭ kuriyatē. ciṟanta aṟivarāṉa aṭiyār, tiṟanta veḷiyaiyē ampalamākak karutuvar. appara veḷiyampalamē tirai nīkkik kāṭṭappaṭum. atuvē ciṟṟampala marumam (citampara rakasiyam) eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu.
naḷi - naṭi - naṭam, naṭaṉam. naṭam - naṭṭam - naṭṭuvaṉ.
naṭṭam - va. nrutta, nāṭya,
ummutal = kūṭutal. um - am - amai. amaital = 1. neruṅkutal, aṭartal. vaḻaiyamai cāral (malaipaṭu. 161). 2. kūṭutal. amai - avai = kūṭṭam, kuḻām. 3. poruntutal. pāṅkamai patalai (kantapu. tiruppara. 9). 4. niṟaital. uṟup pamaintu (kuṟaḷ. 791).
am - ampu - ampal = 1. kūṭutal, kūṭṭam. o. nō : um - kum - kumpu - kumpal. ampal - ampalam = kūṭṭam, avai, kūṭumiṭam, maṉṟam.
2. kuvital, kuvinta arumpu allatu moṭṭu. ampal eṉpatu mukiḻ mukiḻttal. (iṟai. 22, urai). o. nō : kum - kumpu - kūmpu. kum - kumi - kuvi.
3. arumpu pōṉṟa cila rurai paḻi. ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ (tol. kaḷa. 46). ampalum alarum kaḷavu. (iṟai. 22).
ma. ampalam, ampala, tu. ampila, va. ampara.
vaṭacollil lakaram rakaramākat tirintiruttal kāṇka. at tiricollaiyē ampalam eṉṉum iyaṟ colliṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka akaramutaliyiṟ kāṭṭi yiruppatu, iṟṟait tamiḻariṉ iḻivāṉa aṭimait taṉattaiyē kāṭṭum.
civa vaḻipāṭṭu vaṭivam
civaṉuṭaiya ai vakai vaṭivukaḷuḷḷum, potu makkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟ kēṟṟatu ammaiyappa vaṭivamē.
civa vaḻipāṭṭu muṟai
kālaiyiṟ kuḷittu, uṇṇumuṉ, tīya niṉaiviṉṟi amainta vuḷḷattuṭaṉ akka mālai yaṇintu tirunīṟupūcic civap paṭimai muṉ niṉṟu, iyalum pōtellām tēṅkāyuṭaittu vāḻaip paḻattuṭaṉ paṭaittu, naṟumpukai kāṭṭip pūccāttik kai kuvittu, (ōm eṉṉum muḷai mantirattai muṉṉiṭṭa) civa pōṟṟi eṉṉun tiruvain teḻuttai ōti, palvēṟu pōṟṟit toṭarkaḷāl vaḻutti, neṭuñcāṇkiṭai vaṇakkañ ceytu eḻuntiruppatē ciṟanta muṟaippaṭṭa civa vaḻipāṭākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
uḻavarum uḻaippāḷikaḷum toḻilāḷarum kālaiyil civa vaṇakkam maṭṭum ceyya muṭiyum. tirunāṭkaḷilum tiruviḻāk kālattilum ellārum kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyvar.
civaṉai yaṭaintavariṉ mum mācum erintu cāmpalāy viṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai uṇarttaṟkē, tirunīṟu pūcappaṭṭatu. atu pūti (piṅ.). eṉṟum collappaṭum. puḻuti - pūti = tūḷ, nīṟu, tirunīṟu.
tēṅkā yuṭaittu ataṉ nīraic cinti muṟiyaip paṭaippatu, vaḻipaṭuvōṉ taṉ tīviṉai niṉaintu maṉamuṭaintu kaṇṇīr cintit tūymaippaṭa vēṇṭumeṉpataiyum; vāḻaip paḻattaip paṭaippatu, ataṉ cataipōla uḷḷam kaṉintu meṉmaiyum iṉimaiyum peṟa vēṇṭumeṉpataiyum, kuṟippāka vuṇarttum.
civaṉ kōviṟ pūcakar, kurukkaḷ, paṇṭāram, ōtuvār, pulavar, pōṟṟi eṉap pala peyar peṟṟaṉar.
mūvēntarum mutaṟkaṇ civaṉaṭiyārāyiruntu, piṉṉar āriyar (pirāmaṇar) vantu muttirumēṉik koḷkai pukuttiya piṉ, iṭaiyiṭai orōvoruvar māliyattaiyum (vaiṇavattai yum) taḻuviṉar. tammaip pōṉṟē tām vaḻipaṭu teyvamum ēṟṟamāka iruntu iṉpuṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu, tamakkuriya ciṟappai yellām tam teyvattiṟkum ceytaṉar. acciṟappukkaḷ teyvattiṉ oppilā vuyarvu nōkkip paṉmaṭiyuyarvākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
vāṉaḷāvum eḻunilaik kūṭa kōpuramum māṭa maṇṭapaṅkaḷum cuṟṟu matiluṅ koṇṭa tiruvuṇṇāḻikait tirukkōvil, ūrvalattiṟkuc ciṟanta yāṉai kutiraiyoṭṭaka veṇ kāḷaikaḷ, kuṭai koṭi mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, karuvūla kaḷañciya paṇṭacālaikaḷ, palvakai aṇikaṅkaḷ (vākaṉaṅkaḷ), capparaṅkaḷ, vilai yuyarvum ōviya vēlaippāṭṭuc ciṟappumuḷḷa poṉṉāṭai poṉmaṇi yaṇikaḷ, tirukkuḷam, pūṅkā, tiruppaḷḷiyeḻucci yiṉṉiyam, tirumuḻukkāṭṭu, tiruviṉṉamutu paṭaippu, tirunāḷ āravāra ūrvala ulākkaḷ, āṇṭu tōṟum (mut taṭṭu mutal eḻu taṭṭu varai koṇṭa) tērōṭṭa teppat tērt tiruviḻākkaḷ, nila māṉipaṅkaḷ, iyavar, kāvalar ēvalar mēṟpārvalarākiya paṇimakkaḷ, mutaliya pal vakaic ciṟappum vēntarālum maṉṉarālum peruñ celvarālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. icaiyālum naṭattālum iṟai vaṉai iṉpuṟutta nālvakaippaṭṭa ellāk karuviyicaiyum āṭṭum pāṭṭum kōvilkaḷilum tiruvulākkaḷilum nikaḻntaṉa. itaṟkeṉṟē pāṭakarum kaṇikaiyarum naṭṭuvarum muṭṭuvarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar.
koṇ muṭipu (cittāntam)
talaivaṉ taḷaiyaṉ taḷai eṉṉum mūṉṟum toṭakkamilā mupporuḷkaḷ. kāmam (kāmiyam) vekuḷi (āṇavam) mayakkam (māyai) eṉat taḷai mūvakaittu. terintum teriyāmalum ceyyum iruvakait tīviṉaiyum nīkki iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭiṉ, illaṟattālum tuṟavaṟattālum irupālārum vīṭu peṟalām. vīṭṭulakam civaṉulakam (civa vulakam) eṉappaṭum. vīṭu peṟum varai ātaṉ piṟavik kaṭaluḷ aḻuntum. nilaittiṇai, nīrvāḻi, ūri, paṟavai, vilaṅku, māntaṉ, tēvaṉ eṉap piṟavi eḻu vakai.
ituvē civak koṇ muṭipu.
aṉṉīṟum āṉīṟum oṉṟaṉ pālaiyum uṇarttum.
e - ṭu : taṉiyaṉ (taṉippāṭal), taṭiyaṉ (pūcaṇikkāy), alavaṉ, maṭaiyāṉ, kuṇṭaṭiyaṉ, kaṭuvaṉ.
kāmam vekuḷi mayakka mivaimūṉṟaṉ
nāmaṅ keṭakkeṭum nōy.  (kuṟaḷ. 370).
eṉpatil, mum mācu kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kāmaṅ keṭutal eṉpatu, illaṟattiṟkup piṟaṉil viḻaiyā maiyum piṟaṉ poruḷ veḵkāmaiyum; tuṟavaṟattiṟku ācai aṭiyōṭoḻital.
teriyātu mitippiṉum tīccuṭutal pōla, teriyātu ceyyum tīviṉaiyum tīṅku viḷaikkum eṉpatu koḷkai. nalviṉai yāṟ kēṭillai.
nōyellām nōyceytār mēlavām nōyceyyār
nōyiṉmai vēṇṭu pavar.  (kuṟaḷ. 320).
iruḷcēr iruviṉaiyum cērā iṟaivaṉ
poruḷcēr pukaḻpurintār māṭṭu.  (kuṟaḷ. 5).
eṉpatil, iruviṉaiyeṉṟatu terintum teriyātum ceyyum tīviṉaikaḷaiyē.
tacarataṉ vēṭṭaiyāṭiya pōtu teriyātu koṉṟa ciṟuvaṉiṉ kuruṭarāṉa peṟṟōr iṭṭa vaivē (cāpamē), piṉṉar irāmaṉaik kāṭṭiṟ kēkac ceytatu eṉpatu, naṭunilaiyaṟiñar karuttu.
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkkai yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 46).
eṉpataṉāl, illaṟattālum vīṭupēṟuṇṭeṉpatē tamiḻar koḷkaiyām.
āṟṟiṉ oḻukki aṟaṉiḻukkā ilvāḻkkai
nōṟpāriṉ nōṉmai yuṭaittu.  (kuṟaḷ. 48)
ātalāl, tuṟavaṟattāṉ poṟaiyiṉum illaṟattāṉ poṟaiyē peritām.
pēriṉpa vīṭṭaip peṟṟatākap periya purāṇaṅ kūṟum civaṉaṭiyāruṭ perumpālār, illaṟattil niṉṟavarē.
iruvakai yaṟa vāḻkkaiyaiyum iṟaivaṉ ēṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatai yuṇarttaṟkē, avaṉukku ammaiyappa vaṭivum antaṇa vaṭivum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
	meyp poruḷiyal
cuvaiyoḷi yūṟōcai nāṟṟameṉ ṟaintiṉ
vakaiterivāṉ kaṭṭē yulaku.  (kuṟaḷ. 28).
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ākavē,
nilam nīr tī vaḷi veḷi eṉṉum pūtaṅkaḷ aintum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷāṉa nāṟṟam cuvai oḷi ūṟu ōcai eṉṉum aintum, avaṟṟai yaṟiyum karuvikaḷāṉa mūkku nāvu kaṇ mey cevi eṉṉum aṟivup poṟikaḷ aintum, palvēṟu viṉai ceyyum kai kāl vāy eruvāy karuvāy eṉṉum karumap poṟi kaḷ aintum, mati uḷḷam (cittam) maṉam nāṉuṇarvu eṉṉum akak karaṇaṅkaḷ nāṉkum, avaṟṟaik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷai āyntaṟiyum ātaṉum, ataṉ viṉaikaṭ kellām iṭam pōṉṟa nilaikkaḷamākiya kālamum tūṇṭukōlāṉa ūḻum ākiya iraṇṭum, ellāvaṟṟaiyum iyakkum iṟaivaṉum, āka mottam meyp poruḷkaḷ (tattuvaṅkaḷ) irupat teṭṭām.
civamata virivaḷarcci
civa matam, nāḷaṭaiviṟ palvēṟu vaṇakkaṅkaḷaiyum, iṟutiyil tirumāliyattaiyum taṉṉuṭ koṇṭatu.
nāka vaṇakkattār nāka vuruvait tam talaiyucciyil aṇintiruntaṉar. avaraic civaṉiyarākkaṟku, civaṉ muṭimītum nāka vuruviruppatākap paṭimai yamaittu viṭṭaṉar. atōṭu, civaṉ pāmpukaḷaiyē palvēṟu aṇikaḷāka aṇin tiruppatākavum kāṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, nākappaṉ, pāmpaṇiyaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
pāmpalaṅ kārap paraṉ  (tirukkō. 11).
tamiḻakam muḻuvatum vēntarālum toḻappaṭṭa kāḷi, civaṉ tēviyākkappaṭṭāḷ. atu tiruvālaṅkāṭṭut tirunaṭap pōril avaḷ tōlviyuṟṟataṉ viḷaivākak kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. āṇpāṟ kēṟṟa ūrtta naṭaṉam peṇpāṟ kēṟkāmaiyāl, kāḷiyaṭiyārum atai ottuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
uvar - ivar. uvar - ūr. ūrtal - ēṟi naṭattutal allatu cellutal, uyartal. ūr - ūrttam - va. ūrttavam (uÍrdhva).
civaiyuṅ kāḷiyum oṉṟāṉatiṉāl, civai nīli (karuppi) yeṉavum, kāḷi iṟaivi (iṟaivaṉ tēvi) yeṉavum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. ammai aiyai eṉpaṉa iruvarukkum potup peyarkaḷ. ammai - ammā - va. ampā.
kāḷi civaṉ tēviyāṉa piṉ, kāḷiyappaṉ, pēycciyappaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ civaṉukkut tōṉṟiṉa.
viṇṇaka vāḻvu nilaiyaṟṟatāy eḻu piṟaviyuḷ aṭaṅkiṉatiṉālum, viṇṇaka vēntaṉukku maḻai peyvikkum atikāramē yiruntataṉālum, nilaiyāṉa vīṭṭulakat talaivaṉum ellām vallavaṉumāṉa civaṉai vaḻipaṭum vaḻipāṭṭuḷ, vēntaṉ vaṇakkam maṟain toḻintatu.
iṟutiyil, tirumālum peṇṇāka māṟic civa perumāṉiṉ iṭappākat tamarntāṉ eṉṉum katai yeḻuntatu. ari eṉṉum tirumāl peyar itaṉāl tōṉṟiyirukkalām. aram = civappu. araṉ = civaṉ. araṉ - ari (peṇ pāl.). paccaiyaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ piṟkālattatu.
tirumāl matamum oru taṉi matamākat toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu vantiruppiṉum, civaṉukkē iṟaivaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṇmai yum, piṟavā yākkaip periyōṉ kōyilum eṉṉum cilappatikārak kūṟṟum (5 : 179), civa matattiṉ talaimaiyai yuṇarttum. ulaka mellām uṭaiyavar eṉpatu paṟṟi, civaṉukkup peruvuṭaiyār eṉṟum peyar. ēḻuṭaiyāṉ poḻil (tirukkō. 6).
	(2) tirumāl matam
tirumāl niṟamum peyarum
ellām valla iṟaivaṉaiyē, mullai nilat teyva aṭippaṭaiyil nīriṉ kūṟākak koṇṭu, nīraip poḻiyum mukil niṟam paṟṟi māyōṉ (māyaṉ, māyavaṉ) eṉṟum, mālōṉ (māl, mālaṉ, mālavaṉ) eṉṟum, kār vaṇṇaṉ (kariyaṉ, kariyavaṉ) eṉṟum, mukil kūṭum vāṉiṟam paṟṟi nīla vaṇṇaṉ (maṇi vaṇṇaṉ, kaṭal vaṇṇaṉ) eṉṟum, peyariṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉar orucār tamiḻar. māl eṉṉum peyar aṭaiyaṭuttut tirumāl, perumāl eṉa vaḻaṅkum. perumāl - perumāḷ.
mukilukkum atu niṟkum vāṉiṟkum viṇ eṉṉum peyaruṇmaiyāl, appeyarai aṭiyākak koṇṭa viṇṭu eṉṉum peyarum tirumālukku ēṟpaṭṭatu.
viḷḷutal = virital, tiṟattal, veḷiyātal.
viḷ - viṇ = veḷi, vāṉam, mukil, mēlulakam.
viṇ - viṇṭu = veḷi, vāṉam, mukil, tirumāl, mēlulakam.
paccai niṟam nīlaniṟattiṟku iṉamātalāl, paccaiyaṉ, paccaiyappaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum tirumāliṟkut tōṉṟiṉa.
tirumā lirukkai
viṇṭu + nakar = viṇṇakar = 1. tirumāl kōvil, tirumāl vīṭṭulakam.
viṇṇakar - viṇṇakaram.
parama patam = paramaṉ patintirukkum iṭam, vīṭṭulakam, tirumālulakam.
tirumāl malaraṇi
mullai nilattiṟkuriya tuḻāy mālai.
tirumāl ūrti
mullai nilattiṟkuriya kaluḻaṉ.
kaluḻtal = kalattal. kaluḻ - kaluḻaṉ = veṇṭalaiyum cevvuṭampumāka iruniṟaṅ kalanta paṟavai.
kaluḻaṉ - va. karuṭa (garud´a).
tirumāl paṭai
caṅku, cakkaram, vil, vāḷ, taṇṭam eṉṉum aintu.
aimpaṭai yuruvākac ceyta aimpaṭait tāliyeṉṉum aṇiyai, mata vēṟupāṭiṉṟic ciṟu piḷḷaikaṭkut toṉṟu toṭṭuk kāppāka aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tāli kaḷaintaṉṟu milaṉē  (puṟam. 77).
poṉṉuṭait tāli yeṉmakaṉ  (akam. 54).
amaḷit tuñcu maimpaṭait tālik
 kutalaic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭaip putalvar  (maṇi. 7 : 56-7)
aḻakiya vaimpaṭaiyu māramuṅ koṇṭu  (periyāḻ. tiru. 1. 4 : 5)
aimpaṭai cataṅkai cātti  (periya pu. taṭuttāṭ. 4).
taṉpaṭaika ḷāṉa tiru vaimpaṭai tarittē  (kaliṅ. ava. 9)
tāli yaimpaṭai taḻuvu mārpiṭai  (kampa. nāṭu. 58).
aimpaṭai mārpiṟ kāṇēṉ  (tiruviḷai. 39 : 25).
tirumāl nilaikaḷ
vāṉiṟ kaluḻaṉūrtalum, nilattil niṟṟaliruttal kiṭattalum.
kiṭattaṟkuc ciṟanta iṭam tiruvaraṅkam.
tiruvaraṅkam - va. srīraṅka.
tirumāl toḻil
paṭaippu kāppu aḻippu eṉṉum mūṉṟu.
ulakam yāvaiyum tāmuḷa vākkalum
nilaipe ṟuttalum nīkkalum nīṅkalā
alaki lāviḷai yāṭṭuṭai yāravar
talaivar aṉṉavark kēcaraṇ nāṅkaḷē  (kampa. ciṟappup pā. 1).
tirumāl vaḻipāṭu
akka mālaikkut talai māṟākat tuḷacimaṇi mālai yaṇintu, tirunīṟṟiṟkut talaimāṟākat tirumaṇ kāppuc cātti, civa pōṟṟi eṉpataṟkut talaimāṟāka māl pōṟṟi allatu māya pōṟṟi eṉṟu ōti, piṟavakai kaḷillām civaṉiyar pōṉṟē ceytu vaḻipaṭal.
civaṉiya aracar civaṉ kōvilkaṭkuc ceytatu pōṉṟu, māliya aracarum tirumāl kōvilkaṭkuc ciṟappuc ceytu māṉiyam viṭṭaṉar. iru cārāruḷḷum oru cilar, camayap potu nōkkarāyiruntu iru matak kōvilkaṭkum iyaṉṟatu ceytaṉar. tirumāl kōviṟ pūcakar tiruvaṭi piṭippāṉ, nampi eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉar.
koṇ muṭipu
perumpālum civaṉiyattai yottatē. iṟaivaṉ peyarum vīṭṭulakap peyarum cila coṟkaḷum maṭṭum vēṟṟumai.
meyp poruḷiyal
civaṉiyattai yottatē.
civaṉiyamum māliyamum, ellām valla iṟaivaṉāṉa orē teyvattai vevvēṟu peyarāl vaṇaṅkum mataṅkaḷātalāl, civaṉun tirumālum vevvēṟu teyvameṉṟō, avariṭai ēṟṟat tāḻvuṇṭeṉṟō, kumari nāṭṭār koḷḷa villai.
ariyuñ civaṉum oṇṇu, aṟiyātavaṉ vāyil maṇṇu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
araṉatika ṉulakaḷanta variyatika
 ṉeṉṟuraikku maṟivilōrkkup
 parakaticeṉ ṟaṭaivariya paricēpōṟ
 pukalariya paṇpiṟṟāmāl  (kampa. nāṭaviṭ. 24).
eṉṟār kampar. iru matattār piḷḷaikaṭkum aimpaṭaittāli aṇiyappaṭṭu vantatum, itai valiyuṟuttum.
muṟkālattil, āṭavarum peṇṭir pōṉṟē talaimuṭiyai nīḷa vaḷarttuk koṇṭai muṭittaṉar. nōyillā nilaiyil muṭi veṭṭuvatu, avamāṉat tōlvikkum tuyarattiṟkum aṭai yāḷamākak karutappaṭṭatu. iṟaivaṉ muṉ tammait tāḻttu vataṟkē, teyvap paṟṟāḷar tirukkōvilkaṭkuc ceṉṟu tam talaiyai maḻittuk koṇṭaṉar. avvaḻakkam iṉṟum ōraḷavu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, aṭimaikaṭkuk kātil tuḷaiyiṭuvatu akkālattu vaḻakkam. teyvappaṟṟu mikka celvarum, tammai iṟaiyaṭimai yarākak koṇṭu, tam kātil tuḷaiyiṭṭaṉar. atu tūrntu pōkāmaip poruṭṭē, kaṭukkaṉ kuṇṭalam mutaliya kātaṇi kaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar.
mantira veḻuttum cakkaramum poṟitta takaṭṭai uṭkoṇṭa, kuḷicam pōṉṟa teyvak kāppāṉa muṉkaiyaṇiyē, iṉṟu kāppu eṉa vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
nākamum nākappaṭamum pōṉṟa uruvamainta ellā aṇikaḷum, toṭakkattil nāka vaṇakkattaik kāṭṭiyavaiyē.
paṇṭai aimpaṭaittāliyum caṅkuttāliyum āmaittāliyum pōṉṟu, iṉṟu kuṟukkai (ciluvai)t tāliyum mūvilait tāliyum kiṟittavap peṇṭirāl aṇiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam teyvap paṟṟāl aṇiyappaṭum urukkaḷellām, poṉṉālum maṇiyālum iyaṉṟu, aḻakiṟkāka aṇiyum aṇikalaṅkaḷākavē māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟum iyaṟkai vaṭivilēyē aṇiyappaṭa vēṇṭiya akka maṇiyum, orō oruvariṭaip poṟ pūccup peṟṟuviṭukiṉṟatu.
(3) kaṭavuṭ camayam
ūr pēr kālam iṭam vaṇṇam vaṭivam pāl paruvam mutaliya varaiyaṟai yiṉṟi, eṅkum niṟaintu, ellārkkum ellā vaṟṟiṟkum potuvāy, ellām vallatāy, eṉṟum māṟā tirukkum param poruḷai uḷḷattil akak kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭu toḻutu, mukkaraṇat tūymaiyuṭaṉ oḻukuvatē kaṭavuṭ camayamām.
pēr eṉṟatu, iyaṟ peyarum paṇpu paṟṟiya ciṟappup peyarum pōṉṟavaṟṟai.
kaṭavuṭ camayam, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉṉum iruvakai aṟa vāḻkkaikkum potuvām. ataṉāṟ peṟum pēriṉpamum, immai maṟumai yiraṇṭiṟkum potuvām. immaiyiṟ peṟuvatu uṭalirunta vīṭu eṉṟum, maṟumaiyiṟ peṟuvatu uṭaliṟanta vīṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
ellāvaṟṟaiyum kaṭantu niṟpavaṉ kaṭavuḷ. ellā vulakaṅkaḷai yum āḷvatāl āṇṭavaṉ eṉṟum, eṅkun taṅkiyiruppatāl iṟaivaṉ eṉṟum, ellā vuyirkaṭkum uṇavaip pakuttaḷippa tāl pakavaṉ eṉṟum, peyar peṟuvaṉ. āṇṭavaṉ mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷ perun tēva mataṅkaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvatāl, kaṭavuṭ camayattiṟkut taṉic ciṟappākavuriya teyvap peyar kaṭavuḷ eṉpatē.
teyvam eṉṉum peyar, iṭattiṟkēṟpa mūvakait teyvattai yuṅ kuṟikkum. teyvam uṇāvē (tol. 964). eṉpatiṟ ciṟu teyvattaiyum, naṇṇiyapoṉ ṉampalattē naṭampuriyun teyvam (irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pāṭal) eṉpatiṟ perun tēvaṉaiyum, teyvam niṉṟu kollum eṉṉum paḻamoḻi yiṟ kaṭavuḷaiyum, kuṟittal kāṇka. vāṉuṟaiyun teyvattuḷ vaikkappaṭum (kuṟaḷ. 50) eṉpatil, ciṟu teyvattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭa tēvarulaka vāḻiyaik kuṟittatu.
taṉvayatta ṉātal, eṅkum niṟaital, ellām vallaṉātal, ellām aṟital, eṉṟu muṇmai, iyal māṟāmai, muḻut tūymai, oppuyar viṉmai, pēraruḷuṭaimai, varampiliṉpa muṭaimai eṉpaṉa kaṭavuḷiṉ iyalkaḷākac collappeṟum.
ellām valla iṟai vaṇakkattaiyē koṇṭa civaṉiyam, māliyam, yūtam, kiṟittuvam, icalām mutaliya ellā mataṅ kaṭkum, kaṭavuṭ camayam potuvām.
icumavēla icuravēlariṉ muṉṉōṉāṉa āpirakāmiṟku muṉpē, nōvā kālattup peru veḷḷattiṟkum muṉṉarē, kaṭavuḷai ulakil mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭavar kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻarē. kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ col tolkāppiyattil āḷap paṭṭiruppatē, ataṟkuc cāṉṟām.
kāmap pakuti kaṭavuḷum varaiyār.  (tol. puṟat. 27).
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē.  (tol. puṟat. 33)
kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ col, ellāvaṟṟaiyuṅ kaṭantu eṇṇiṟkum eṭṭāta param poruḷaiyē kuṟikkum. ciṟu teyvavaṇakka āriyar (pirāmaṇar), piṟkālattil ataip piḻaipaṭa āṇṭu viṭṭaṉar.
kaṭavuṭku uruvamiṉmaiyāl, uruva vaṇakkamu millai. evarum eṅkum eṉṟum tamittum piṟaroṭu kūṭiyum, kaṭavuḷait toḻalām.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kaṭavuṭ camayattār. ataṉāl, mutaṟ pakavaṉ, vālaṟivaṉ, malarmicai yēki, viruppu veṟuppillāṉ, iṟaivaṉ, aimpulam veṉṟāṉ, uvamaiyili, aṟavāḻi yantaṇaṉ, eṇ kuṇattāṉ, piṟavi nīkki eṉak kaṭavuṭkuriya pal camayap potup peyarkaḷaiyē tam kaṭavuḷ vāḻttil āṇṭār.
aṅkiṅ keṉātapaṭi yeṅkumpira kācamāy
	āṉanta pūrttiyāki
aruḷoṭu niṟaintatetu taṉṉaruḷ veḷikkuḷē
	akilāṇṭa kōṭiyellām
taṅkum paṭikkiccai vaittuyirk kuyirāyt
	taḻaittatetu maṉavākkiṉil
taṭṭāmal niṉṟatetu camayakō ṭikaḷelām
	tanteyvam enteyvameṉ
ṟeṅkun toṭarntetir vaḻakkiṭavum niṉṟatetu
	eṅkaṇum peruvaḻakkāy
yātiṉum vallavoru cittāki yiṉpamāy
	eṉṟaikku muḷḷatetumēṟ
kaṅkulpaka laṟaniṉṟa ellaiyuḷa tetuvatu
	karuttiṟ kicaintatuvē
kaṇṭaṉa velāmōṉa vuruveḷiya tākavum
	karutiyañ caliceykuvōm.  (tāyu. paraciva. 1).
paṇṇē ṉuṉakkāṉa pūcaiyoru vaṭivilē
	pāvitti ṟaiñcavāṅkē
pārkkiṉṟa malarūṭu nīyē yiruttiyap
	paṉimala reṭukkamaṉamum
naṇṇē ṉalāmaliru kaitāṉ kuvikkaveṉiṉ
	nāṇumeṉ ṉuḷaniṟṟinī
nāṉkumpi ṭumpō taraikkumpi ṭātalāl
	nāṉpūcai ceyyalmuṟaiyō
viṇṇē viṇātiyām pūtamē nātamē
	vētamē vētāntamē
mētakka kēḷviyē kēḷviyām pūmikkuḷ
	vittē yavittiṉ muḷaiyē
kaṇṇē karuttē eṉ eṇṇē yeḻuttē
	katikkāṉa mōṉavaṭivē
karutariya ciṟcapaiyi lāṉanta niruttamiṭu
	karuṇākarak kaṭavuḷē.  (tāyu. karuṇā. 6).
eṭṭut ticaiyum patiṉāṟu kōṇamu meṅkumoṉṟāy
muṭṭit tatumpi muḷaittōṅku cōtiyai mūṭarellām
kaṭṭic curuṭṭittam kakkattil vaippar karuttilvaiyār
paṭṭap pakalai yiraveṉṟu kūṟiṭum pātakarē.  (paṭṭiṉat. potu. 30).
uḷiyiṭṭa kallaiyum oppiṭṭa cāntaiyum ūttaiyaṟap
puḷiyiṭṭa cempaiyum pōṟṟuki lēṉuyar poṉṉeṉavē
oḷiyiṭṭa tāḷiraṇ ṭuḷḷē yiruttuva tuṇmaiyeṉṟu
veḷiyiṭ ṭaṭaittuvait tēṉiṉi mēloṉṟum vēṇṭilaṉē. 
(paṭṭiṉat. potu. 61).
muppāḻum pāḻāy mutaṟpāḻuñ cūṉiyamāy
appāḻum pāḻā yaṉpuceyva tekkālam.  (pattira. 113).
veṭṭa veḷikkuḷḷē viḷaṅkuñ catācivattaik
kiṭṭa varattēṭik kirupaiceyva tekkālam.  (pattira. 199).
naṭṭa kallait teyva meṉṟu nālu puṭpañ cāttiyē
cuṟṟi vantu muṇamu ṇeṉṟu collu mantiram ētaṭā
naṭṭa kallum pēcu mōnam nāta ṉuḷḷi rukkaiyil
cuṭṭa caṭṭi caṭṭu vamka ṟiccu vaiya ṟiyumō.  (civa. vāk. 518).
koṇ muṭipu
civa matattiṟkuk kūṟiya koṇmuṭipē kaṭavuṭ camayattiṟkum. talaivaṉ taḷaiyaṉ taḷai eṉṉum mupporuḷai; kaṭavuḷ kaṭṭuṇi kaṭṭu eṉṟu coṉ māṟṟiyuṅ kūṟalām.
illaṟa vāyilākavum iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭi yaṭaiyalā mātalāl, nal viṉaiyāṟ kēṭillai.
ceykuvaṅ kollō nalviṉai yeṉavē
aiyam aṟāar kacaṭīṇṭu kāṭci
nīṅkā neñcattut tuṇivil lōrē
yāṉai vēṭṭuvaṉ yāṉaiyum peṟumē
ataṉāl,
uyarnta vēṭṭat tuyarntici ṉōrkkuc
ceyviṉai maruṅkiṉ eyta luṇṭeṉiṉ
toyyā vulakattu nukarcciyuṅ kūṭum
toyyā vulakattu nukarcci yilleṉiṉ
māṟip piṟappiṉ iṉmaiyuṅ kūṭum
māṟip piṟavā rāyiṉum imayattuk
kōṭuyarn taṉṉa tammicai naṭṭut
tītil yākkaiyoṭu māytaltavat talaiyē.  (puṟam. 214).
piṟavārāyiṉum eṉpatil ummai etirmaṟai. acai nilai eṉṟu paḻaiya vurai kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
illaṟattālum vīṭu peṟalām eṉpatē kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻar koḷkai. cuntara mūrtti nāyaṉār caṅkili paravai yārākiya tēva kaṇikaiyaroṭu iṉpam nukarntatu, iṟaivaṉuk kuṭampāṭeṉṟu collappaṭum pōtu, iṟaivaṉ vakutta muṟaippaṭi karaṇattoṭu maṇanta kaṟpuṭai maṉaiviyoṭu illaṟam naṭattuvatu, eṅṅaṉ iṟaivaṉukku ēṟkā tirukkum?
	meyp poruḷiyal
civa matattiṟkuk kūṟiyatē kaṭavuṭ camayattiṟkum.
kaṭavuṭ camayam tuṟaviyarkkē uriya teṉṟum, illaṟattārkku uruva vaṇakkam iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉṟum, taṉṉalap pirāmaṇap pūcāriyarāl, oru tavaṟāṉa karuttu iṟṟait tamiḻa ruḷḷattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kiṟittavarum mukamatiyarum uruvat tuṇai yiṉṟiyē iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. kaṟṟāruṅ kallārumāṉa tamiḻarum, vīṭṭilum kāṭṭilum vaḻip pōkki lum tuṉpam nērnta viṭattu, aṇṇāntu vāṉai nōkki āṇṭavaṉē! allatu kaṭavuḷē eṉṟu viḷittu vēṇṭutal ceyvatu; allatu oruvar aṟamuṟai tavaṟi aṭṭūḻiyañ ceyyumpōtu, vāṉai nōkki, atō pōkiṟāṉē, avaṉ kēṭpāṉ. eṉṟu muṟaiyiṭuvatu, iṉṟum vaḻakkamāyiruk kiṉṟatu. ātalāl, kallārkkō, potu makkaṭkō, illaṟat tārkkō, kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum vaḻipāṭum ēṟkā teṉpatu poruntātu.
uruva vaṇakkap paḻaiya ēṟpāṭu (Old Testament) nīṅki, uruvilā vaṇakkap putiya ēṟpāṭu (New Testament) kumari nāṭṭilēyē aṟiñariṭait toṭaṅki viṭṭateṉṟum, atu īraṟattārkkum ēṟku meṉṟum, uṇmai yaṟika.
perumaippeṇ
ammāṉ makaḷum attai makaḷumpōla muṟaikārap peṇ ṇāyiṉ urimaip peṇ eṉṟum, vēṟu celvar vīṭṭup peṇṇāyiṉ perumaippeṇ eṉṟum aḻaippatu vaḻakkam.  (ta. ti. 19).
peṟṟi
iṟumpūtu āṟṟaṟ pēṟṟaic citti eṉṟum, atai aṭaintavaraic cittar eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar colvar. citti eṉṉum colliṟkuk kaikūṭal allatu muyaṟci veṟṟi eṉpatē poruḷ. ātalāl cittiyaip peṟṟi eṉṉum cittaraip peṟṟiyar eṉṟum tamiḻiṟ kūṟalām.
peṟu - peṟṟi - peṟṟatu, peṟṟa taṉmai, pēṟu; peṟṟiyār = ciṟanta pēṟu peṟṟavar. pēṟu eṉṉum potuc coṟkum ciṟanta pēṟu eṉṉum poruḷ iruttalāl. cittaraip peṟṟiyar eṉṟu colvatu poruttamē.  (ta. i. va. 95).
peṟṟiyar ākār (cittar ākār)
piṟappāl ēṟṟat tāḻvu vakuttup piṟarai ikaḻvōr, uḷḷat tūymaiyum illār; ulakat tuṟavum koḷḷār. oruvakaip peṟṟiyum (cittiyum) ollār.  (ta. i. va. 95).
peṟṟōr
peṟṟōr maram pōlvar. piḷḷaikaḷ kiḷaikaḷum koḻuntum pōlvar. ataṉāl makaṉaik kompaṉ eṉṟum makaḷaik kompu eṉṟum iruvaraiyum potuppaṭak kulak koḻuntu eṉṟum kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
oru marattiṉ aṭiyil muḷaikkum muḷaiyaip pōṉṟu makaṉ iruttaliṉ avaṉukkuk kāṉmuḷai eṉṟum peyar.  (col. 3).
pē
pē - va. pī (bh)
pēpē eṉpatu accak kuṟippu.
pē = accam. pēemutir kaṭavuḷ  (kuṟun. 87).
pē - pēm = accam.
pēmnām urumeṉa varūuṅ kiḷavi
āmuṟai mūṉṟum accap poruḷa.  (tol. 848).
pē - pēy = accam, añcappaṭum āvi.
pē - pī - paya = accam.  (va. va : 210).
pēcu(1)
pēcu - va. pāṣ (bh)
pē - pēcu. pēcutal = utaṭacaittuc collutal, moḻital, uraiyāṭutal. pakaram itaḻp piṟappiṉatu.
pēcu - pēccu = col, urai, coṟpoḻivu, moḻi.  (va. va : 210 - 211).
pēcu(2)
poḷḷutal = tuḷaittal. poḷḷal = tuḷai. poḷ = tuḷai, uṭṭuḷai (piṅ.).
poḷ - poy. o. nō : toḷ - toy.
poy = 1. tuḷai, uṭṭuḷai. poyporu muṭaṅkukai (cilap. 15 : 50) 2. marappontu (piṅ.). 3. uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu. 4. viḷaicca lillātatu. poyppiñcu, poyccīppu, (u.va.). 5. uṇmai yillātatu. poruḷ keṭuttup poymēṟ koḷīi (kuṟaḷ. 938). poykkatai. 6. māyai. poynnīr, (W.). 7. nilaiyāmai. puṟputamē yaṉṉa poykkuṭi vāḻkkaiyai (tirunūṟ. 3). 8. pōliyāṉatu. 9. ceyaṟkaiyāṉatu. poykkāl. 10. ēmāṟṟuvatu, poymmāṉ, poykkuḻi.
poytal = (ce. ku. vi.). 1 tuḷaipaṭutal. 2. tuḷaikkappaṭutal. poytaka ṭoṉṟu porunti (kamparā. pañca. 49). 3. tuḷaiyiṉiṉṟu oḻukutal - (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. corital.. ikaliṉ poyta (kamparā. kiṅkarar. 50). 2. vīḻttutal. avai pārcōrap poytāṉ (kamparā. aṭca. 33).
poy - pey. peytal = (ce. ku. vi). mēṉiṉṟu oḻukutal. peyyeṉap peyyum maḻai (kuṟaḷ. 53) - (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. vārttal. pāṇar maṇṭai niṟaiyap peymmār (puṟanā. 115). 2. kala mutaliyavaṟṟil iṭutal. ulaippey taṭuvatu pōlun tuyar (nālaṭi. 114). 3. ciṟunīr kaḻittal. 4. tūvutal.
pey - pēy. pēytal = peytal (u.va.). pēyntuṅ keṭṭatu, ōyntuṅ keṭṭatu (paḻa.). ip paḻamoḻiyiṟ pēyntum eṉṉum col ōyntum eṉpataṉoṭu etukai nōkkit tirintatai, pāṭṭurai nūlē eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (cey. 78) nīṭṭum vaḻi nīṭṭal eṉṉum ceyyuḷ tiripāka amaikka iṭantarumēṉum, koccaiyeṉṟu karutappaṭum iraṇṭōr ulaka vaḻakkuc col vaṭiviṉiṉṟum ilakkiyac coṟkaḷ amaintiruppatāl pēytal eṉṉum collait tiripiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatākak koḷvatē takkatām.
e - ṭu : koṇṭuvā - koṇṭā - koṇā - koṇar.
iccol vaṭivukaḷuḷ, koṇṭā eṉpatu vaḻunilaiyākavum, ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa koṇā, koṇar eṉpaṉa vaḻānilai yākavum, iruttalai nōkkuka.
pēy - pēyu - pēcu. o. nō : tēy - tēyu - tēcu.
pēcutal = vāṉ nīrai oḻuka viṭuvatu pōṉṟu vāy collai oḻuka viṭutal.
oḻukutal ōṭṭaiyiṉiṉṟu viḻutal allatu cintutal, kalattiṉiṉṟu nīr vārttal eṉpatu, ataṉ vāyākiya ōṭṭai vaḻi oḻuka viṭutal. vāṉ maḻai peyvatu kalattiṉiṉṟu nīr vārttal pōṉṟiruttalāl, vāṉiṉ ṟamaiyā toḻukku (kuṟaḷ. 20) eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
maḻai tūṟaluṅ koṭṭutalumiṉṟi naṭuttiṟamākap peyyiṉ peytaleṉṟum, koṭṭutalum ūṟṟutalum pōlak kaṉattup peyyiṉ poḻital eṉṟum, collappaṭum.
poḻital - tiraḷutal. poḻittal - tiraṭṭutal. poḻippurai - tiraṭṭurai. poḻil - tiraṇṭa cōlai.
oruvar iyalpākac collāṭal maḻai peytal pōṉ ṟiruttalāṟ pēcutal eṉṟum, viraivākac collāṭal maḻai poḻital pōṉṟiruttalāṟ poḻital eṉṟum, collappaṭu tal toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu. oru maṇi nēram poḻintu taḷḷi viṭṭār eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu, kallā makkaḷ vāyiṉiṉṟu varuvataiyuṅ kāṇalām. iv vaḻakki ṉiṉṟē, coṟpoḻivu, coṟpoḻivāḷar eṉṉuṅ kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ ikkālattil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl, poḻital eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē pēcutal eṉṉum collum maḻai viṉaiyoppumai paṟṟit tōṉṟiyateṉa uṇaralām.
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ
pēcu eṉṉun teṉcolliṉiṉṟē, pāṣ (bhaÍsh, eṉṉum vaṭacollum tirintuḷḷatu. itai yuṇarntē ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyait (Tamil Lexicon) tokutta vaṭamoḻi veṟiyarum, pēcu eṉṉum talaiccollaiyaṭuttu “cf. bhaÍs´” eṉṟu kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
bhas, to speak, talk, say, tell.
bhashaka, speaking, talking about, KaÍv.
bhashana, the act of speaking, talking, speech, talk, Nir; Mn; MBh.
bhasha, Speech, language (esp. common or vernacular Speech as opp. to Vedic or in later time to Sanskrit) Nir; PaÍn; Mn; MBh; any PraÍkit dialect or a particular group of 5 of them. (Maharashtri, SØauraseniÍ, MaÍgadhiÍ, PraÍcya and Avanti, also called PanÃca VidhaÍ bhaÍshaÍ.
bhashika, belonging to the common or vernacular speech, Nir.
bhashita, spoken, uttered, said, spoken to, addressed, Mn; MBh.
bhashitarya, to be spoken to or addressed, R.
bhashitri, a speaker, talker, SBr; MBh; KaÍv.
bhashin, saying, speaking, loquacious, MBk; KaÍv.
bhashya, speaking, talking, Susr; any work in the common or vernacular speech, VPraÍt. GrS; Hariv; an explanatory work, exposition, explanation, commentary esp. on technical sutras, Name of PatanÃjali’s commentary on the sutras of PaÍn´ini, mahaÍ bhaÍshya.
sam-bhash, to speak together, converse with, speak to, join in a conversation.
sam-bhashana, conversation, discourse with.
subhashana, Name of a person.
subhashita, spoken well or eloquently.
subhashitam, good or eloquent speech, witty saying.
Gk. phasis, speech, utterance, expression.
aphasia, loss of speech, or of understanding of language, owing to brain damage; f. mod. L. f. Gk. aphatos, speechless (a, not, pha, to speak).
A allatu a eṉṟa (kirēkka) eḻuttiṟkuk kirēkkattil a, ā eṉṟa olikaḷē uḷa. ātalāl, pēcu eṉṟa collē, intiya āriyariṉ muṉṉōr kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkup pakkattil vāḻntu vantapōtu, avar moḻiyil pāṣ (bhaÍsh) eṉṟum, kirēkka moḻiyiṟ pā (phaÍ) eṉṟum, tirintiruttal vēṇṭum.
kiḷēṉ (Klein) āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalic coṟkaḷ
“aphasis, n., loss of the faculty of speech (med.) - Medical L., fr. priv. pref. a - and phasis, speech, from the base of phanai, to speak, which is rel. to phame, voice, report, rumour, and cog. with L. fama, talk, report, rumour” (p. 42).
“aphemia, n, a kind of aphasis (med.) - Medical L. coined by the French surgeon and anthropologist Paul Broca (1824 - 80) in 1860 fr. priv-pref. 
a - and Gk. pheme, voice, which is rel. to phanai, to say, speak”. (p. 42).
“fama, n., the personification of rumour in Roman mythology.” (p. 273).
“fame, n. F. fame, fr. L. fame, talk, report, rumor, tradition, reputation, lit, ‘saying’, from the stem of for, faÍri, pp. fatus, to speak, rel. to fateÍri, to confess, fabula, narrative, account, tale, story, fas, divine law (lit. utterance, fr. I. E. base, bhaÍ, to speak, tell, say, whence also OI bhanati, speaks, Arm. bay, gen. bayi, word, term, bey, he said, ban, gen. bani, word, speech, judgement, Gk. phemi, I say; pheme, Dor. phaÍmaÍ, voice, report, rumour, phatis, saying, speech, report, OSlav bajo, bajati, to talk, tell, basni, fable, tale, charm, OE. boian, to boast, ON. boÍn, OE. beÍn, prayer, request. Cp. phone, speech sound, Cp. also abandon, affable, aphasla, aphemia, apophasis, ban, to prohibit, ban, ediet. bandit, banish, bifarious, blame, blaspheme, boon, confabulate, confess, defamation, defame, euphemism, fabulous, famous, fascinate, fate, infamous, infamy, infant, infantry; multifarious, nefarious, - phasia, - phemia.  Poly-phemns, preface, prefatory, profess, prophet, trifarious”. (p. 273).

pottakam

pullutal = poruntutal. pul - pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu - pottu - poṭṭu.

cuvaṭi. cērttal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
